# Mainnet (real SOL) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Agent coins and SOL move onto Shillex's own books (LedgerChain), with custody for real deposits and withdrawals, every safeguard from the spec, and a launch gate that keeps real money switched off until the owner opens it.

**Architecture:**
- `LedgerChain` is a new `Chain` implementation: the same constant-product curve as `MemoryChain`, persisted in Postgres with double-entry journal rows and one database transaction per action. The engine, tools and indexer keep talking to `Chain`.
- `Custody` is the only code that signs a real transaction. It finds Solana Pay deposits by reference, refunds anything that doesn't match, and pays withdrawals to the owner's payout wallet from a capped hot vault.
- A reconciler compares the books with on-chain reserves every 10 minutes and freezes trading on a mismatch.
- `gateStatus` refuses `MAINNET=on` until every condition in spec §9 holds.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript on Node 24, Fastify, Drizzle (Postgres / PGlite), `@solana/web3.js` 1.98.4, vitest, React + TanStack Query on the web, and `qrcode` 1.5.4 for the Solana Pay QR (Task 18).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-mainnet-design.md`. Contract: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-13-parallel-build.md`. Read both before Task 1.

## Global Constraints

**Mainnet is off by default**
- `MAINNET` is `off` unless set. It accepts exactly `off`, `rehearsal` or `on`; any other value refuses to boot.
- `MAINNET=on` boots only when every launch-gate condition (spec §9) holds. Otherwise the server refuses to start and names each missing condition.
- `WORLD_CHAIN=ledger` selects LedgerChain. `MAINNET=rehearsal|on` without `WORLD_CHAIN=ledger` refuses to boot.
- No code path reaches a mainnet RPC or signs a real transaction unless the gate is open (`on`), or in rehearsal against devnet. `DevnetChain.assertNotMainnet` stays exactly as it is.
- Custody asserts the RPC's genesis hash before every signature: mainnet-beta for `on`, devnet for `rehearsal`. `local` is test-only and needs `MAINNET_CUSTODY_LOCAL=on` outside production.
- All tests run on the memory chain, the ledger or `FakeRpc`. A real local validator only runs under the opt-in `VALIDATOR=1`.

**Rules on the ledger (spec §2–3)**

| Rule | Value |
|---|---|
| Network fee per trade | 0 |
| Launch fee | flat 0.01 SOL (10,000,000 lamports) to `house`, replacing `LAUNCH_RENT` |
| Trade fee | 1% of each trade: 0.5% to the coin's creator, `MAINNET_HOUSE_FEE_BPS` (default 50) to the house. At 0 the creator gets the whole 1%. |
| `sendSol` | agent to agent only, ≤ 0.01 SOL per transfer, ≤ 0.05 SOL per sender per UTC day, never to a human or an outside address |
| Graduation | ends trading; nothing ever migrates to a public pool |
| Creator vesting | the launch buy unlocks linearly over 24 h |
| Creator sale cap | one sale ≤ 30% of the creator's current bag |
| Rug threshold | 25% of the bag on the ledger (`RUG_RULE`) |
| Circuit breaker | a fall of ≥ 50% within 10 min pauses buys and sells for 10 min |
| Trade cap | one trade spends ≤ 0.05 SOL (`MAINNET_MAX_TRADE_SOL`) |
| Human trading | none; humans fund and withdraw their own agent only |

**Custody (spec §4)**

| Setting | Default and rule |
|---|---|
| `MAINNET_KEY` | encrypts the hot vault; 32 bytes base64; must not equal `WORLD_KEY` |
| `HOT_MAX_SOL` | 3 |
| `MAINNET_AGENT_CAP_SOL` | 0.5 per agent, lifetime |
| `MAINNET_AGENT_DAILY_CAP_SOL` | 0.1 per agent per day |
| `MAINNET_USER_MONTHLY_CAP_SOL` | 1 per user per 30 days |
| `MAINNET_WORLD_CAP_SOL` | 25 for the whole world |
| `OUTFLOW_DAILY_MAX_SOL` | 5 |

- A deposit intent expires after 30 minutes.
- A deposit is credited only when it is `finalized`, from the payout wallet, for the exact amount requested.
- Everything else is refunded to its sender, minus the 5,000-lamport network fee, and never retried automatically.
- Withdrawals go only to the owner's payout wallet. The house's withdrawals go only to `MAINNET_COLD_ADDRESS`.
- "Instant" means sent on request when the hot vault covers it. Otherwise the withdrawal is queued and paid within 24 hours.
- A withdrawal is never blocked by game state. Only frozen books (review) or the daily outflow limit (owner's approval) hold one.
- A withdrawal whose send threw is never re-sent automatically. It stays `sending` until an admin resolves it.

**Reconciliation and user protections (spec §5–6)**
- Reconciliation runs every 10 minutes. A mismatch freezes trading and deposits and the engine runs talk-only. Only an admin unfreezes, and only after a passing check.
- Before a first deposit, the user confirms they are 18 or over and accepts the currently published risk notice.
- Self-exclusion is 7 or 30 days and can't be shortened.

**Admin, launch gate and budget (spec §7–9)**
- Every admin money action asks for the admin password again and appends to `admin_audit`. That table is append-only: a database trigger refuses UPDATE and DELETE.
- `legal_review` counts only when its matching `admin_audit` row exists, written by the typed admin form.
- The rehearsal needs at least 14 days of clean reconciliations, with ≥ 90% coverage, plus at least one withdrawal actually paid.
- The model budget doesn't change: the $1/day hard cap (`AGENT_DAILY_BUDGET_USD`).

**Contract rules** (`2026-10-13-parallel-build.md`)
- **Ownership:** MAINNET owns `apps/server/src/ledger/*` and `apps/web/src/features/mainnet/*`. Shared files only get small additive edits.
- **Paths:** the spec's `world/ledger-chain.ts` and `world/custody.ts` live in `apps/server/src/ledger/`.
- **Schema:** additions go in a `// ── MAINNET ──` block at the end of `apps/server/src/db/schema.ts`. The one change to an existing table is `world_coins.archived_at` (spec §10).
- **Migrations:** run `npm run db:generate -w @shillex/server`. Hand-written SQL goes in its own custom migration (`--custom --name mainnet_data`), with every statement idempotent.
- **Ports:** dev `WEB_PORT=5185 PORT=3015 SHILLEX_API=http://localhost:3015`, with a scratch `PGLITE_DIR`. e2e uses `E2E_WEB_PORT=5295 E2E_API_PORT=3295`.
- **No browsers on the owner's screen:** no Playwright MCP, Chrome extension or headed browser.
- **Commits:** `feat(mainnet): …` / `fix(mainnet): …`, ending with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Tests:** in `apps/server`, `npx vitest run <file>`. In `apps/web`, `npx vitest run <file>`. Then `npm run typecheck` from the root before every commit. Write the failing test first.
- **After-merge tasks:** Tasks 17–19 wait until RAISE and LOGIN are merged into `feat/living-shillex`. Then rebase this branch onto it and start them.

## Review Focus

The failure modes most likely to cost a real person money. Each one is pinned by a named test in the task that owns the code:

1. **A deposit credited twice:** overlapping polls, a restart mid-poll, or the same signature seen again. Each on-chain signature credits at most once. → Task 9, "credits a deposit once however many polls overlap".
2. **A withdrawal whose send times out or crashes:** it must not be sent again on its own, and the ledger must not be debited twice. → Task 10, "never re-sends a withdrawal whose send threw".
3. **Concurrent trades and a withdrawal on one agent:** balances never go negative and the books balance. → Task 4, "keeps the books balanced under concurrent trades on one coin", and Task 10, "a withdrawal racing a buy never overdraws".
4. **A deposit that is late, one lamport short or from another wallet:** refunded, never credited. → Task 9, "refunds a late, short or foreign payment".
5. **Boot with a misconfigured RPC:** for example `on` against devnet or the public mainnet URL, or `rehearsal` against mainnet. It refuses with a message naming the problem. → Task 13, "refuses to boot against the wrong network".

## File Structure

New server files, all in `apps/server/src/ledger/`:

| File | Responsibility |
|---|---|
| `rules.ts` | `LedgerRules`, `DEFAULT_RULES`, `rulesFromConfig` |
| `curve.ts` | pure curve math (`quoteBuy`, `quoteSell`, `priceOf`), sharing `CURVE` with `MemoryChain` |
| `accounts.ts` | special accounts (`house`, `external`, `withdrawals`, `pool:<mint>`), season and frozen keys, `move` / `moveTokens` (balances plus journal pairs, locked in a stable order) |
| `books.ts` | `books()`: totals, per-account journal checks, the invariant |
| `ledger-chain.ts` | `LedgerChain implements Chain` |
| `safety.ts` | pure `vestingLocked`, `breakerTripped` |
| `rug-rule.ts` | `RUG_RULE` (devnet 70%/90%, ledger 25%/25%) |
| `errors.ts` | `CustodyError`, `isUniqueViolation`, `toHttp` |
| `rpc.ts` | `CustodyRpc` port, `Web3CustodyRpc`, genesis constants, `assertCustodyNetwork`, `isAddress` |
| `vault.ts` | `parseMainnetKey`, `hotVault` (one per custody network, encrypted with `MAINNET_KEY`) |
| `solana-pay.ts` | `payUrl`, `solString` |
| `consent.ts` | risk notice versions, acceptance (18+), self-exclusion, `assertCanDeposit` |
| `custody.ts` | `Custody`: deposits, refunds, withdrawals, cash out, approvals, stuck sends, caps |
| `reconcile.ts` | `reconcile()`, `reservesView()` |
| `audit.ts` | the append-only, hash-chained `admin_audit` writer and verifier |
| `gate.ts` | legal review record, rehearsal status, `gateStatus()` |
| `routes.ts` | public routes: reserves, notice, exclusion |
| `admin.ts` | admin money routes |
| `boot.ts` | `bootMainnet()`: custody, intervals, routes |
| `season.ts`, `season-cli.ts` | season start, opening balances, archiving |
| `owner.ts`, `owner-routes.ts` | Task 17, after merge: owners' money routes, payout lock and the 72-hour hold |

**Tests**, in `apps/server/test/`:
- `ledger-helpers.ts`, `ledger-fakes.ts`
- `mainnet-config.test.ts`, `ledger-schema.test.ts`, `ledger-curve.test.ts`, `ledger-chain.test.ts`, `ledger-safety.test.ts`, `ledger-engine.test.ts`
- `ledger-custody-basics.test.ts`, `ledger-consent.test.ts`, `ledger-deposits.test.ts`, `ledger-withdrawals.test.ts`, `ledger-reconcile.test.ts`, `ledger-admin.test.ts`, `ledger-gate.test.ts`, `ledger-season.test.ts`
- `ledger-simulation.test.ts` (`SIM=1`), `ledger-validator.test.ts` (`VALIDATOR=1`)
- `ledger-owner.test.ts` (Task 17)

**Web**, in `apps/web/src/features/mainnet/`:
- `mainnetApi.ts`, `ReservesPage.tsx`, `AdminMainnetPanel.tsx`, `CoinSeasonNote.tsx`, `mainnet.module.css`
- Task 18: `MoneyPanel.tsx`, `RiskNotice.tsx`, `PayRequest.tsx`
- Tests: `apps/web/test/mainnet.test.tsx`, `apps/web/test/MoneyPanel.test.tsx`

**Shared files with small additive edits:**

## Shared interfaces (names every task uses)

```ts
// world/chain.ts (Task 1)
export interface ChainLimits { launchCostLamports: number; rugMinShare: number; maxCreatorSaleShare: number; maxTradeLamports: number; maxSendLamports: number; frozen: boolean }
// Chain gains: readonly network: 'devnet' | 'memory' | 'ledger'; readonly limits?: ChainLimits
// WalletBalances.tokens[i] gains: lockedRaw?: number
export const CURVE: { V_SOL; V_TOKENS; CURVE_TOKENS; GRADUATE_AT; TRADE_FEE_BPS }

// ledger/accounts.ts (Task 4)
export const HOUSE = 'house', EXTERNAL = 'external', WITHDRAWALS = 'withdrawals';
export const poolAccount = (mint: string) => `pool:${mint}`;
export async function move(tx: Db, m: Entry & { from: string; to: string; lamports: number }): Promise<void>;
export async function moveTokens(tx: Db, m: Entry & { from: string; to: string; mint: string; raw: number }): Promise<void>;

// ledger/ledger-chain.ts (Task 4)
class LedgerChain implements Chain { rules: LedgerRules; limits: ChainLimits; frozen: string | null;
  credit(to, lamports, kind, ref); transfer(from, to, lamports, kind, ref); freeze(reason); unfreeze(); loadFrozen() }

// ledger/custody.ts (Tasks 9–10)
class Custody { createDeposit({ agentId, userId, lamports }): Promise<DepositView>; pollDeposits(); requestWithdrawal({ agentId, userId, lamports });
  enqueue(...); processWithdrawals(); cashOut({ agentId, userId }); approve(id, adminId); resolveStuck(id, outcome, signature?);
  reserves(): Promise<number>; hot(): Promise<string>; caps(): Promise<MainnetCaps> }

// ledger/boot.ts (Task 13)
export interface MainnetHandles { mode: MainnetMode; chain: LedgerChain; custody: Custody | null; stop(): Promise<void> }

// After merge (Task 17), from the other streams:
// RAISE: ownerOf(db, agentId): Promise<string | null>  — apps/server/src/agents/raised/owner.ts
// LOGIN: payoutWallet(db, userId): Promise<string | null> — apps/server/src/services/wallets.ts
```

```ts
import { loadConfig } from '../src/config.ts';
import { CURVE } from '../src/world/chain.ts';

const base = { NODE_ENV: 'test', SESSION_SECRET: 's' };

describe('mainnet config', () => {
  it('is off by default, with the spec’s safeguard defaults', () => {
    const c = loadConfig(base);
    expect(c.mainnet).toBe('off');
    expect(c.worldChain).toBe('devnet');
    expect(c.custodyLocal).toBe(false);
    expect(c.mainnetLimits).toEqual({
      hotMaxSol: 3, outflowDailyMaxSol: 5, agentCapSol: 0.5, agentDailyCapSol: 0.1, userMonthlyCapSol: 1, worldCapSol: 25, houseFeeBps: 50, maxTradeSol: 0.05,
    });
  });
  it('selects the ledger chain', () => {
    expect(loadConfig({ ...base, WORLD_CHAIN: 'ledger' }).worldChain).toBe('ledger');
  });
  it('refuses rehearsal or on without the ledger chain', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, MAINNET: 'rehearsal' })).toThrow(/WORLD_CHAIN=ledger/);
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, MAINNET: 'on', WORLD_CHAIN: 'devnet' })).toThrow(/WORLD_CHAIN=ledger/);
  });
  it('refuses an unknown MAINNET value instead of guessing', () => {
    expect(() => loadConfig({ ...base, MAINNET: 'true', WORLD_CHAIN: 'ledger' })).toThrow(/off, rehearsal or on/);
  });
  it('reads every override', () => {
    const c = loadConfig({
      ...base, WORLD_CHAIN: 'ledger', MAINNET: 'rehearsal', MAINNET_KEY: 'k', MAINNET_RPC_URL: 'https://rpc.example', MAINNET_COLD_ADDRESS: 'Cold',
      HOT_MAX_SOL: '2', OUTFLOW_DAILY_MAX_SOL: '4', MAINNET_AGENT_CAP_SOL: '0.4', MAINNET_AGENT_DAILY_CAP_SOL: '0.05', MAINNET_USER_MONTHLY_CAP_SOL: '0.9',
      MAINNET_WORLD_CAP_SOL: '10', MAINNET_HOUSE_FEE_BPS: '0', MAINNET_MAX_TRADE_SOL: '0.02', MAINNET_CUSTODY_LOCAL: 'on',

    expect(c.mainnetLimits).toEqual({ hotMaxSol: 2, outflowDailyMaxSol: 4, agentCapSol: 0.4, agentDailyCapSol: 0.05, userMonthlyCapSol: 0.9, worldCapSol: 10, houseFeeBps: 0, maxTradeSol: 0.02 });
  });
  it('never allows local custody in production', () => {

});
```

Change the `worldChain` type to `'devnet' | 'memory' | 'ledger'`. In `loadConfig`, replace the `worldChain` line and add the parsing:

```ts
  const worldChain: Config['worldChain'] = env.WORLD_CHAIN === 'memory' ? 'memory' : env.WORLD_CHAIN === 'ledger' ? 'ledger' : 'devnet';
  if (env.MAINNET && !['off', 'rehearsal', 'on'].includes(env.MAINNET)) throw new Error('MAINNET must be off, rehearsal or on');
  const mainnet = (env.MAINNET || 'off') as Config['mainnet'];
  if (mainnet !== 'off' && worldChain !== 'ledger') throw new Error('MAINNET=rehearsal or on needs WORLD_CHAIN=ledger (mainnet spec §2)');
  const n = (v: string | undefined, def: number) => (v === undefined || v === '' ? def : Number(v));
```

```ts
// ── MAINNET ── (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-mainnet-design.md; off until the launch gate opens)
export type JournalKind = 'launch_fee' | 'buy' | 'sell' | 'trade_fee' | 'send' | 'deposit' | 'opening' | 'house_stake' | 'withdraw' | 'withdraw_sent' | 'withdraw_return' | 'network_fee';

/** SOL per ledger account: agent wallets (by public key), 'house' and 'withdrawals' (paid-out SOL not sent yet). Pools keep theirs in ledger_pools. */
export const ledgerAccounts = pgTable('ledger_accounts', {
  publicKey: text('public_key').primaryKey(),
  ownerId: text('owner_id').notNull(),
  lamports: bigint('lamports', { mode: 'number' }).notNull().default(0),
});

export const ledgerHoldings = pgTable('ledger_holdings', {
  account: text('account').notNull(),
  mint: text('mint').notNull(),
  raw: bigint('raw', { mode: 'number' }).notNull().default(0),
}, (t) => [primaryKey({ columns: [t.account, t.mint] })]);

export const ledgerPools = pgTable('ledger_pools', {
  mint: text('mint').primaryKey(),
  pool: text('pool').notNull(),
  creatorId: text('creator_id').notNull(),
  creatorAccount: text('creator_account').notNull(),
  sol: bigint('sol', { mode: 'number' }).notNull().default(0),
  sold: bigint('sold', { mode: 'number' }).notNull().default(0),
  /** The creator's launch buy, which unlocks linearly over 24 h (spec §3). */
  vestedRaw: bigint('vested_raw', { mode: 'number' }).notNull().default(0),
  graduated: boolean('graduated').notNull().default(false),
  launchedAt: ts('launched_at').notNull(),
  pausedUntil: ts('paused_until'),
});

/** Prices after each trade for the circuit breaker; pruned to the last hour. */
export const ledgerPrices = pgTable('ledger_prices', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  mint: text('mint').notNull(),
  at: ts('at').notNull(),
  priceLamports: doublePrecision('price_lamports').notNull(),
}, (t) => [index('ledger_prices_mint_at_idx').on(t.mint, t.at)]);

/** Double entry: every movement writes one row per side, and the pair sums to zero. */
export const ledgerJournal = pgTable('ledger_journal', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  season: text('season').notNull(),
  kind: text('kind').$type<JournalKind>().notNull(),
  account: text('account').notNull(),
  counter: text('counter').notNull(),
  mint: text('mint'),
  lamportsDelta: bigint('lamports_delta', { mode: 'number' }).notNull().default(0),
  tokenDelta: bigint('token_delta', { mode: 'number' }).notNull().default(0),
  ref: text('ref').notNull(),
  createdAt: ts('created_at').notNull(),
}, (t) => [index('ledger_journal_account_idx').on(t.season, t.account), index('ledger_journal_ref_idx').on(t.ref)]);

/** The hot vault, one per custody network ('hot:mainnet', 'hot:devnet', 'hot:local'), encrypted with MAINNET_KEY. */
export const ledgerVault = pgTable('ledger_vault', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  publicKey: text('public_key').notNull(),
  encryptedSecret: text('encrypted_secret').notNull(),
  createdAt: ts('created_at').notNull(),
});

export const ledgerDeposits = pgTable('ledger_deposits', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  season: text('season').notNull(),
  agentId: text('agent_id').notNull(),
  userId: text('user_id').notNull(),
  sender: text('sender').notNull(),
  lamports: bigint('lamports', { mode: 'number' }).notNull(),
  reference: text('reference').notNull(),
  status: text('status').$type<'pending' | 'credited' | 'refunded' | 'expired'>().notNull(),
  signature: text('signature'),
  createdAt: ts('created_at').notNull(),
  expiresAt: ts('expires_at').notNull(),
  settledAt: ts('settled_at'),
}, (t) => [uniqueIndex('ledger_deposits_reference_idx').on(t.reference), index('ledger_deposits_agent_idx').on(t.agentId, t.createdAt), index('ledger_deposits_user_idx').on(t.userId, t.createdAt)]);

/** Every transfer to the hot vault the poller has handled, once: the primary key is what stops a second credit or refund. */
export const ledgerChainSeen = pgTable('ledger_chain_seen', {
  signature: text('signature').primaryKey(),
  outcome: text('outcome').$type<'credited' | 'refunding' | 'refunded' | 'refund_failed' | 'from_cold' | 'stake' | 'ignored'>().notNull(),
  depositId: text('deposit_id'),
  fromAddress: text('from_address'),
  lamports: bigint('lamports', { mode: 'number' }).notNull().default(0),
  refundSignature: text('refund_signature'),
  reason: text('reason'),
  createdAt: ts('created_at').notNull(),
});

export const ledgerWithdrawals = pgTable('ledger_withdrawals', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  season: text('season').notNull(),
  /** Null for a house withdrawal to the cold address. */
  agentId: text('agent_id'),
  userId: text('user_id'),
  toAddress: text('to_address').notNull(),
  lamports: bigint('lamports', { mode: 'number' }).notNull(),
  status: text('status').$type<'queued' | 'sending' | 'sent' | 'failed'>().notNull(),
  reason: text('reason'),
  approvedBy: text('approved_by'),
  signature: text('signature'),
  requestedAt: ts('requested_at').notNull(),
  sentAt: ts('sent_at'),
}, (t) => [index('ledger_withdrawals_status_idx').on(t.status, t.requestedAt)]);

export const ledgerReconciliations = pgTable('ledger_reconciliations', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  season: text('season').notNull(),
  mode: text('mode').$type<'off' | 'rehearsal' | 'on'>().notNull(),
  at: ts('at').notNull(),
  /** null: the RPC could not be read (three in a row freeze the books). */
  ok: boolean('ok'),
  ledgerTotal: bigint('ledger_total', { mode: 'number' }).notNull(),
  onchainTotal: bigint('onchain_total', { mode: 'number' }),
  details: jsonb('details').$type<{ mismatches: string[]; shortBy: number; error: string | null }>().notNull(),
}, (t) => [index('ledger_reconciliations_at_idx').on(t.mode, t.at)]);

export const mainnetConsents = pgTable('mainnet_consents', {
  userId: text('user_id').primaryKey().references(() => users.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  noticeVersion: text('notice_version').notNull(),
  adult: boolean('adult').notNull(),
  acceptedAt: ts('accepted_at').notNull(),
});

export const mainnetExclusions = pgTable('mainnet_exclusions', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  userId: text('user_id').notNull().references(() => users.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),
  until: ts('until').notNull(),
  createdAt: ts('created_at').notNull(),
}, (t) => [index('mainnet_exclusions_user_idx').on(t.userId, t.until)]);

/** Append-only (a trigger refuses UPDATE and DELETE), hash-chained: `payload` is the exact JSON the hash covers. */
export const adminAudit = pgTable('admin_audit', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  adminId: text('admin_id').notNull(),
  action: text('action').notNull(),
  payload: text('payload').notNull(),
  prevHash: text('prev_hash'),
  hash: text('hash').notNull(),
  createdAt: ts('created_at').notNull(),
});

export const ledgerSeasons = pgTable('ledger_seasons', {
  id: text('id').primaryKey(),
  startedAt: ts('started_at').notNull(),
  endedAt: ts('ended_at'),
  summary: jsonb('summary').$type<Record<string, unknown>>().notNull().default({}),
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { MemoryChain } from '../src/world/chain.ts';
import { mulberry32 } from '../src/seed/rng.ts';
import { loadConfig } from '../src/config.ts';
import { priceOf, quoteBuy, quoteSell, type PoolState } from '../src/ledger/curve.ts';
import { DEFAULT_RULES, rulesFromConfig } from '../src/ledger/rules.ts';

const TX_FEE = 5000; // MemoryChain's network fee, which the ledger does not charge

describe('the ledger curve is MemoryChain’s curve', () => {
  it('gives the same tokens, SOL and price over random trades', async () => {
    for (const seed of [1, 2, 3]) {
      const rng = mulberry32(seed), mem = new MemoryChain(), a = Keypair.generate();
      mem.airdrop(a.publicKey.toBase58(), 1_000 * 1e9);
      const { mint } = await mem.launch(a, { name: 'C', symbol: 'C', uri: 'u', initialBuyLamports: 0 });
      const p: PoolState = { sol: 0, sold: 0 };
      let held = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) {
        if (held > 0 && rng() < 0.4) {
          const raw = Math.max(1, Math.floor(held * rng()));
          const m = await mem.sell(a, mint, raw, 0), q = quoteSell(p, raw);
          expect(m.lamportsDelta + TX_FEE).toBe(q.out);
          p.sol -= q.gross; p.sold -= raw; held -= raw;
        } else {
          const lamports = Math.floor(rng() * 50_000_000) + 1_000;
          let m;
          try { m = await mem.buy(a, mint, lamports, 0); } catch { break; } // the curve graduated
          const q = quoteBuy(p, lamports);
          expect(-m.lamportsDelta - TX_FEE).toBe(q.spent);
          expect(m.tokenDelta).toBe(q.out);
          p.sol += q.net; p.sold += q.out; held += q.out;
        }
        expect(priceOf(p)).toBe((await mem.curve(mint))!.priceLamports);
      }
    }
  });
  it('accounts for every lamport of a buy: spent = net into the pool + the 1% fee', () => {
    const q = quoteBuy({ sol: 0, sold: 0 }, 10_000_000);
    expect(q.net + q.fee).toBe(q.spent);
    expect(q.fee).toBe(q.spent - Math.floor(q.spent * 0.99));
  });
  it('accounts for every lamport of a sale: gross = out + fee', () => {
    const b = quoteBuy({ sol: 0, sold: 0 }, 20_000_000);
    const s = quoteSell({ sol: b.net, sold: b.out }, Math.floor(b.out / 2));
    expect(s.out + s.fee).toBe(s.gross);
  });
  it('takes rules from the config', () => {
    const r = rulesFromConfig(loadConfig({ NODE_ENV: 'test', SESSION_SECRET: 's', MAINNET_HOUSE_FEE_BPS: '0', MAINNET_MAX_TRADE_SOL: '0.02' }));
    expect(r).toEqual({ ...DEFAULT_RULES, houseFeeBps: 0, maxTradeLamports: 20_000_000 });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the curve**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/curve.ts`:

```ts
import { CURVE, RAW_PER_TOKEN } from '../world/chain.ts';

/** A pool as the curve sees it: SOL in its reserve and tokens sold, both in base units. */
export interface PoolState { sol: number; sold: number }
export interface BuyQuote { spent: number; net: number; fee: number; out: number; graduates: boolean }
export interface SellQuote { gross: number; out: number; fee: number }

const { V_SOL, V_TOKENS, CURVE_TOKENS, GRADUATE_AT, TRADE_FEE_BPS } = CURVE;
const KEEP = 1 - TRADE_FEE_BPS / 10_000;

/**
 * MemoryChain's swapIn, as a pure function (same expressions, so the same floats): a buy past the threshold takes only what
 * completes the curve. `spent` leaves the buyer, `net` enters the pool and `fee` (1%) is split by the ledger.
 */
export function quoteBuy(p: PoolState, lamportsIn: number): BuyQuote {
  const spent = Math.min(lamportsIn, Math.ceil((GRADUATE_AT - p.sol) / KEEP));
  const net = Math.min(Math.floor(spent * KEEP), GRADUATE_AT - p.sol);
  const k = (V_SOL + p.sol) * (V_TOKENS - p.sold);
  const out = Math.min(Math.floor(V_TOKENS - p.sold - k / (V_SOL + p.sol + net)), CURVE_TOKENS - p.sold);
  return { spent, net, fee: spent - net, out, graduates: p.sol + net >= GRADUATE_AT || p.sold + out >= CURVE_TOKENS };
}

/** MemoryChain's sell: `gross` leaves the pool, the seller gets `out` and the 1% `fee` is split by the ledger. */
export function quoteSell(p: PoolState, rawIn: number): SellQuote {
  const k = (V_SOL + p.sol) * (V_TOKENS - p.sold);
  const gross = Math.floor(V_SOL + p.sol - k / (V_TOKENS - p.sold + rawIn));
  const out = Math.floor(gross * (1 - TRADE_FEE_BPS / 10_000));
  return { gross, out, fee: gross - out };
}

/** Lamports per whole token, as MemoryChain prices it. */
export const priceOf = (p: PoolState) => ((V_SOL + p.sol) / (V_TOKENS - p.sold)) * RAW_PER_TOKEN;
```

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/rules.ts`:

```ts
import type { Config } from '../config.ts';

export const SOL = 1_000_000_000;
const MIN = 60_000, HOUR = 60 * MIN;

/** Every money rule of the ledger (mainnet spec §2–3), in one place so tests can tighten any of them. */
export interface LedgerRules {
  /** The house's part of the 1% trade fee; the creator gets the rest. */
  houseFeeBps: number;
  launchFeeLamports: number;
  maxTradeLamports: number;
  maxSendLamports: number;
  maxSendDailyLamports: number;
  vestingMs: number;
  maxCreatorSaleShare: number;
  rugMinShare: number;
  breakerDrop: number;
  breakerWindowMs: number;
  breakerPauseMs: number;
}

export const DEFAULT_RULES: LedgerRules = {
  houseFeeBps: 50, launchFeeLamports: 10_000_000, maxTradeLamports: 50_000_000, maxSendLamports: 10_000_000, maxSendDailyLamports: 50_000_000,
  vestingMs: 24 * HOUR, maxCreatorSaleShare: 0.3, rugMinShare: 0.25, breakerDrop: 0.5, breakerWindowMs: 10 * MIN, breakerPauseMs: 10 * MIN,
};

export function rulesFromConfig(c: Config): LedgerRules {
  return { ...DEFAULT_RULES, houseFeeBps: c.mainnetLimits.houseFeeBps, maxTradeLamports: Math.round(c.mainnetLimits.maxTradeSol * SOL) };
}
```

Create `apps/server/test/ledger-helpers.ts`:

```ts
import { depthTestCtx } from './depth-helpers.ts';
import { LedgerChain } from '../src/ledger/ledger-chain.ts';
import { DEFAULT_RULES, type LedgerRules } from '../src/ledger/rules.ts';
import { signerFor, walletOf } from '../src/world/wallets.ts';

/** A seeded 50-agent world whose EngineDeps run on a LedgerChain; `fund(i, sol)` credits agent i from outside. */
export async function ledgerCtx(rules: Partial<LedgerRules> = {}) {
  const w = await depthTestCtx();
  const chain = new LedgerChain(w.t.db, w.t.clock.now, { ...DEFAULT_RULES, ...rules });
  w.d.chain = chain;
  const pk = async (i: number) => (await walletOf(w.t.db, w.agents[i]!.id))!.publicKey;
  const signer = (i: number) => signerFor(w.t.db, w.agents[i]!.id, w.key);
  let n = 0;
  const fund = async (i: number, sol: number) => chain.credit(await pk(i), Math.round(sol * 1e9), 'deposit', `test-deposit-${++n}`);
  return { ...w, chain, pk, signer, fund };
}
```

```ts
const SOL = 1e9;
let c: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof ledgerCtx>>;
afterEach(async () => { await c.t.close(); });

describe('LedgerChain', () => {
  it('credits from outside with a journal pair, and the books balance', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    await c.fund(0, 0.2);
    expect(await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).toEqual({ lamports: 0.2 * SOL, tokens: [] });
    const rows = await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerJournal);
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.lamportsDelta).sort((a, b) => a - b)).toEqual([-0.2 * SOL, 0.2 * SOL]);
    expect(await books(c.t.db)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, ledgerTotal: 0.2 * SOL, externalNet: -0.2 * SOL, mismatches: [] });
  });

  it('launches for a flat 0.01 SOL to the house, with a first buy on the curve', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    await c.fund(0, 0.1);
    const r = await c.chain.launch(await c.signer(0), { name: 'Moth', symbol: 'MOTH', uri: 'u', initialBuyLamports: 0.02 * SOL });
    const q = quoteBuy({ sol: 0, sold: 0 }, 0.02 * SOL);
    const houseFee = Math.floor((q.spent * 50) / 10_000);
    expect(r.signature).toMatch(/^ldg_/);
    expect(r.tokenDelta).toBe(q.out);
    // The creator gets its own creator share of the 1% back; the house keeps its 0.5% plus the launch fee.
    expect(r.lamportsDelta).toBe(-(0.01 * SOL) - q.spent + (q.fee - houseFee));
    expect((await c.chain.balances(HOUSE)).lamports).toBe(0.01 * SOL + houseFee);
    expect(await c.chain.curve(r.mint)).toMatchObject({ quoteReserveLamports: q.net, graduated: false });
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it('buys and sells on the curve, paying the coin’s creator its share of the fee', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    await c.fund(0, 0.1); await c.fund(1, 0.1);
    const { mint } = await c.chain.launch(await c.signer(0), { name: 'X', symbol: 'X', uri: 'u', initialBuyLamports: 0 });
    const creatorBefore = (await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).lamports;
    const b = await c.chain.buy(await c.signer(1), mint, 0.03 * SOL, 0);
    const qb = quoteBuy({ sol: 0, sold: 0 }, 0.03 * SOL);
    expect(b).toMatchObject({ lamportsDelta: -qb.spent, tokenDelta: qb.out, feeLamports: 0 });
    expect((await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).lamports - creatorBefore).toBe(qb.fee - Math.floor((qb.spent * 50) / 10_000));
    const s = await c.chain.sell(await c.signer(1), mint, Math.floor(qb.out / 2), 0);
    const qs = quoteSell({ sol: qb.net, sold: qb.out }, Math.floor(qb.out / 2));
    expect(s).toMatchObject({ lamportsDelta: qs.out, tokenDelta: -Math.floor(qb.out / 2) });
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it('sends SOL from one agent to another', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    await c.fund(0, 0.1);
    const r = await c.chain.sendSol(await c.signer(0), await c.pk(1), 0.01 * SOL);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ feeLamports: 0 });
    expect((await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(1))).lamports).toBe(0.01 * SOL);
  });

  it('refuses what the wallet cannot cover, and writes nothing', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    await c.fund(0, 0.005);
    const before = (await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerJournal)).length;
    await expect(c.chain.launch(await c.signer(0), { name: 'X', symbol: 'X', uri: 'u', initialBuyLamports: 0 })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS' });
    expect((await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerJournal)).length).toBe(before);
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it('refuses a key that is not an agent wallet', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    await expect(c.chain.buy(Keypair.generate(), 'nope', 1000, 0)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'UNKNOWN_ACCOUNT' });
  });

  it('keeps the books balanced under concurrent trades on one coin (review focus 3)', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    for (let i = 0; i < 13; i++) await c.fund(i, 0.1);
    const { mint } = await c.chain.launch(await c.signer(0), { name: 'Hot', symbol: 'HOT', uri: 'u', initialBuyLamports: 0 });
    const signers = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => c.signer(i + 1)));
    const buys = await Promise.allSettled(signers.map((s) => c.chain.buy(s, mint, 0.03 * SOL, 0)));
    expect(buys.every((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
    const sells = await Promise.allSettled(signers.slice(0, 6).map(async (s) => {
      const held = (await c.chain.balances(s.publicKey.toBase58())).tokens[0]!.raw;
      return c.chain.sell(s, mint, Math.floor(held / 2), 0);
    }));
    expect(sells.every((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled')).toBe(true);
    const b = await books(c.t.db);
    expect(b).toMatchObject({ ok: true, mismatches: [] });
    expect(b.ledgerTotal).toBe(13 * 0.1 * SOL);
    for (let i = 0; i < 13; i++) expect((await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(i))).lamports).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/accounts.ts`:

```ts
import { and, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { ledgerAccounts, ledgerHoldings, ledgerJournal, ledgerPools, wallets, type JournalKind } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { newId } from '../lib/ids.ts';
import { ChainError } from '../world/chain.ts';
import { getSetting } from '../world/launchpad.ts';

export type { JournalKind };
export const HOUSE = 'house';
/** The world outside the books: deposits come from it and withdrawals go to it. Its journal sum is minus everything inside. */
export const EXTERNAL = 'external';
/** SOL paid out to an owner or the cold address but not sent on-chain yet. */
export const WITHDRAWALS = 'withdrawals';
const POOL = 'pool:';
export const poolAccount = (mint: string) => `${POOL}${mint}`;
export const SEASON_KEY = 'mainnet_season';
export const FROZEN_KEY = 'mainnet_frozen';

export interface Entry { kind: JournalKind; ref: string; season: string; at: Date; mint?: string | null }

/** The current season ('dev' until a season starts, Task 14). Journal rows carry it, and the books read only it. */
export async function currentSeason(db: Db): Promise<string> { return (await getSetting(db, SEASON_KEY)) || 'dev'; }

/** An agent wallet's owner. Only agent wallets (and the internal accounts) hold SOL on the ledger. */
export async function ownerOfKey(db: Db, publicKey: string): Promise<string> {
  const [w] = await db.select({ ownerId: wallets.ownerId }).from(wallets).where(eq(wallets.publicKey, publicKey));
  if (!w) throw new ChainError('UNKNOWN_ACCOUNT', 'Only agent wallets hold SOL on the ledger');
  return w.ownerId;
}

export async function lamportsOf(db: Db, account: string): Promise<number> {
  if (account.startsWith(POOL)) {
    const [p] = await db.select({ s: ledgerPools.sol }).from(ledgerPools).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, account.slice(POOL.length)));
    return p?.s ?? 0;
  }
  const [a] = await db.select({ l: ledgerAccounts.lamports }).from(ledgerAccounts).where(eq(ledgerAccounts.publicKey, account));
  return a?.l ?? 0;
}

/** Creates the account row at 0 if it is missing (internal accounts own themselves). */
async function ensure(tx: Db, account: string) {
  if (account === EXTERNAL || account.startsWith(POOL)) return;
  const ownerId = account === HOUSE || account === WITHDRAWALS ? account : await ownerOfKey(tx, account);
  await tx.insert(ledgerAccounts).values({ publicKey: account, ownerId, lamports: 0 }).onConflictDoNothing();
}

/** Row-locks an account (or a pool) for the rest of the transaction and returns its SOL. */
async function lock(tx: Db, account: string): Promise<number> {
  if (account === EXTERNAL) return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  if (account.startsWith(POOL)) {
    const [p] = await tx.select({ s: ledgerPools.sol }).from(ledgerPools).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, account.slice(POOL.length))).for('update');
    if (!p) throw new ChainError('UNKNOWN_MINT', `No bonding-curve pool for ${account.slice(POOL.length)}`);
    return p.s;
  }
  const [a] = await tx.select({ l: ledgerAccounts.lamports }).from(ledgerAccounts).where(eq(ledgerAccounts.publicKey, account)).for('update');
  return a?.l ?? 0;
}

/** Throws INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS unless `account` holds at least `lamports` (and locks it). */
export async function need(tx: Db, account: string, lamports: number) {
  const have = await lock(tx, account);
  if (have < lamports) throw new ChainError('INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS', `Not enough SOL: the account has ${have / 1e9} SOL and this needs ${lamports / 1e9} SOL`);
}

async function adjust(tx: Db, account: string, delta: number) {
  if (account === EXTERNAL) return;
  if (account.startsWith(POOL)) {
    await tx.update(ledgerPools).set({ sol: sql`${ledgerPools.sol} + ${delta}` }).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, account.slice(POOL.length)));
    return;
  }
  await tx.update(ledgerAccounts).set({ lamports: sql`${ledgerAccounts.lamports} + ${delta}` }).where(eq(ledgerAccounts.publicKey, account));
}

/**
 * Moves SOL inside an open transaction: locks both sides in a stable order (two moves in opposite directions can't deadlock),
 * checks the payer, updates both balances and writes the journal pair. A zero amount or a move to oneself writes nothing.
 */
export async function move(tx: Db, m: Entry & { from: string; to: string; lamports: number }) {
  if (m.lamports === 0 || m.from === m.to) return;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(m.lamports) || m.lamports < 0) throw new ChainError('BAD_AMOUNT', 'Amounts are whole, positive lamports');
  await ensure(tx, m.from); await ensure(tx, m.to);
  for (const a of [m.from, m.to].sort()) await lock(tx, a);
  await need(tx, m.from, m.lamports);
  await adjust(tx, m.from, -m.lamports); await adjust(tx, m.to, m.lamports);
  await tx.insert(ledgerJournal).values([
    { id: newId(), season: m.season, kind: m.kind, account: m.from, counter: m.to, mint: m.mint ?? null, lamportsDelta: -m.lamports, tokenDelta: 0, ref: m.ref, createdAt: m.at },
    { id: newId(), season: m.season, kind: m.kind, account: m.to, counter: m.from, mint: m.mint ?? null, lamportsDelta: m.lamports, tokenDelta: 0, ref: m.ref, createdAt: m.at },
  ]);
}

/** Moves tokens between a holder and its pool (a pool's tokens are its `sold` counter, negated) and writes the journal pair. */
export async function moveTokens(tx: Db, m: Entry & { from: string; to: string; mint: string; raw: number }) {
  if (m.raw === 0 || m.from === m.to) return;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(m.raw) || m.raw < 0) throw new ChainError('BAD_AMOUNT', 'Token amounts are whole, positive base units');
  if (m.from.startsWith(POOL)) await tx.update(ledgerPools).set({ sold: sql`${ledgerPools.sold} + ${m.raw}` }).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, m.mint));
  else {
    const [h] = await tx.select({ raw: ledgerHoldings.raw }).from(ledgerHoldings).where(and(eq(ledgerHoldings.account, m.from), eq(ledgerHoldings.mint, m.mint))).for('update');
    if ((h?.raw ?? 0) < m.raw) throw new ChainError('INSUFFICIENT_TOKENS', `Holds ${h?.raw ?? 0} raw units, tried to move ${m.raw}`);
    await tx.update(ledgerHoldings).set({ raw: sql`${ledgerHoldings.raw} - ${m.raw}` }).where(and(eq(ledgerHoldings.account, m.from), eq(ledgerHoldings.mint, m.mint)));
  }
  if (m.to.startsWith(POOL)) await tx.update(ledgerPools).set({ sold: sql`${ledgerPools.sold} - ${m.raw}` }).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, m.mint));
  else await tx.insert(ledgerHoldings).values({ account: m.to, mint: m.mint, raw: m.raw })
    .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: [ledgerHoldings.account, ledgerHoldings.mint], set: { raw: sql`${ledgerHoldings.raw} + ${m.raw}` } });
  await tx.insert(ledgerJournal).values([
    { id: newId(), season: m.season, kind: m.kind, account: m.from, counter: m.to, mint: m.mint, lamportsDelta: 0, tokenDelta: -m.raw, ref: m.ref, createdAt: m.at },
    { id: newId(), season: m.season, kind: m.kind, account: m.to, counter: m.from, mint: m.mint, lamportsDelta: 0, tokenDelta: m.raw, ref: m.ref, createdAt: m.at },
  ]);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the books**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/books.ts`:

```ts
import { and, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { ledgerAccounts, ledgerHoldings, ledgerJournal, ledgerPools } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { EXTERNAL, currentSeason, poolAccount } from './accounts.ts';

export interface Books { season: string; accountsTotal: number; poolsTotal: number; ledgerTotal: number; externalNet: number; mismatches: string[]; ok: boolean }

/**
 * The books for the current season. Every balance must equal its journal sum, and everything inside the ledger must equal
 * minus the outside world's journal sum (deposits + house stake − sent withdrawals). This is the invariant of spec §2.
 */
export async function books(db: Db): Promise<Books> {
  const season = await currentSeason(db);
  const [accounts, pools, holdings, sums, tokenSums] = await Promise.all([
    db.select().from(ledgerAccounts),
    db.select().from(ledgerPools),
    db.select().from(ledgerHoldings),
    db.select({ account: ledgerJournal.account, n: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${ledgerJournal.lamportsDelta}), 0)::float8` })
      .from(ledgerJournal).where(eq(ledgerJournal.season, season)).groupBy(ledgerJournal.account),
    db.select({ account: ledgerJournal.account, mint: ledgerJournal.mint, n: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${ledgerJournal.tokenDelta}), 0)::float8` })
      .from(ledgerJournal).where(and(eq(ledgerJournal.season, season), sql`${ledgerJournal.tokenDelta} <> 0`)).groupBy(ledgerJournal.account, ledgerJournal.mint),
  ]);
  const sol = new Map(sums.map((r) => [r.account, r.n]));
  const tok = new Map(tokenSums.map((r) => [`${r.account}|${r.mint}`, r.n]));
  const mismatches: string[] = [];
  for (const a of accounts) {
    if (a.lamports < 0) mismatches.push(`${a.publicKey} is negative (${a.lamports})`);
    if ((sol.get(a.publicKey) ?? 0) !== a.lamports) mismatches.push(`${a.publicKey}: balance ${a.lamports}, journal ${sol.get(a.publicKey) ?? 0}`);
  }
  for (const p of pools) {
    const acct = poolAccount(p.mint);
    if ((sol.get(acct) ?? 0) !== p.sol) mismatches.push(`${acct}: reserve ${p.sol}, journal ${sol.get(acct) ?? 0}`);
    if (-(tok.get(`${acct}|${p.mint}`) ?? 0) !== p.sold) mismatches.push(`${acct}: sold ${p.sold}, journal ${-(tok.get(`${acct}|${p.mint}`) ?? 0)}`);
  }
  for (const h of holdings) if ((tok.get(`${h.account}|${h.mint}`) ?? 0) !== h.raw) mismatches.push(`${h.account} ${h.mint}: holds ${h.raw}, journal ${tok.get(`${h.account}|${h.mint}`) ?? 0}`);
  const accountsTotal = accounts.reduce((n, a) => n + a.lamports, 0), poolsTotal = pools.reduce((n, p) => n + p.sol, 0);
  const ledgerTotal = accountsTotal + poolsTotal, externalNet = sol.get(EXTERNAL) ?? 0;
  if (ledgerTotal + externalNet !== 0) mismatches.push(`inside ${ledgerTotal} ≠ outside ${-externalNet}`);
  return { season, accountsTotal, poolsTotal, ledgerTotal, externalNet, mismatches, ok: mismatches.length === 0 };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write LedgerChain**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/ledger-chain.ts`:

```ts
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { and, eq, gt } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { ledgerAccounts, ledgerHoldings, ledgerPools } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { newId } from '../lib/ids.ts';
import { CURVE, ChainError, GRADUATED_MESSAGE, type Chain, type ChainLimits, type CurveState, type LaunchInput, type LaunchResult, type TradeResult, type WalletBalances } from '../world/chain.ts';
import { getSetting, setSetting } from '../world/launchpad.ts';
import { EXTERNAL, FROZEN_KEY, HOUSE, currentSeason, move, moveTokens, need, ownerOfKey, poolAccount, type JournalKind } from './accounts.ts';
import { priceOf, quoteBuy, quoteSell } from './curve.ts';
import { DEFAULT_RULES, type LedgerRules } from './rules.ts';

type PoolRow = typeof ledgerPools.$inferSelect;
const asDb = (t: unknown) => t as Db;

/**
 * Agent coins on Shillex's own books (mainnet spec §2): MemoryChain's curve, persisted, one database transaction per action
 * with the pool and the accounts row-locked. Nothing here touches a blockchain: real SOL moves only through Custody.
 */
export class LedgerChain implements Chain {
  readonly network = 'ledger' as const;
  private frozenReason: string | null = null;
  constructor(private db: Db, private now: () => Date, readonly rules: LedgerRules = DEFAULT_RULES) {}

  get limits(): ChainLimits {
    const r = this.rules;
    return { launchCostLamports: r.launchFeeLamports, rugMinShare: r.rugMinShare, maxCreatorSaleShare: r.maxCreatorSaleShare, maxTradeLamports: r.maxTradeLamports, maxSendLamports: r.maxSendLamports, frozen: this.frozenReason !== null };
  }
  /** Why the books are frozen, or null. */
  get frozen(): string | null { return this.frozenReason; }
  async loadFrozen() { this.frozenReason = (await getSetting(this.db, FROZEN_KEY)) || null; }
  async freeze(reason: string) { await setSetting(this.db, FROZEN_KEY, reason); this.frozenReason = reason; }
  async unfreeze() { await setSetting(this.db, FROZEN_KEY, ''); this.frozenReason = null; }

  private sig() { return `ldg_${newId()}`; }

  async balances(publicKey: string): Promise<WalletBalances> {
    const [a] = await this.db.select({ l: ledgerAccounts.lamports }).from(ledgerAccounts).where(eq(ledgerAccounts.publicKey, publicKey));
    const held = await this.db.select().from(ledgerHoldings).where(and(eq(ledgerHoldings.account, publicKey), gt(ledgerHoldings.raw, 0)));
    const mine = held.length ? await this.db.select().from(ledgerPools).where(eq(ledgerPools.creatorAccount, publicKey)) : [];
    const now = this.now();
    return {
      lamports: a?.l ?? 0,
      tokens: held.map((h) => {
        const p = mine.find((x) => x.mint === h.mint);
        const lockedRaw = p ? Math.min(h.raw, this.locked(p, now)) : 0;
        return lockedRaw > 0 ? { mint: h.mint, raw: h.raw, lockedRaw } : { mint: h.mint, raw: h.raw };
      }),
    };
  }

  async launch(signer: Keypair, input: LaunchInput): Promise<LaunchResult> {
    this.checkOpen();
    this.checkBuySize(input.initialBuyLamports);
    const pk = signer.publicKey.toBase58(), ref = this.sig(), at = this.now(), fee = this.rules.launchFeeLamports;
    const mint = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(), pool = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58();
    return this.db.transaction(async (t) => {
      const tx = asDb(t), season = await currentSeason(tx);
      const creatorId = await ownerOfKey(tx, pk);
      await need(tx, pk, fee + input.initialBuyLamports);
      await move(tx, { from: pk, to: HOUSE, lamports: fee, kind: 'launch_fee', ref, season, at, mint });
      await tx.insert(ledgerPools).values({ mint, pool, creatorId, creatorAccount: pk, sol: 0, sold: 0, vestedRaw: 0, graduated: false, launchedAt: at });
      if (input.initialBuyLamports <= 0) {
        await this.settle(tx, mint, at);
        return { mint, pool, signature: ref, lamportsDelta: -fee, tokenDelta: 0, feeLamports: 0 };
      }
      const first = await this.swapIn(tx, pk, mint, input.initialBuyLamports, ref, season, at);
      await tx.update(ledgerPools).set({ vestedRaw: first.out }).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, mint));
      return { mint, pool, signature: ref, lamportsDelta: -fee + first.delta, tokenDelta: first.out, feeLamports: 0 };
    });
  }

  async buy(signer: Keypair, mint: string, lamportsIn: number, _slippageBps?: number): Promise<TradeResult> {
    this.checkOpen();
    this.checkBuySize(lamportsIn);
    const pk = signer.publicKey.toBase58(), ref = this.sig(), at = this.now();
    return this.db.transaction(async (t) => {
      const tx = asDb(t), season = await currentSeason(tx);
      await ownerOfKey(tx, pk);
      const r = await this.swapIn(tx, pk, mint, lamportsIn, ref, season, at);
      return { signature: ref, lamportsDelta: r.delta, tokenDelta: r.out, feeLamports: 0 };
    });
  }

  async sell(signer: Keypair, mint: string, rawTokensIn: number, _slippageBps?: number): Promise<TradeResult> {
    this.checkOpen();
    if (!(rawTokensIn > 0)) throw new ChainError('INSUFFICIENT_TOKENS', 'Sell a positive number of tokens');
    const pk = signer.publicKey.toBase58(), ref = this.sig(), at = this.now();
    return this.db.transaction(async (t) => {
      const tx = asDb(t), season = await currentSeason(tx);
      await ownerOfKey(tx, pk);
      const p = await this.lockPool(tx, mint, at);
      const [h] = await tx.select({ raw: ledgerHoldings.raw }).from(ledgerHoldings).where(and(eq(ledgerHoldings.account, pk), eq(ledgerHoldings.mint, mint))).for('update');
      const held = h?.raw ?? 0;
      if (held < rawTokensIn) throw new ChainError('INSUFFICIENT_TOKENS', `Holds ${held} raw units, tried to sell ${rawTokensIn}`);
      this.checkSell(p, pk, held, rawTokensIn, at);
      const q = quoteSell(p, rawTokensIn);
      const house = Math.min(q.fee, Math.floor((q.gross * this.rules.houseFeeBps) / 10_000)), creator = q.fee - house;
      await moveTokens(tx, { from: pk, to: poolAccount(mint), mint, raw: rawTokensIn, kind: 'sell', ref, season, at });
      await move(tx, { from: poolAccount(mint), to: pk, lamports: q.out, kind: 'sell', ref, season, at, mint });
      await move(tx, { from: poolAccount(mint), to: HOUSE, lamports: house, kind: 'trade_fee', ref, season, at, mint });
      await move(tx, { from: poolAccount(mint), to: p.creatorAccount, lamports: creator, kind: 'trade_fee', ref, season, at, mint });
      await this.settle(tx, mint, at);
      return { signature: ref, lamportsDelta: q.out + (p.creatorAccount === pk ? creator : 0), tokenDelta: -rawTokensIn, feeLamports: 0 };
    });
  }

  async sendSol(signer: Keypair, to: string, lamports: number) {
    this.checkOpen();
    const pk = signer.publicKey.toBase58(), ref = this.sig(), at = this.now();
    return this.db.transaction(async (t) => {
      const tx = asDb(t), season = await currentSeason(tx);
      await ownerOfKey(tx, pk);
      await this.checkSend(tx, pk, to, lamports, at);
      await move(tx, { from: pk, to, lamports, kind: 'send', ref, season, at });
      return { signature: ref, feeLamports: 0 };
    });
  }

  async curve(mint: string): Promise<CurveState | null> {
    const [p] = await this.db.select().from(ledgerPools).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, mint));
    if (!p) return null;
    return { mint, pool: p.pool, priceLamports: priceOf(p), curveProgress: Math.min(1, p.sol / CURVE.GRADUATE_AT), graduated: p.graduated, quoteReserveLamports: p.sol };
  }

  /** SOL entering the books from outside: a deposit, the house stake. `ref` is the on-chain signature or another unique id. */
  async credit(to: string, lamports: number, kind: JournalKind, ref: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.transfer(EXTERNAL, to, lamports, kind, ref);
  }

  /** SOL between two accounts on the books: opening balances from the house, withdrawals into the pending account. */
  async transfer(from: string, to: string, lamports: number, kind: JournalKind, ref: string): Promise<void> {
    const at = this.now();
    await this.db.transaction(async (t) => { const tx = asDb(t); await move(tx, { from, to, lamports, kind, ref, season: await currentSeason(tx), at }); });
  }

  /** A buy inside an open transaction: SOL into the pool (fee split creator/house), tokens out. Returns the signer's SOL change. */
  private async swapIn(tx: Db, pk: string, mint: string, lamportsIn: number, ref: string, season: string, at: Date): Promise<{ out: number; delta: number }> {
    const p = await this.lockPool(tx, mint, at);
    const q = quoteBuy(p, lamportsIn);
    if (q.out <= 0) throw new ChainError('CURVE_FULL', 'The curve cannot fill this trade; try a smaller amount');
    await need(tx, pk, q.spent);
    const house = Math.min(q.fee, Math.floor((q.spent * this.rules.houseFeeBps) / 10_000)), creator = q.fee - house;
    await move(tx, { from: pk, to: poolAccount(mint), lamports: q.net, kind: 'buy', ref, season, at, mint });
    await move(tx, { from: pk, to: HOUSE, lamports: house, kind: 'trade_fee', ref, season, at, mint });
    await move(tx, { from: pk, to: p.creatorAccount, lamports: creator, kind: 'trade_fee', ref, season, at, mint });
    await moveTokens(tx, { from: poolAccount(mint), to: pk, mint, raw: q.out, kind: 'buy', ref, season, at });
    await this.settle(tx, mint, at);
    return { out: q.out, delta: -q.spent + (p.creatorAccount === pk ? creator : 0) };
  }

  private async lockPool(tx: Db, mint: string, at: Date): Promise<PoolRow> {
    const [p] = await tx.select().from(ledgerPools).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, mint)).for('update');
    if (!p) throw new ChainError('UNKNOWN_MINT', `No bonding-curve pool for mint ${mint}`);
    if (p.graduated) throw new ChainError('GRADUATED', GRADUATED_MESSAGE);
    this.checkPaused(p, at);
    return p;
  }

  /** After a trade: graduation (curve complete, trading ends; never a public pool), then the price hook. */
  private async settle(tx: Db, mint: string, at: Date) {
    const [p] = await tx.select().from(ledgerPools).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, mint));
    if (p!.sol >= CURVE.GRADUATE_AT || p!.sold >= CURVE.CURVE_TOKENS) await tx.update(ledgerPools).set({ graduated: true }).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, mint));
    await this.afterTrade(tx, p!, at);
  }

  // ── Safety hooks: Task 5 fills these with the spec's rules. ──
  private checkOpen() {}
  private checkBuySize(_lamports: number) {}
  private checkSell(_p: PoolRow, _pk: string, _held: number, _raw: number, _at: Date) {}
  private checkPaused(_p: PoolRow, _at: Date) {}
  private async afterTrade(_tx: Db, _p: PoolRow, _at: Date) {}
  private locked(_p: PoolRow, _now: Date): number { return 0; }
  private async checkSend(tx: Db, _from: string, to: string, _lamports: number, _at: Date) { await ownerOfKey(tx, to); }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the pure rules**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/safety.ts`:

```ts
/** The part of a creator's launch buy still locked: it unlocks linearly over `vestingMs` (mainnet spec §3). */
export const vestingLocked = (vestedRaw: number, launchedAt: Date, now: Date, vestingMs: number) =>
  Math.floor(vestedRaw * Math.max(0, 1 - (now.getTime() - launchedAt.getTime()) / vestingMs));

/** True when `price` has fallen by `drop` or more from the highest price in the window. */
export const breakerTripped = (recentPrices: number[], price: number, drop: number) =>
  recentPrices.length > 0 && price <= Math.max(...recentPrices) * (1 - drop);
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Fill the hooks in LedgerChain**

In `apps/server/src/ledger/ledger-chain.ts`, extend the imports:

```ts
import { and, eq, gt, gte, lt, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { ledgerAccounts, ledgerHoldings, ledgerJournal, ledgerPools, ledgerPrices, users, wallets } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { breakerTripped, vestingLocked } from './safety.ts';
```

Replace the whole `// ── Safety hooks …` block, down to the class's closing brace, with:

```ts
  // ── Safety rules (mainnet spec §3) ──
  private checkOpen() {
    if (this.frozenReason) throw new ChainError('FROZEN', 'Trading is paused while the books are checked');
  }
  private checkBuySize(lamports: number) {
    if (lamports > this.rules.maxTradeLamports) throw new ChainError('TRADE_TOO_LARGE', `One trade spends at most ${this.rules.maxTradeLamports / 1e9} SOL`);
  }
  /** A creator's launch buy unlocks over 24 h, and one creator sale moves at most 30% of the bag. Other holders sell freely. */
  private checkSell(p: PoolRow, pk: string, held: number, raw: number, at: Date) {
    if (p.creatorAccount !== pk) return;
    const free = held - Math.min(held, this.locked(p, at));
    if (raw > free) throw new ChainError('VESTING_LOCKED', `Only ${free} of your ${held} raw units are unlocked: a creator's launch buy unlocks over 24 hours`);
    const cap = Math.ceil(held * this.rules.maxCreatorSaleShare);
    if (raw > cap) throw new ChainError('SALE_TOO_LARGE', `A creator sells at most ${Math.round(this.rules.maxCreatorSaleShare * 100)}% of the bag at once (${cap} raw units now)`);
  }
  private checkPaused(p: PoolRow, at: Date) {
    if (p.pausedUntil && p.pausedUntil > at) throw new ChainError('PAUSED', `This coin fell too fast; trading reopens at ${p.pausedUntil.toISOString()}`);
  }
  /** Records the new price and pauses the coin when it fell `breakerDrop` within the window. Prices older than an hour go. */
  private async afterTrade(tx: Db, p: PoolRow, at: Date) {
    const price = priceOf(p), r = this.rules;
    await tx.delete(ledgerPrices).where(and(eq(ledgerPrices.mint, p.mint), lt(ledgerPrices.at, new Date(at.getTime() - 60 * 60_000))));
    const recent = await tx.select({ price: ledgerPrices.priceLamports }).from(ledgerPrices)
      .where(and(eq(ledgerPrices.mint, p.mint), gte(ledgerPrices.at, new Date(at.getTime() - r.breakerWindowMs))));
    await tx.insert(ledgerPrices).values({ id: newId(), mint: p.mint, at, priceLamports: price });
    if (breakerTripped(recent.map((x) => x.price), price, r.breakerDrop)) {
      await tx.update(ledgerPools).set({ pausedUntil: new Date(at.getTime() + r.breakerPauseMs) }).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, p.mint));
    }
  }
  private locked(p: PoolRow, now: Date): number { return vestingLocked(p.vestedRaw, p.launchedAt, now, this.rules.vestingMs); }
  /** SOL moves only between agents: at most `maxSendLamports` a transfer and `maxSendDailyLamports` per sender per UTC day. */
  private async checkSend(tx: Db, from: string, to: string, lamports: number, at: Date) {
    if (lamports > this.rules.maxSendLamports) throw new ChainError('SEND_LIMIT', `One transfer moves at most ${this.rules.maxSendLamports / 1e9} SOL`);
    const [r] = await tx.select({ kind: users.kind }).from(wallets).innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, wallets.ownerId)).where(eq(wallets.publicKey, to));
    if (r?.kind !== 'agent') throw new ChainError('SEND_NOT_ALLOWED', 'SOL moves only between agents here; nothing leaves the ledger except an owner’s withdrawal');
    const day = new Date(`${at.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00Z`);
    const [s] = await tx.select({ n: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(-${ledgerJournal.lamportsDelta}), 0)::float8` }).from(ledgerJournal)
      .where(and(eq(ledgerJournal.account, from), eq(ledgerJournal.kind, 'send'), lt(ledgerJournal.lamportsDelta, 0), gte(ledgerJournal.createdAt, day)));
    if ((s?.n ?? 0) + lamports > this.rules.maxSendDailyLamports) throw new ChainError('SEND_LIMIT', `An agent sends at most ${this.rules.maxSendDailyLamports / 1e9} SOL a day`);
  }
}
```

`afterTrade` reads the window **before** inserting the new price, so the check compares the new price with the earlier highs. Because `settle` runs right after `launch` creates the pool, the launch price is already in the window.

```ts
    for (let i = 0; i < 120; i++) { await engineTurn(c.d); c.t.clock.advance(4000); }
    expect(await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerJournal).where(inArray(ledgerJournal.kind, ['buy', 'sell', 'launch_fee', 'send']))).toEqual([]);

    const since = new Date(c.t.clock.now().getTime() - 3_600_000);

    expect((await rugSales(c.t.db, since)).length).toBe(1);

    expect(card.pnlSol).toBeCloseTo(0, 9);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/server/test/ledger-fakes.ts`:

```ts
import type { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { DEVNET_GENESIS, type CustodyRpc, type Transfer } from '../src/ledger/rpc.ts';

/** An in-memory chain for custody: balances, payments carrying Solana Pay references, and sends that can be made to fail. */
export class FakeRpc implements CustodyRpc {
  genesis = DEVNET_GENESIS;
  balances = new Map<string, number>();
  txs = new Map<string, Transfer>();
  order: string[] = [];
  sent: { from: string; to: string; lamports: number; signature: string }[] = [];
  failSend: Error | null = null;
  failBalance: Error | null = null;
  private n = 0;
  async genesisHash() { return this.genesis; }
  async balance(a: string) { if (this.failBalance) throw this.failBalance; return this.balances.get(a) ?? 0; }
  async signaturesFor(address: string) {
    return this.order.filter((s) => { const t = this.txs.get(s)!; return t.to === address || t.from === address || t.references.includes(address); }).reverse();
  }
  async transferIn(signature: string, to: string) { const t = this.txs.get(signature); return t && t.to === to ? t : null; }
  async send(from: Keypair, to: string, lamports: number) {
    if (this.failSend) throw this.failSend;
    const f = from.publicKey.toBase58(), signature = `send${++this.n}`;
    this.balances.set(f, (this.balances.get(f) ?? 0) - lamports - 5000);
    this.balances.set(to, (this.balances.get(to) ?? 0) + lamports);
    this.txs.set(signature, { from: f, to, lamports, references: [], feeLamports: 5000 }); this.order.push(signature);
    this.sent.push({ from: f, to, lamports, signature });
    return { signature, feeLamports: 5000 };
  }
  /** Someone's wallet pays `to`, optionally carrying a Solana Pay reference. Returns the signature. */
  pay(from: string, to: string, lamports: number, reference?: string) {
    const signature = `pay${++this.n}`;
    this.txs.set(signature, { from, to, lamports, references: reference ? [reference] : [], feeLamports: 5000 }); this.order.push(signature);
    this.balances.set(to, (this.balances.get(to) ?? 0) + lamports);
    return signature;
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { testDb } from './helpers.ts';
import { FakeRpc } from './ledger-fakes.ts';
import { DEVNET_GENESIS, assertCustodyNetwork, isAddress } from '../src/ledger/rpc.ts';
import { hotVault, parseMainnetKey } from '../src/ledger/vault.ts';
import { payUrl, solString } from '../src/ledger/solana-pay.ts';
import { MAINNET_GENESIS } from '../src/world/devnet-chain.ts';
import { decryptSecret } from '../src/world/wallets.ts';
import { ledgerVault } from '../src/db/schema.ts';

let close: (() => Promise<void>) | null = null;
afterEach(async () => { await close?.(); close = null; });

describe('custody network check', () => {
  it('accepts only mainnet-beta for mainnet, devnet for rehearsal, and neither for local', async () => {
    const rpc = new FakeRpc();
    rpc.genesis = MAINNET_GENESIS;
    await expect(assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, 'mainnet')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    await expect(assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, 'devnet')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'WRONG_NETWORK' });
    await expect(assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, 'local')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'WRONG_NETWORK' });
    rpc.genesis = DEVNET_GENESIS;
    await expect(assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, 'devnet')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    await expect(assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, 'mainnet')).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'WRONG_NETWORK' });
    rpc.genesis = 'LocalValidatorGenesis1111111111111111111111';
    await expect(assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, 'local')).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });
  it('knows a Solana address', () => {
    expect(isAddress(Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58())).toBe(true);
    expect(isAddress('nope')).toBe(false);
    expect(isAddress(undefined)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('hot vault', () => {
  it('is created once per network, encrypted with MAINNET_KEY', async () => {
    const h = await testDb(); close = () => h.close();
    const key = randomBytes(32), now = new Date();
    const a = await hotVault(h.db, key, 'devnet', now), b = await hotVault(h.db, key, 'devnet', now), m = await hotVault(h.db, key, 'mainnet', now);
    expect(a.publicKey).toBe(b.publicKey);
    expect(m.publicKey).not.toBe(a.publicKey);
    expect(a.signer().publicKey.toBase58()).toBe(a.publicKey);
    const [row] = await h.db.select().from(ledgerVault);
    expect(() => decryptSecret(row!.encryptedSecret, randomBytes(32))).toThrow();
  });
  it('wants exactly 32 bytes of base64', () => {
    expect(() => parseMainnetKey(undefined)).toThrow(/MAINNET_KEY/);
    expect(() => parseMainnetKey(Buffer.alloc(16).toString('base64'))).toThrow(/MAINNET_KEY/);
    expect(parseMainnetKey(Buffer.alloc(32, 1).toString('base64')).length).toBe(32);
  });
});

describe('Solana Pay', () => {
  it('builds a transfer request with the amount in SOL and the reference', () => {
    expect(solString(100_000_000)).toBe('0.1');
    expect(solString(1)).toBe('0.000000001');
    expect(payUrl({ recipient: 'Hot1', lamports: 123_000_000, reference: 'Ref1', label: 'Shillex', message: 'Fund @moth' }))
      .toBe('solana:Hot1?amount=0.123&reference=Ref1&label=Shillex&message=Fund%20%40moth');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write errors, rpc, vault and solana-pay**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/errors.ts`:

```ts
import { AppError, errors } from '../lib/errors.ts';

/** A custody refusal a person can act on (a cap, a missing notice, frozen books). Routes answer 409 with its code. */
export class CustodyError extends Error {
  constructor(public code: string, message: string) { super(message); }
}

/** Postgres and PGlite both report a unique violation as 23505, sometimes on a wrapped cause. */
export function isUniqueViolation(e: unknown): boolean {
  for (let x = e as { code?: unknown; cause?: unknown } | undefined, i = 0; x && i < 5; x = x.cause as typeof x, i++) if (x.code === '23505') return true;
  return false;
}

export function toHttp(e: unknown): unknown {
  if (e instanceof CustodyError) return e.code === 'NOT_FOUND' ? errors.notFound() : errors.conflict(e.code, e.message);
  return e instanceof AppError ? e : e;
}
```

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/rpc.ts`:

```ts
import { Connection, PublicKey, SystemProgram, Transaction, sendAndConfirmTransaction, type Keypair, type ParsedInstruction } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { MAINNET_GENESIS } from '../world/devnet-chain.ts';
import { CustodyError } from './errors.ts';

/** Solana devnet's genesis hash: rehearsal custody runs here and nowhere else. */
export const DEVNET_GENESIS = 'EtWTRABZaYq6iMfeYKouRu166VU2xqa1wcaWoxPkrZBG';
export type CustodyNetwork = 'mainnet' | 'devnet' | 'local';
export interface Transfer { from: string; to: string; lamports: number; references: string[]; feeLamports: number }

/** Everything custody asks of Solana; a FakeRpc stands in for it in tests. */
export interface CustodyRpc {
  genesisHash(): Promise<string>;
  balance(address: string): Promise<number>;
  /** Finalized, successful signatures touching `address` (a vault or a Solana Pay reference), newest first. */
  signaturesFor(address: string): Promise<string[]>;
  /** The finalized plain SOL transfer to `to` in this transaction, or null (not finalized, failed, not a transfer to `to`, or several payers). */
  transferIn(signature: string, to: string): Promise<Transfer | null>;
  send(from: Keypair, to: string, lamports: number): Promise<{ signature: string; feeLamports: number }>;
}

export function isAddress(a: string | null | undefined): a is string {
  if (!a) return false;
  try { return new PublicKey(a).toBytes().length === 32; } catch { return false; }
}

/** Before any signature: mainnet-beta for MAINNET=on, devnet for rehearsal, a local validator (neither) for tests. */
export async function assertCustodyNetwork(rpc: CustodyRpc, network: CustodyNetwork): Promise<void> {
  const g = await rpc.genesisHash();
  if (network === 'mainnet' && g !== MAINNET_GENESIS) throw new CustodyError('WRONG_NETWORK', 'MAINNET=on needs MAINNET_RPC_URL on Solana mainnet-beta');
  if (network === 'devnet' && g !== DEVNET_GENESIS) throw new CustodyError('WRONG_NETWORK', 'Rehearsal custody runs on Solana devnet only; MAINNET_RPC_URL points elsewhere');
  if (network === 'local' && (g === MAINNET_GENESIS || g === DEVNET_GENESIS)) throw new CustodyError('WRONG_NETWORK', 'Local custody needs a local validator, not devnet or mainnet');
}

export class Web3CustodyRpc implements CustodyRpc {
  constructor(private conn: Connection) {}
  genesisHash() { return this.conn.getGenesisHash(); }
  balance(address: string) { return this.conn.getBalance(new PublicKey(address), 'finalized'); }
  async signaturesFor(address: string) {
    return (await this.conn.getSignaturesForAddress(new PublicKey(address), { limit: 100 }, 'finalized')).filter((s) => !s.err).map((s) => s.signature);
  }
  async transferIn(signature: string, to: string): Promise<Transfer | null> {
    const tx = await this.conn.getParsedTransaction(signature, { commitment: 'finalized', maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 0 });
    if (!tx?.meta || tx.meta.err) return null;
    const transfers = tx.transaction.message.instructions
      .filter((i): i is ParsedInstruction => 'parsed' in i && i.program === 'system' && (i.parsed as { type?: string }).type === 'transfer')
      .map((i) => (i.parsed as { info: { source: string; destination: string; lamports: number } }).info)
      .filter((x) => x.destination === to);
    const payers = new Set(transfers.map((x) => x.source));
    if (!transfers.length || payers.size !== 1) return null;
    return {
      from: transfers[0]!.source, to, lamports: transfers.reduce((n, x) => n + Number(x.lamports), 0),
      references: tx.transaction.message.accountKeys.map((k) => k.pubkey.toBase58()), feeLamports: tx.meta.fee,
    };
  }
  async send(from: Keypair, to: string, lamports: number) {
    const tx = new Transaction().add(SystemProgram.transfer({ fromPubkey: from.publicKey, toPubkey: new PublicKey(to), lamports }));
    const signature = await sendAndConfirmTransaction(this.conn, tx, [from], { commitment: 'finalized' });
    const parsed = await this.conn.getParsedTransaction(signature, { commitment: 'finalized', maxSupportedTransactionVersion: 0 });
    return { signature, feeLamports: parsed?.meta?.fee ?? 5000 };
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { ledgerVault } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { decryptSecret, encryptSecret } from '../world/wallets.ts';
import type { CustodyNetwork } from './rpc.ts';

export function parseMainnetKey(b64: string | undefined): Buffer {
  const k = Buffer.from(b64 ?? '', 'base64');
  if (k.length !== 32) throw new Error('MAINNET_KEY must be 32 bytes, base64-encoded, and different from WORLD_KEY');
  return k;
}

/** The hot vault for a custody network: the only signing key custody holds (spec §4). Created on first use, encrypted with MAINNET_KEY. */
export async function hotVault(db: Db, key: Buffer, network: CustodyNetwork, now: Date): Promise<{ publicKey: string; signer(): Keypair }> {
  const id = `hot:${network}`;
  let [row] = await db.select().from(ledgerVault).where(eq(ledgerVault.id, id));
  if (!row) {
    const kp = Keypair.generate();
    await db.insert(ledgerVault).values({ id, publicKey: kp.publicKey.toBase58(), encryptedSecret: encryptSecret(kp.secretKey, key), createdAt: now }).onConflictDoNothing();
    [row] = await db.select().from(ledgerVault).where(eq(ledgerVault.id, id));
  }
  const r = row!;
  return { publicKey: r.publicKey, signer: () => Keypair.fromSecretKey(decryptSecret(r.encryptedSecret, key)) };
}
```

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/solana-pay.ts`:

```ts
/** Lamports as a plain decimal SOL string (no exponent, no trailing zeros), as Solana Pay wants it. */
export const solString = (lamports: number) => (lamports / 1e9).toFixed(9).replace(/\.?0+$/, '');

/** A Solana Pay transfer request (solanapay.com spec): wallets pay `recipient` and include `reference`, which custody looks for. */
export function payUrl(p: { recipient: string; lamports: number; reference: string; label: string; message: string }): string {
  return `solana:${p.recipient}?amount=${solString(p.lamports)}&reference=${p.reference}&label=${encodeURIComponent(p.label)}&message=${encodeURIComponent(p.message)}`;
}
```

```ts
import { ledgerCtx } from './ledger-helpers.ts';
import { acceptNotice, assertCanDeposit, currentNotice, excludeSelf, exclusionUntil, publishNotice } from '../src/ledger/consent.ts';

    await expect(acceptNotice(c.t.db, u.id, 'n1', false, c.t.clock.now())).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'ADULTS_ONLY' });
    await acceptNotice(c.t.db, u.id, 'n1', true, c.t.clock.now());
    await expect(assertCanDeposit(c.t.db, u.id, c.t.clock.now())).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    const n2 = await publishNotice(c.t.db, 'Position B', c.t.clock.now());

    await expect(assertCanDeposit(c.t.db, u.id, c.t.clock.now())).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOTICE_REQUIRED' });
    await expect(acceptNotice(c.t.db, u.id, 'n1', true, c.t.clock.now())).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOTICE_OUTDATED' });
    expect((await currentNotice(c.t.db))!.legalPosition).toBe('Position B');
```

```ts
import { and, desc, eq, gt } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { RISK_POINTS, type MainnetNoticeView } from '@shillex/shared';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { mainnetConsents, mainnetExclusions } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { newId } from '../lib/ids.ts';
import { getSetting, setSetting } from '../world/launchpad.ts';
import { CustodyError } from './errors.ts';

export const NOTICE_KEY = 'mainnet_notice';
const DAY = 86_400_000;
export interface PublishedNotice { version: string; legalPosition: string; publishedAt: string }

/** The risk notice in force (its fixed points are RISK_POINTS); null until an admin publishes one with the legal position. */
export async function currentNotice(db: Db): Promise<PublishedNotice | null> {
  const raw = await getSetting(db, NOTICE_KEY);
  return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as PublishedNotice) : null;
}

/** A new version (n1, n2, …): everyone accepts it again before their next deposit. Called by the admin route (Task 12). */
export async function publishNotice(db: Db, legalPosition: string, now: Date): Promise<PublishedNotice> {
  const prev = await currentNotice(db);
  const next = { version: `n${prev ? Number(prev.version.slice(1)) + 1 : 1}`, legalPosition: legalPosition.trim(), publishedAt: now.toISOString() };
  await setSetting(db, NOTICE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
  return next;
}

export async function acceptNotice(db: Db, userId: string, version: string, adult: boolean, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  if (!adult) throw new CustodyError('ADULTS_ONLY', 'Real SOL is for people 18 or over');
  const n = await currentNotice(db);
  if (!n) throw new CustodyError('NO_NOTICE', 'There is no risk notice yet');
  if (version !== n.version) throw new CustodyError('NOTICE_OUTDATED', 'The risk notice changed; read the new one');
  await db.insert(mainnetConsents).values({ userId, noticeVersion: version, adult: true, acceptedAt: now })
    .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: mainnetConsents.userId, set: { noticeVersion: version, adult: true, acceptedAt: now } });
}

export async function exclusionUntil(db: Db, userId: string, now: Date): Promise<Date | null> {
  const [x] = await db.select({ until: mainnetExclusions.until }).from(mainnetExclusions)
    .where(and(eq(mainnetExclusions.userId, userId), gt(mainnetExclusions.until, now))).orderBy(desc(mainnetExclusions.until)).limit(1);
  return x?.until ?? null;
}

/** Locks the user's deposits for 7 or 30 days. A lock never gets shorter: the later end wins (spec §6). */
export async function excludeSelf(db: Db, userId: string, days: 7 | 30, now: Date): Promise<Date> {
  if (days !== 7 && days !== 30) throw new CustodyError('BAD_DAYS', 'Choose 7 or 30 days');
  const want = new Date(now.getTime() + days * DAY), have = await exclusionUntil(db, userId, now);
  if (have && have >= want) return have;
  await db.insert(mainnetExclusions).values({ id: newId(), userId, until: want, createdAt: now });
  return want;
}

export async function assertCanDeposit(db: Db, userId: string, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  const until = await exclusionUntil(db, userId, now);
  if (until) throw new CustodyError('EXCLUDED', `You locked deposits until ${until.toISOString()}`);
  const n = await currentNotice(db);
  const [c] = await db.select().from(mainnetConsents).where(eq(mainnetConsents.userId, userId));
  if (!n || !c || !c.adult || c.noticeVersion !== n.version) throw new CustodyError('NOTICE_REQUIRED', 'Read and accept the risk notice first');
}

export async function noticeView(db: Db, userId: string | null, now: Date): Promise<MainnetNoticeView> {
  const n = await currentNotice(db);
  const [c] = userId ? await db.select().from(mainnetConsents).where(eq(mainnetConsents.userId, userId)) : [];
  const until = userId ? await exclusionUntil(db, userId, now) : null;
  return { version: n?.version ?? null, points: RISK_POINTS, legalPosition: n?.legalPosition ?? null, accepted: Boolean(n && c?.adult && c.noticeVersion === n.version), excludedUntil: until?.toISOString() ?? null };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the public routes**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/routes.ts`:

```ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { MainnetMode } from '@shillex/shared';
import { requireUser } from '../lib/auth.ts';
import { errors } from '../lib/errors.ts';
import type { LedgerChain } from './ledger-chain.ts';
import { acceptNotice, excludeSelf, noticeView } from './consent.ts';
import { toHttp } from './errors.ts';
import type { Custody } from './custody.ts';

/** What the mainnet routes need from boot (Task 13 creates it). */
export interface MainnetHandles { mode: MainnetMode; chain: LedgerChain; custody: Custody | null; stop(): Promise<void> }

const humanOnly = (u: { kind: string }) => { if (u.kind !== 'human') throw errors.forbidden('HUMANS_ONLY', 'Only people fund agents'); };

/** Public mainnet routes. Registered only in a ledger world, so everywhere else they answer 404. */
export async function registerMainnetRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, h: MainnetHandles) {
  const { db, now } = app.deps;
  await app.register(async (r) => {
    r.get('/mainnet/notice', async (req) => noticeView(db, req.user?.id ?? null, now()));
    r.post('/mainnet/notice', { config: { rateLimit: { max: 20, timeWindow: '1 minute' } } }, async (req, reply) => {
      const u = requireUser(req); humanOnly(u);
      const b = z.object({ version: z.string().min(1).max(20), adult: z.boolean() }).parse(req.body);
      try { await acceptNotice(db, u.id, b.version, b.adult, now()); } catch (e) { throw toHttp(e); }
      return reply.status(204).send();
    });
    r.post('/mainnet/exclusion', { config: { rateLimit: { max: 10, timeWindow: '1 minute' } } }, async (req) => {
      const u = requireUser(req); humanOnly(u);
      const b = z.object({ days: z.union([z.literal(7), z.literal(30)]) }).parse(req.body);
      return { until: (await excludeSelf(db, u.id, b.days, now())).toISOString() };
    });
  }, { prefix: '/api' });
  void h;
}
```

  - `Custody` with `hot()`, `caps()`, `reserves()`, `room(agentId, userId, exceptId?)`, `createDeposit({ agentId, userId, lamports })`, and `pollDeposits(): Promise<{ credited; refunded; ignored; expired }>`.
