```ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { FakeRpc } from './ledger-fakes.ts';
import { loadConfig } from '../src/config.ts';
import { Custody, capsFromConfig, type CustodyDeps } from '../src/ledger/custody.ts';
import { acceptNotice, publishNotice } from '../src/ledger/consent.ts';

/** A ledger world with custody on a FakeRpc (devnet genesis), a cold address, and one owner who accepted the notice and has a payout wallet. */
export async function custodyCtx(over: Partial<CustodyDeps> = {}, env: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  const c = await ledgerCtx();
  const rpc = new FakeRpc();
  const payouts = new Map<string, string>();
  const notes: string[] = [];
  const cold = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58();
  const custody = new Custody({
    db: c.t.db, rpc, key: randomBytes(32), network: 'devnet', cold, caps: capsFromConfig(loadConfig({ NODE_ENV: 'test', SESSION_SECRET: 's', ...env })),
    now: c.t.clock.now, chain: c.chain, worldKey: c.key, payoutOf: async (u) => payouts.get(u) ?? null,
    notify: async (_u, _a, s) => { notes.push(s); }, ...over,
  });
  const owner = await c.t.createUser('owner1');
  await publishNotice(c.t.db, 'Reviewed: test position.', c.t.clock.now());
  await acceptNotice(c.t.db, owner.id, 'n1', true, c.t.clock.now());
  const payer = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58();
  payouts.set(owner.id, payer);
  return { ...c, rpc, custody, owner, payer, payouts, cold, notes, agentId: c.agents[0]!.id };
}
```

Create `apps/server/test/ledger-deposits.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { custodyCtx } from './ledger-helpers.ts';
import { chainTxs, ledgerChainSeen, ledgerDeposits } from '../src/db/schema.ts';
import { books } from '../src/ledger/books.ts';
import { excludeSelf } from '../src/ledger/consent.ts';

const SOL = 1e9;
let c: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof custodyCtx>>;
afterEach(async () => { await c.t.close(); });
const agentBalance = async () => (await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).lamports;

describe('deposits', () => {
  it('credits an exact payment from the payout wallet at finalized, once', async () => {
    c = await custodyCtx();
    const d = await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.1 * SOL });
    expect(d).toMatchObject({ status: 'pending', lamports: 0.1 * SOL, hot: await c.custody.hot() });
    expect(d.url).toBe(`solana:${d.hot}?amount=0.1&reference=${d.reference}&label=Shillex&message=Fund%20your%20Shillex%20agent`);
    const sig = c.rpc.pay(c.payer, d.hot, 0.1 * SOL, d.reference);
    expect(await c.custody.pollDeposits()).toMatchObject({ credited: 1, refunded: 0 });
    expect(await agentBalance()).toBe(0.1 * SOL);
    expect((await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerDeposits))[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'credited', signature: sig });
    expect((await c.t.db.select().from(chainTxs).where(eq(chainTxs.kind, 'deposit')))[0]).toMatchObject({ agentId: c.agentId, lamportsDelta: 0.1 * SOL, signature: sig });
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(c.notes).toEqual(['got your 0.1 SOL deposit']);
  });

  it('credits a deposit once however many polls overlap (review focus 1)', async () => {
    c = await custodyCtx();
    const d = await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.1 * SOL });
    c.rpc.pay(c.payer, d.hot, 0.1 * SOL, d.reference);
    const runs = await Promise.all([c.custody.pollDeposits(), c.custody.pollDeposits(), c.custody.pollDeposits()]);
    expect(runs.reduce((n, r) => n + r.credited, 0)).toBe(1);
    await c.custody.pollDeposits();
    expect(await agentBalance()).toBe(0.1 * SOL);
    expect(c.rpc.sent).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('refunds a late, short or foreign payment, minus the network fee (review focus 4)', async () => {
    c = await custodyCtx();
    const late = await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.05 * SOL });
    c.t.clock.advance(31 * 60_000);
    c.rpc.pay(c.payer, late.hot, 0.05 * SOL, late.reference);
    const short = await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.02 * SOL });
    c.rpc.pay(c.payer, short.hot, 0.02 * SOL - 1, short.reference);
    const foreign = await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.02 * SOL });
    const stranger = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58();
    c.rpc.pay(stranger, foreign.hot, 0.02 * SOL, foreign.reference);
    const random = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58();
    c.rpc.pay(random, foreign.hot, 0.03 * SOL);
    expect(await c.custody.pollDeposits()).toMatchObject({ credited: 0, refunded: 4 });
    expect(await agentBalance()).toBe(0);
    expect(c.rpc.sent.map((s) => [s.to, s.lamports]).sort()).toEqual([
      [c.payer, 0.05 * SOL - 5000], [c.payer, 0.02 * SOL - 1 - 5000], [stranger, 0.02 * SOL - 5000], [random, 0.03 * SOL - 5000],
    ].sort());
    const statuses = (await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerDeposits)).map((r) => r.status).sort();
    expect(statuses).toEqual(['refunded', 'refunded', 'refunded']);
  });

  it('never refunds the cold address, and never retries a failed refund', async () => {
    c = await custodyCtx();
    const hot = await c.custody.hot();
    c.rpc.pay(c.cold, hot, 1 * SOL);
    c.rpc.failSend = new Error('rpc down');
    c.rpc.pay(Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(), hot, 0.01 * SOL);
    await c.custody.pollDeposits();
    c.rpc.failSend = null;
    await c.custody.pollDeposits();
    expect(c.rpc.sent).toEqual([]);
    const seen = await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerChainSeen);
    expect(seen.map((s) => s.outcome).sort()).toEqual(['from_cold', 'refund_failed']);
  });

  it('holds every cap, counting pending requests', async () => {
    c = await custodyCtx({}, { MAINNET_AGENT_CAP_SOL: '0.15', MAINNET_WORLD_CAP_SOL: '0.25' });
    await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.1 * SOL });
    await expect(c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.01 * SOL })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'CAP' }); // daily 0.1
    c.t.clock.advance(86_400_000);
    await expect(c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.06 * SOL })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'CAP', message: /lifetime/ });
    await c.chain.credit((await c.pk(5))!, 0.2 * SOL, 'house_stake', 'stake-x');
    await expect(c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.05 * SOL })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'CAP', message: /world/ });
  });

  it('refuses without the notice, during an exclusion, without a payout wallet, or while frozen', async () => {
    c = await custodyCtx();
    const other = await c.t.createUser('nonotice');
    c.payouts.set(other.id, c.payer);
    await expect(c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: other.id, lamports: 0.1 * SOL })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOTICE_REQUIRED' });
    c.payouts.delete(c.owner.id);
    await expect(c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.1 * SOL })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NO_PAYOUT_WALLET' });
    c.payouts.set(c.owner.id, c.payer);
    await expect(c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.001 * SOL })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'BAD_AMOUNT' });
    await c.chain.freeze('test');
    await expect(c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.1 * SOL })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'FROZEN' });
    await c.chain.unfreeze();
    await excludeSelf(c.t.db, c.owner.id, 7, c.t.clock.now());
    await expect(c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.1 * SOL })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'EXCLUDED' });
  });

  it('refuses to sign a refund on the wrong network', async () => {
    c = await custodyCtx();
    c.rpc.genesis = 'SomethingElse111111111111111111111111111111';
    c.rpc.pay(Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(), await c.custody.hot(), 0.01 * SOL);
    await expect(c.custody.pollDeposits()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'WRONG_NETWORK' });
    expect(c.rpc.sent).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

Replace `apps/server/src/ledger/custody.ts` with:

```ts
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { and, eq, gte, inArray, lt, sql, type SQL } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { DepositView } from '@shillex/shared';
import type { Config } from '../config.ts';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { ledgerChainSeen, ledgerDeposits } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { newId } from '../lib/ids.ts';
import { getSetting } from '../world/launchpad.ts';
import { recordTx } from '../world/ledger.ts';
import { walletOf } from '../world/wallets.ts';
import { EXTERNAL, currentSeason, move } from './accounts.ts';
import { books } from './books.ts';
import { assertCanDeposit } from './consent.ts';
import { CustodyError, isUniqueViolation } from './errors.ts';
import type { LedgerChain } from './ledger-chain.ts';
import { assertCustodyNetwork, type CustodyNetwork, type CustodyRpc, type Transfer } from './rpc.ts';
import { payUrl } from './solana-pay.ts';
import { hotVault } from './vault.ts';

const SOL = 1_000_000_000, MIN = 60_000, DAY = 86_400_000;
export const MIN_DEPOSIT = 10_000_000;
export const INTENT_MS = 30 * MIN;
/** A refund returns the payment minus this, the network fee of sending it back. */
export const REFUND_FEE_LAMPORTS = 5_000;
export const CAPS_KEY = 'mainnet_caps';

/** The caps of spec §4, in lamports. */
export interface MainnetCaps { agentLifetime: number; agentDaily: number; userMonthly: number; world: number; hotMax: number; outflowDaily: number }
export function capsFromConfig(c: Config): MainnetCaps {
  const l = c.mainnetLimits, s = (x: number) => Math.round(x * SOL);
  return { agentLifetime: s(l.agentCapSol), agentDaily: s(l.agentDailyCapSol), userMonthly: s(l.userMonthlyCapSol), world: s(l.worldCapSol), hotMax: s(l.hotMaxSol), outflowDaily: s(l.outflowDailyMaxSol) };
}
/** The caps in force: the config's, lowered (never raised) by an admin override (Task 12). */
export async function effectiveCaps(db: Db, base: MainnetCaps): Promise<MainnetCaps> {
  const raw = await getSetting(db, CAPS_KEY);
  const o = raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as Partial<MainnetCaps>) : {};
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(base).map(([k, v]) => [k, Math.min(v, o[k as keyof MainnetCaps] ?? v)])) as unknown as MainnetCaps;
}

export interface CustodyDeps {
  db: Db; rpc: CustodyRpc; key: Buffer; network: CustodyNetwork; cold: string | null; caps: MainnetCaps; now: () => Date; chain: LedgerChain;
  /** The agents' keys (WORLD_KEY): cash-out sells as the agent. Never used to sign on-chain. */
  worldKey: Buffer;
  /** The owner's payout wallet: deposits must come from it and withdrawals go only to it (LOGIN's payoutWallet, Task 17). */
  payoutOf(userId: string): Promise<string | null>;
  notify?(userId: string, agentId: string, summary: string): Promise<void>;
}
type DepositRow = typeof ledgerDeposits.$inferSelect;
class Skip extends Error {}

export const toDepositView = (r: DepositRow, hot: string): DepositView => ({
  id: r.id, agentId: r.agentId, reference: r.reference, lamports: r.lamports, sol: r.lamports / SOL, hot, status: r.status, expiresAt: r.expiresAt.toISOString(),
  url: payUrl({ recipient: hot, lamports: r.lamports, reference: r.reference, label: 'Shillex', message: 'Fund your Shillex agent' }),
});

/**
 * The only code that signs a real transaction (spec §7). Deposits arrive by Solana Pay reference and are credited only when they
 * match their request exactly; anything else goes back to its sender. Withdrawals (Task 10) go only to the payout wallet or,
 * for the house, the cold address.
 */
export class Custody {
  constructor(readonly d: CustodyDeps) {}
  get cold() { return this.d.cold; }
  get network() { return this.d.network; }
  async hot(): Promise<string> { return (await hotVault(this.d.db, this.d.key, this.d.network, this.d.now())).publicKey; }
  protected async signer(): Promise<Keypair> { return (await hotVault(this.d.db, this.d.key, this.d.network, this.d.now())).signer(); }
  async caps(): Promise<MainnetCaps> { return effectiveCaps(this.d.db, this.d.caps); }
  /** On-chain SOL behind the books: the hot vault plus the cold address. */
  async reserves(): Promise<number> {
    const hot = await this.d.rpc.balance(await this.hot());
    return hot + (this.d.cold ? await this.d.rpc.balance(this.d.cold) : 0);
  }

  /** How much more may come in for this agent and owner, and which cap binds. Pending requests count, so parallel ones can't overshoot. */
  async room(agentId: string, userId: string, exceptId?: string): Promise<{ lamports: number; why: string }> {
    const { db, now } = this.d, caps = await this.caps(), t = now(), season = await currentSeason(db);
    const day = new Date(`${t.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00Z`), month = new Date(t.getTime() - 30 * DAY);
    const live = and(eq(ledgerDeposits.season, season), inArray(ledgerDeposits.status, ['pending', 'credited']), exceptId ? sql`${ledgerDeposits.id} <> ${exceptId}` : undefined);
    const sum = async (...w: (SQL | undefined)[]) => (await db.select({ n: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${ledgerDeposits.lamports}), 0)::float8` }).from(ledgerDeposits).where(and(live, ...w)))[0]!.n;
    const [agentAll, agentDay, userMonth, pending] = await Promise.all([
      sum(eq(ledgerDeposits.agentId, agentId)), sum(eq(ledgerDeposits.agentId, agentId), gte(ledgerDeposits.createdAt, day)),
      sum(eq(ledgerDeposits.userId, userId), gte(ledgerDeposits.createdAt, month)), sum(eq(ledgerDeposits.status, 'pending')),
    ]);
    const inside = (await books(db)).ledgerTotal;
    const options: [number, string][] = [
      [caps.agentLifetime - agentAll, 'This agent’s lifetime cap'], [caps.agentDaily - agentDay, 'This agent’s daily cap'],
      [caps.userMonthly - userMonth, 'Your 30-day cap'], [caps.world - inside - pending, 'The world’s cap'],
    ];
    const [lamports, why] = options.reduce((a, b) => (b[0] < a[0] ? b : a));
    return { lamports: Math.max(0, lamports), why };
  }

  /** A Solana Pay request to the hot vault with a fresh reference, valid for 30 minutes (spec §4). */
  async createDeposit(i: { agentId: string; userId: string; lamports: number }): Promise<DepositView> {
    const { db, now } = this.d;
    if (this.d.chain.frozen) throw new CustodyError('FROZEN', 'Deposits are paused while the books are checked');
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(i.lamports) || i.lamports < MIN_DEPOSIT) throw new CustodyError('BAD_AMOUNT', 'Deposits are at least 0.01 SOL');
    await assertCanDeposit(db, i.userId, now());
    const sender = await this.d.payoutOf(i.userId);
    if (!sender) throw new CustodyError('NO_PAYOUT_WALLET', 'Choose a payout wallet first: deposits come from it and withdrawals go back to it');
    const room = await this.room(i.agentId, i.userId);
    if (i.lamports > room.lamports) throw new CustodyError('CAP', `${room.why}: you can add at most ${room.lamports / SOL} SOL now`);
    const t = now();
    const row: DepositRow = {
      id: newId(), season: await currentSeason(db), agentId: i.agentId, userId: i.userId, sender, lamports: i.lamports,
      reference: Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(), status: 'pending', signature: null, createdAt: t, expiresAt: new Date(t.getTime() + INTENT_MS), settledAt: null,
    };
    await db.insert(ledgerDeposits).values(row);
    return toDepositView(row, await this.hot());
  }

  /**
   * Every transfer to the hot vault not handled yet, oldest first: credit it if it matches its request, otherwise refund it.
   * Transfers from the cold address are left for an admin to record as house stake (Task 12). Each signature is handled once:
   * its ledger_chain_seen row is written in the same transaction as the credit, or claimed before a refund is sent.
   */
  async pollDeposits(): Promise<{ credited: number; refunded: number; ignored: number; expired: number }> {
    const { db, rpc, now } = this.d;
    const out = { credited: 0, refunded: 0, ignored: 0, expired: 0 };
    await assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, this.d.network);
    const hot = await this.hot();
    const sigs = await rpc.signaturesFor(hot);
    const seen = new Set(sigs.length ? (await db.select({ s: ledgerChainSeen.signature }).from(ledgerChainSeen).where(inArray(ledgerChainSeen.signature, sigs))).map((r) => r.s) : []);
    for (const sig of sigs.filter((s) => !seen.has(s)).reverse()) {
      const t = await rpc.transferIn(sig, hot);
      if (!t || t.lamports <= 0) { await this.mark(sig, 'ignored', null, null); out.ignored++; continue; }
      if (this.d.cold && t.from === this.d.cold) { await this.mark(sig, 'from_cold', t, null); continue; }
      const [intent] = t.references.length ? await db.select().from(ledgerDeposits).where(inArray(ledgerDeposits.reference, t.references)).limit(1) : [];
      const reason = await this.refusal(intent ?? null, t);
      if (!reason) { if (await this.creditDeposit(sig, intent!, t)) out.credited++; continue; }
      if (await this.refund(sig, t, intent ?? null, reason)) out.refunded++;
    }
    out.expired = (await db.update(ledgerDeposits).set({ status: 'expired' })
      .where(and(eq(ledgerDeposits.status, 'pending'), lt(ledgerDeposits.expiresAt, now()))).returning({ id: ledgerDeposits.id })).length;
    return out;
  }

  private async mark(sig: string, outcome: 'ignored' | 'from_cold', t: Transfer | null, reason: string | null) {
    await this.d.db.insert(ledgerChainSeen).values({ signature: sig, outcome, depositId: null, fromAddress: t?.from ?? null, lamports: t?.lamports ?? 0, refundSignature: null, reason, createdAt: this.d.now() }).onConflictDoNothing();
  }

  private async refusal(intent: DepositRow | null, t: Transfer): Promise<string | null> {
    if (!intent) return 'no matching deposit request';
    if (intent.status !== 'pending') return `the request was already ${intent.status}`;
    if (this.d.now() > intent.expiresAt) return 'the request had expired';
    if (t.from !== intent.sender) return 'paid from a wallet other than the payout wallet';
    if (t.lamports !== intent.lamports) return 'the amount differs from the request';
    if (this.d.chain.frozen) return 'deposits are paused while the books are checked';
    try { await assertCanDeposit(this.d.db, intent.userId, this.d.now()); } catch (e) { return (e as Error).message; }
    const room = await this.room(intent.agentId, intent.userId, intent.id);
    if (t.lamports > room.lamports) return `${room.why} would be exceeded`;
    return null;
  }

  private async creditDeposit(sig: string, intent: DepositRow, t: Transfer): Promise<boolean> {
    const { db, now } = this.d, at = now();
    const pk = (await walletOf(db, intent.agentId))!.publicKey;
    try {
      await db.transaction(async (x) => {
        const tx = x as unknown as Db;
        await tx.insert(ledgerChainSeen).values({ signature: sig, outcome: 'credited', depositId: intent.id, fromAddress: t.from, lamports: t.lamports, refundSignature: null, reason: null, createdAt: at });
        const done = await tx.update(ledgerDeposits).set({ status: 'credited', signature: sig, settledAt: at })
          .where(and(eq(ledgerDeposits.id, intent.id), eq(ledgerDeposits.status, 'pending'))).returning({ id: ledgerDeposits.id });
        if (!done.length) throw new Skip();
        await move(tx, { from: EXTERNAL, to: pk, lamports: t.lamports, kind: 'deposit', ref: sig, season: await currentSeason(tx), at });
        await recordTx(tx, { signature: sig, agentId: intent.agentId, turnId: null, kind: 'deposit', counterparty: t.from, lamportsDelta: t.lamports, createdAt: at });
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof Skip || isUniqueViolation(e)) return false;
      throw e;
    }
    await this.d.notify?.(intent.userId, intent.agentId, `got your ${t.lamports / SOL} SOL deposit`);
    return true;
  }

  /** Claims the signature first, so two polls can never both refund it; a failed send is left for an admin (it may have landed). */
  private async refund(sig: string, t: Transfer, intent: DepositRow | null, reason: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const { db, rpc, now } = this.d;
    const claimed = await db.insert(ledgerChainSeen).values({ signature: sig, outcome: 'refunding', depositId: intent?.id ?? null, fromAddress: t.from, lamports: t.lamports, refundSignature: null, reason, createdAt: now() })
      .onConflictDoNothing().returning({ s: ledgerChainSeen.signature });
    if (!claimed.length) return false;
    const back = t.lamports - REFUND_FEE_LAMPORTS;
    if (back <= 0) { await db.update(ledgerChainSeen).set({ outcome: 'ignored' }).where(eq(ledgerChainSeen.signature, sig)); return false; }
    try {
      const r = await rpc.send(await this.signer(), t.from, back);
      await db.update(ledgerChainSeen).set({ outcome: 'refunded', refundSignature: r.signature }).where(eq(ledgerChainSeen.signature, sig));
      if (intent) await db.update(ledgerDeposits).set({ status: 'refunded', signature: sig, settledAt: now() }).where(and(eq(ledgerDeposits.id, intent.id), eq(ledgerDeposits.status, 'pending')));
      return true;
    } catch (e) {
      await db.update(ledgerChainSeen).set({ outcome: 'refund_failed', reason: `${reason}; refund failed: ${(e as Error).message}` }).where(eq(ledgerChainSeen.signature, sig));
      return false;
    }
  }
}
```

Three notes on behaviour:
- A refused late payment marks its expired request `refunded`. The `status = 'pending'` condition still matches it, because the expiry update runs only at the end of the poll.
- Each deposit request has its own random reference, so `inArray(reference, t.references)` finds at most one.
- `recordTx` takes a `Db`, and the cast transaction works the same way as in `agents/migrate.ts`.

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { custodyCtx } from './ledger-helpers.ts';
import { chainTxs, ledgerWithdrawals } from '../src/db/schema.ts';
import { books } from '../src/ledger/books.ts';
import { HOUSE, WITHDRAWALS, lamportsOf } from '../src/ledger/accounts.ts';

const SOL = 1e9;
let c: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof custodyCtx>>;
afterEach(async () => { await c.t.close(); });

/** The owner's agent holds `sol` from a real deposit; the house holds a little stake to pay network fees. */
async function funded(sol = 0.1, env: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  c = await custodyCtx({}, env);
  const d = await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: sol * SOL });
  c.rpc.pay(c.payer, d.hot, sol * SOL, d.reference);
  await c.custody.pollDeposits();
  c.rpc.pay(c.cold, d.hot, 0.01 * SOL);
  await c.chain.credit(HOUSE, 0.01 * SOL, 'house_stake', 'stake-1');
  return d.hot;
}

describe('withdrawals', () => {
  it('pays the payout wallet at once when the hot vault covers it; the house pays the fee', async () => {
    await funded();
    const w = await c.custody.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 'all' });
    expect(w).toMatchObject({ status: 'queued', lamports: 0.1 * SOL, to: c.payer, position: 1 });
    expect(await c.custody.processWithdrawals()).toMatchObject({ sent: 1, held: 0, failed: 0 });
    expect(c.rpc.sent).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ to: c.payer, lamports: 0.1 * SOL })]);
    expect((await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).lamports).toBe(0);
    expect(await lamportsOf(c.t.db, WITHDRAWALS)).toBe(0);
    expect(await lamportsOf(c.t.db, HOUSE)).toBe(0.01 * SOL - 5000);
    expect((await c.t.db.select().from(chainTxs).where(eq(chainTxs.kind, 'withdraw')))[0]).toMatchObject({ lamportsDelta: -0.1 * SOL });
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(await c.custody.reserves()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual((await books(c.t.db)).ledgerTotal);
  });

  it('sends only to the payout wallet in force when it was requested', async () => {
    await funded();
    const next = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58();
    c.payouts.set(c.owner.id, next);
    const w = await c.custody.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.05 * SOL });
    expect(w.to).toBe(next);
    await expect(c.custody.enqueue({ agentId: null, userId: null, to: next, lamports: 1000, approvedBy: 'admin' })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'COLD_ONLY' });
  });

  it('holds past the daily outflow limit until an admin approves', async () => {
    await funded(0.1, { OUTFLOW_DAILY_MAX_SOL: '0.05' });
    const w = await c.custody.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.08 * SOL });
    expect(await c.custody.processWithdrawals()).toMatchObject({ sent: 0, held: 1 });
    expect((await c.custody.withdrawals({ id: w.id }))[0]!.reason).toMatch(/outflow limit/);
    await c.custody.approve(w.id, 'admin-1');
    expect(await c.custody.processWithdrawals()).toMatchObject({ sent: 1 });
  });

  it('holds while the books are frozen and while the hot vault is low', async () => {
    const hot = await funded();
    await c.chain.freeze('mismatch');
    const w = await c.custody.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.05 * SOL });
    await c.custody.processWithdrawals();
    expect((await c.custody.withdrawals({ id: w.id }))[0]!.reason).toMatch(/review/);
    await c.chain.unfreeze();
    c.rpc.balances.set(hot, 1000);
    await c.custody.processWithdrawals();
    expect((await c.custody.withdrawals({ id: w.id }))[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'queued', reason: expect.stringMatching(/hot vault/) });
  });

  it('never re-sends a withdrawal whose send threw (review focus 2)', async () => {
    await funded();
    const w = await c.custody.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.05 * SOL });
    c.rpc.failSend = new Error('timeout');
    expect(await c.custody.processWithdrawals()).toMatchObject({ failed: 1 });
    c.rpc.failSend = null;
    expect(await c.custody.processWithdrawals()).toMatchObject({ sent: 0, failed: 0 });
    expect(c.rpc.sent).toEqual([]);
    const [row] = await c.custody.withdrawals({ id: w.id });
    expect(row).toMatchObject({ status: 'sending', reason: expect.stringMatching(/check the chain/) });
    await c.custody.resolveStuck(w.id, 'return');
    expect((await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).lamports).toBe(0.1 * SOL);
    expect((await c.custody.withdrawals({ id: w.id }))[0]!.status).toBe('failed');
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it('finishes a stuck withdrawal that did land, after checking the transaction', async () => {
    const hot = await funded();
    const w = await c.custody.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.05 * SOL });
    await c.t.db.update(ledgerWithdrawals).set({ status: 'sending' }).where(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, w.id));
    const wrong = c.rpc.pay(hot, c.payer, 0.04 * SOL);
    await expect(c.custody.resolveStuck(w.id, 'sent', wrong)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOT_THIS_TRANSFER' });
    const right = c.rpc.pay(hot, c.payer, 0.05 * SOL);
    await c.custody.resolveStuck(w.id, 'sent', right);
    expect((await c.custody.withdrawals({ id: w.id }))[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'sent', signature: right });
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it('a withdrawal racing a buy never overdraws (review focus 3)', async () => {

    await c.fund(1, 0.1);

    const results = await Promise.allSettled([
      c.chain.buy(await c.signer(0), mint, 0.04 * SOL, 0),
      c.custody.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.04 * SOL }),
    ]);
    expect(results.filter((r) => r.status === 'fulfilled').length).toBe(1);
    expect((await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).lamports).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
  });

  it('cashes out: sells what vesting allows, then withdraws everything free', async () => {
    await funded(0.1);
    await c.fund(1, 0.1);
    const { mint } = await c.chain.launch(await c.signer(1), { name: 'X', symbol: 'X', uri: 'u', initialBuyLamports: 0 });
    await c.chain.buy(await c.signer(0), mint, 0.03 * SOL, 0);
    const r = await c.custody.cashOut({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id });
    expect(r.sold).toEqual([mint]);
    expect((await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).tokens).toEqual([]);
    expect(r.withdrawal).toMatchObject({ to: c.payer, status: 'queued' });
    expect((await c.chain.balances(await c.pk(0))).lamports).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the withdrawal methods**

In `apps/server/src/ledger/custody.ts`, extend the imports:

```ts
import { asc, desc } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { WithdrawalView } from '@shillex/shared';
import { ledgerPools, ledgerWithdrawals } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { ChainError } from '../world/chain.ts';
import { refreshCoinPrice } from '../world/market.ts';
import { signerFor } from '../world/wallets.ts';
import { HOUSE, WITHDRAWALS, lamportsOf, need } from './accounts.ts';
import { isAddress } from './rpc.ts';
```

Merge these into the existing import lines rather than repeating a module. Then add, above the class:

```ts
export const MIN_WITHDRAW = 1_000_000;
/** Kept back for the network fee of one send. */
export const FEE_MARGIN = 10_000;
type WithdrawalRow = typeof ledgerWithdrawals.$inferSelect;
const utcDay = (t: Date) => new Date(`${t.toISOString().slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00Z`);
```

Add these methods inside the class:

```ts
  /** An owner's withdrawal: always to the payout wallet in force at request time (spec §4, §7). */
  async requestWithdrawal(i: { agentId: string; userId: string; lamports: number | 'all' }): Promise<WithdrawalView> {
    const to = await this.d.payoutOf(i.userId);
    if (!to) throw new CustodyError('NO_PAYOUT_WALLET', 'Choose a payout wallet first');
    return this.enqueue({ agentId: i.agentId, userId: i.userId, to, lamports: i.lamports, approvedBy: null });
  }

  /**
   * Moves the amount from the agent's account (or the house's) into the pending account and queues the send. Game state never
   * blocks this (spec §4): only processWithdrawals decides when it leaves. The house's SOL leaves only for the cold address.
   */
  async enqueue(i: { agentId: string | null; userId: string | null; to: string; lamports: number | 'all'; approvedBy: string | null }): Promise<WithdrawalView> {
    const { db, now } = this.d;
    if (!isAddress(i.to)) throw new CustodyError('BAD_ADDRESS', 'That is not a Solana address');
    if (i.agentId === null && i.to !== this.d.cold) throw new CustodyError('COLD_ONLY', 'The house’s SOL leaves only for the cold address');
    const from = i.agentId ? (await walletOf(db, i.agentId))!.publicKey : HOUSE;
    const id = newId(), at = now();
    const row = await db.transaction(async (x) => {
      const tx = x as unknown as Db, season = await currentSeason(tx);
      const have = await lamportsOf(tx, from);
      const amount = i.lamports === 'all' ? have : i.lamports;
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(amount) || amount < MIN_WITHDRAW) throw new CustodyError('BAD_AMOUNT', 'Withdrawals are at least 0.001 SOL');
      await need(tx, from, amount);
      await move(tx, { from, to: WITHDRAWALS, lamports: amount, kind: 'withdraw', ref: id, season, at });
      const r: WithdrawalRow = { id, season, agentId: i.agentId, userId: i.userId, toAddress: i.to, lamports: amount, status: 'queued', reason: null, approvedBy: i.approvedBy, signature: null, requestedAt: at, sentAt: null };
      await tx.insert(ledgerWithdrawals).values(r);
      if (i.agentId) await recordTx(tx, { signature: `ldg_${id}`, agentId: i.agentId, turnId: null, kind: 'withdraw', counterparty: i.to, lamportsDelta: -amount, createdAt: at });
      return r;
    });
    return (await this.withdrawals({ id: row.id }))[0]!;
  }

  /** Why a queued withdrawal must wait, or null when it can go now. */
  private async holdReason(w: WithdrawalRow, caps: MainnetCaps, hot: string): Promise<string | null> {
    const { db, rpc, now } = this.d;
    if (this.d.chain.frozen && !w.approvedBy) return 'review: the books did not reconcile, an admin checks before anything leaves';
    if (!w.approvedBy) {
      const [s] = await db.select({ n: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${ledgerWithdrawals.lamports}), 0)::float8` }).from(ledgerWithdrawals)
        .where(and(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.status, 'sent'), gte(ledgerWithdrawals.sentAt, utcDay(now()))));
      if ((s?.n ?? 0) + w.lamports > caps.outflowDaily) return 'the daily outflow limit is reached: it waits for the owner’s approval';
    }
    if ((await rpc.balance(hot)) < w.lamports + FEE_MARGIN) return 'the hot vault is low: the owner tops it up and it is paid within 24 hours';
    if ((await lamportsOf(db, HOUSE)) < FEE_MARGIN) return 'the house cannot pay the network fee yet';
    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Sends what can go, oldest first. A row is claimed (queued → sending) before its send; a send that throws stays `sending`
   * with a note and is never retried here: it may have landed, so an admin checks the chain and resolves it (Task 12).
   */
  async processWithdrawals(): Promise<{ sent: number; held: number; failed: number }> {
    const { db, rpc } = this.d, out = { sent: 0, held: 0, failed: 0 };
    const queued = await db.select().from(ledgerWithdrawals).where(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.status, 'queued')).orderBy(asc(ledgerWithdrawals.requestedAt));
    if (!queued.length) return out;
    const caps = await this.caps(), hot = await this.hot();
    for (const w of queued) {
      const hold = await this.holdReason(w, caps, hot);
      if (hold) {
        if (w.reason !== hold) await db.update(ledgerWithdrawals).set({ reason: hold }).where(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, w.id));
        out.held++; continue;
      }
      const claimed = await db.update(ledgerWithdrawals).set({ status: 'sending', reason: null })
        .where(and(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, w.id), eq(ledgerWithdrawals.status, 'queued'))).returning({ id: ledgerWithdrawals.id });
      if (!claimed.length) continue;
      try {
        await assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, this.d.network);
        const s = await rpc.send(await this.signer(), w.toAddress, w.lamports);
        await this.settleSent(w, s.signature, s.feeLamports);
        out.sent++;
      } catch (e) {
        await db.update(ledgerWithdrawals).set({ reason: `send failed (${(e as Error).message}); it may still have landed: check the chain before resolving` }).where(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, w.id));
        out.failed++;
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  private async settleSent(w: WithdrawalRow, signature: string, feeLamports: number) {
    const { db, now } = this.d, at = now();
    await db.transaction(async (x) => {
      const tx = x as unknown as Db, season = await currentSeason(tx);
      await tx.update(ledgerWithdrawals).set({ status: 'sent', signature, sentAt: at, reason: null }).where(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, w.id));
      await move(tx, { from: WITHDRAWALS, to: EXTERNAL, lamports: w.lamports, kind: 'withdraw_sent', ref: signature, season, at });
      await move(tx, { from: HOUSE, to: EXTERNAL, lamports: Math.min(feeLamports, await lamportsOf(tx, HOUSE)), kind: 'network_fee', ref: signature, season, at });
    });
    if (w.userId && w.agentId) await this.d.notify?.(w.userId, w.agentId, `sent ${w.lamports / SOL} SOL to your wallet`);
  }

  /** An admin lets a held withdrawal past the outflow limit or the review hold. */
  async approve(id: string, adminId: string): Promise<void> {
    const r = await this.d.db.update(ledgerWithdrawals).set({ approvedBy: adminId }).where(and(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, id), eq(ledgerWithdrawals.status, 'queued'))).returning({ id: ledgerWithdrawals.id });
    if (!r.length) throw new CustodyError('NOT_QUEUED', 'Only a queued withdrawal can be approved');
  }

  /**
   * A `sending` withdrawal after an admin checked the chain. 'sent': the given transaction must be a transfer of exactly this
   * amount from the hot vault to this address. 'return': nothing left, so the SOL goes back to the agent (or the house).
   */
  async resolveStuck(id: string, outcome: 'sent' | 'return', signature?: string): Promise<void> {
    const { db, rpc, now } = this.d;
    const [w] = await db.select().from(ledgerWithdrawals).where(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, id));
    if (!w || w.status !== 'sending') throw new CustodyError('NOT_STUCK', 'Only a withdrawal stuck in sending can be resolved');
    if (outcome === 'sent') {
      const t = signature ? await rpc.transferIn(signature, w.toAddress) : null;
      if (!t || t.from !== (await this.hot()) || t.lamports !== w.lamports) throw new CustodyError('NOT_THIS_TRANSFER', 'That transaction is not this withdrawal leaving the hot vault');
      await this.settleSent(w, signature!, t.feeLamports);
      return;
    }
    const at = now(), back = w.agentId ? (await walletOf(db, w.agentId))!.publicKey : HOUSE;
    await db.transaction(async (x) => {
      const tx = x as unknown as Db;
      await tx.update(ledgerWithdrawals).set({ status: 'failed', reason: 'returned after a failed send' }).where(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, w.id));
      await move(tx, { from: WITHDRAWALS, to: back, lamports: w.lamports, kind: 'withdraw_return', ref: `return_${w.id}`, season: await currentSeason(tx), at });
      if (w.agentId) await recordTx(tx, { signature: `ldg_return_${w.id}`, agentId: w.agentId, turnId: null, kind: 'withdraw', counterparty: w.toAddress, lamportsDelta: w.lamports, createdAt: at });
    });
  }

  /** Sells what the rules allow (vesting, the 30% creator cap; paused or graduated coins stay held), then withdraws all free SOL. */
  async cashOut(i: { agentId: string; userId: string }): Promise<{ sold: string[]; withdrawal: WithdrawalView | null }> {
    const { db, chain, now } = this.d;
    const signer = await signerFor(db, i.agentId, this.d.worldKey), pk = signer.publicKey.toBase58();
    const sold: string[] = [];
    for (const t of (await chain.balances(pk)).tokens) {
      const [p] = await db.select({ creator: ledgerPools.creatorAccount }).from(ledgerPools).where(eq(ledgerPools.mint, t.mint));
      const free = t.raw - (t.lockedRaw ?? 0);
      const raw = p?.creator === pk ? Math.min(free, Math.floor(t.raw * chain.rules.maxCreatorSaleShare)) : free;
      if (raw <= 0) continue;
      try {
        const r = await chain.sell(signer, t.mint, raw, 10_000);
        await recordTx(db, { signature: r.signature, agentId: i.agentId, turnId: null, kind: 'sell', mint: t.mint, lamportsDelta: r.lamportsDelta, tokenDelta: r.tokenDelta, createdAt: now() });
        await refreshCoinPrice(db, chain, t.mint, now());
        sold.push(t.mint);
      } catch (e) { if (!(e instanceof ChainError)) throw e; }
    }
    const free = (await chain.balances(pk)).lamports;
    return { sold, withdrawal: free >= MIN_WITHDRAW ? await this.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: i.agentId, userId: i.userId, lamports: 'all' }) : null };
  }

  async withdrawals(f: { id?: string; agentId?: string; open?: boolean }): Promise<WithdrawalView[]> {
    const { db } = this.d;
    const rows = await db.select().from(ledgerWithdrawals)
      .where(and(f.id ? eq(ledgerWithdrawals.id, f.id) : undefined, f.agentId ? eq(ledgerWithdrawals.agentId, f.agentId) : undefined, f.open ? inArray(ledgerWithdrawals.status, ['queued', 'sending']) : undefined))
      .orderBy(desc(ledgerWithdrawals.requestedAt)).limit(100);
    const queue = (await db.select({ id: ledgerWithdrawals.id }).from(ledgerWithdrawals).where(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.status, 'queued')).orderBy(asc(ledgerWithdrawals.requestedAt))).map((r) => r.id);
    return rows.map((r) => ({
      id: r.id, agentId: r.agentId, lamports: r.lamports, sol: r.lamports / SOL, to: r.toAddress, status: r.status, reason: r.reason,
      position: r.status === 'queued' ? queue.indexOf(r.id) + 1 : null, requestedAt: r.requestedAt.toISOString(), sentAt: r.sentAt?.toISOString() ?? null, signature: r.signature,
    }));
  }
```

In the "finishes a stuck withdrawal" test, `FakeRpc.pay(hot, payer, …)` stands in for the send that landed. It records a transfer from the hot vault to the payer, which `transferIn(signature, payer)` returns.

```ts
async function deposited(sol = 0.1) {

  const d = await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: sol * SOL });
  c.rpc.pay(c.payer, d.hot, sol * SOL, d.reference);

  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write reconcile**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/reconcile.ts`:

```ts
import { desc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { MainnetMode, ReservesView } from '@shillex/shared';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { ledgerReconciliations } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { newId } from '../lib/ids.ts';
import { books } from './books.ts';
import type { Custody } from './custody.ts';
import type { LedgerChain } from './ledger-chain.ts';
import type { MainnetHandles } from './routes.ts';

/** Unreadable checks in a row (30 minutes at the 10-minute cadence) before the books freeze anyway. */
export const UNREACHABLE_LIMIT = 3;
export type ReconciliationRow = typeof ledgerReconciliations.$inferSelect;

/**
 * Spec §5, every 10 minutes: the journal must match every balance, and the hot vault plus the cold address must cover the books.
 * A mismatch freezes trading and deposits (the engine runs talk-only, withdrawals wait for review). Only an admin unfreezes.
 */
export async function reconcile(d: { db: Db; chain: LedgerChain; custody: Custody | null; mode: MainnetMode; now: () => Date }): Promise<ReconciliationRow> {
  const b = await books(d.db), at = d.now();
  let onchain: number | null = null, error: string | null = null;
  if (d.custody) { try { onchain = await d.custody.reserves(); } catch (e) { error = (e as Error).message; } }
  const shortBy = onchain !== null && onchain < b.ledgerTotal ? b.ledgerTotal - onchain : 0;
  const ok = error ? null : b.ok && shortBy === 0;
  const row: ReconciliationRow = { id: newId(), season: b.season, mode: d.mode, at, ok, ledgerTotal: b.ledgerTotal, onchainTotal: onchain, details: { mismatches: b.mismatches, shortBy, error } };
  await d.db.insert(ledgerReconciliations).values(row);
  if (ok === false && !d.chain.frozen) {
    await d.chain.freeze(`The books did not reconcile at ${at.toISOString()}: ${shortBy ? `reserves short by ${shortBy} lamports` : b.mismatches.slice(0, 3).join('; ')}`);
  }
  if (ok === null && !d.chain.frozen) {
    const last = await d.db.select({ ok: ledgerReconciliations.ok }).from(ledgerReconciliations).where(eq(ledgerReconciliations.mode, d.mode)).orderBy(desc(ledgerReconciliations.at)).limit(UNREACHABLE_LIMIT);
    if (last.length === UNREACHABLE_LIMIT && last.every((r) => r.ok === null)) await d.chain.freeze(`Custody's RPC was unreachable for ${UNREACHABLE_LIMIT} checks in a row: ${error}`);
  }
  return row;
}

/** The public proof-of-reserves page (spec §5): vault addresses, the books' total and when a check last passed. */
export async function reservesView(db: Db, h: MainnetHandles): Promise<ReservesView> {
  const [last] = await db.select().from(ledgerReconciliations).orderBy(desc(ledgerReconciliations.at)).limit(1);
  const [pass] = await db.select({ at: ledgerReconciliations.at }).from(ledgerReconciliations).where(eq(ledgerReconciliations.ok, true)).orderBy(desc(ledgerReconciliations.at)).limit(1);
  return {
    mode: h.mode, network: h.custody?.network ?? null, hot: h.custody ? await h.custody.hot() : null, cold: h.custody?.cold ?? null,
    ledgerTotalSol: (await books(db)).ledgerTotal / 1e9, onchainTotalSol: last?.onchainTotal == null ? null : last.onchainTotal / 1e9,
    lastCheckAt: last?.at.toISOString() ?? null, lastPassAt: pass?.at.toISOString() ?? null, frozen: h.chain.frozen !== null,
  };
}
```

In `apps/server/src/ledger/routes.ts`, inside the registered plugin, add the route and import `reservesView`. Then remove the `void h;` line, because `h` is now used:

```ts

```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { hash } from '@node-rs/argon2';
import { custodyCtx } from './ledger-helpers.ts';
import { createSession, SESSION_COOKIE } from '../src/lib/auth.ts';
import { adminAudit, users } from '../src/db/schema.ts';
import { registerMainnetAdminRoutes } from '../src/ledger/admin.ts';
import { registerMainnetRoutes } from '../src/ledger/routes.ts';
import { verifyAudit } from '../src/ledger/audit.ts';
import { LEGAL_CONFIRMATION, LEGAL_KEY, legalReviewValid } from '../src/ledger/gate.ts';
import { HOUSE, lamportsOf } from '../src/ledger/accounts.ts';
import { setSetting } from '../src/world/launchpad.ts';
import { newId } from '../src/lib/ids.ts';

const SOL = 1e9;
let c: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof custodyCtx>>;
afterEach(async () => { await c.t.close(); });

async function adminCtx() {
  c = await custodyCtx();
  const id = newId();
  await c.t.db.insert(users).values({ id, username: 'admin', displayName: 'admin', passwordHash: await hash('pw'), createdAt: new Date('2026-01-01') });
  const admin = { id, username: 'admin', cookie: `${SESSION_COOKIE}=${await createSession(c.t.db, id, c.t.clock.now())}` };
  const h = { mode: 'rehearsal' as const, chain: c.chain, custody: c.custody, stop: async () => {} };
  await registerMainnetRoutes(c.t.app, h);
  await registerMainnetAdminRoutes(c.t.app, h);
  return { admin, as: c.t.as(admin) };
}

describe('admin money actions', () => {
  it('are admins only, ask for the password again, and land in the audit trail', async () => {
    const { as } = await adminCtx();
    const stranger = await c.t.createUser('stranger');
    expect((await c.t.as(stranger).post('/api/admin/mainnet/unfreeze', { password: 'pw' })).status).toBe(403);
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/caps', { password: 'nope', caps: { worldCapSol: 10 } })).body.code).toBe('BAD_PASSWORD');
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/caps', { password: 'pw', caps: { worldCapSol: 10 } })).status).toBe(204);
    expect((await as.get('/api/admin/mainnet/status')).body.caps.worldCapSol).toBe(10);
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/caps', { password: 'pw', caps: { worldCapSol: 30 } })).body.code).toBe('CAPS_ONLY_LOWER');
    const audit = await c.t.db.select().from(adminAudit);
    expect(audit.map((a) => a.action)).toEqual(['caps']);
    expect(await verifyAudit(c.t.db)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('records a transfer from the cold address as house stake, once', async () => {
    const { as } = await adminCtx();
    const sig = c.rpc.pay(c.cold, await c.custody.hot(), 2 * SOL);
    await c.custody.pollDeposits();
    expect((await as.get('/api/admin/mainnet/status')).body.fromCold).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ signature: sig, sol: 2 })]);
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/stake', { password: 'pw', signature: sig })).status).toBe(204);
    expect(await lamportsOf(c.t.db, HOUSE)).toBe(2 * SOL);
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/stake', { password: 'pw', signature: sig })).status).toBe(409);
  });

  it('sends the house’s SOL only to the cold address, approved', async () => {
    const { as } = await adminCtx();
    const sig = c.rpc.pay(c.cold, await c.custody.hot(), 1 * SOL);
    await c.custody.pollDeposits();
    await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/stake', { password: 'pw', signature: sig });
    const r = await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/house-withdraw', { password: 'pw', sol: 0.5 });
    expect(r.status).toBe(200);
    expect(r.body).toMatchObject({ to: c.cold, sol: 0.5 });
    await c.custody.processWithdrawals();
    expect(c.rpc.sent).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ to: c.cold, lamports: 0.5 * SOL })]);
  });

  it('publishes the notice and records the legal review only through the typed form', async () => {
    const { as } = await adminCtx();
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/notice', { password: 'pw', legalPosition: 'Counsel: allowed for adults in X.' })).body).toMatchObject({ version: 'n2' });
    const review = { password: 'pw', reviewer: 'A. Lawyer', date: '2026-11-01', note: 'Reviewed the design.', confirm: 'yes' };
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/legal-review', review)).body.code).toBe('CONFIRMATION_MISMATCH');
    expect(await legalReviewValid(c.t.db)).toBe(false);
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/legal-review', { ...review, confirm: LEGAL_CONFIRMATION })).status).toBe(204);
    expect(await legalReviewValid(c.t.db)).toBe(true);
    expect((await as.get('/api/admin/mainnet/status')).body.legalReview).toMatchObject({ reviewer: 'A. Lawyer', confirmedBy: 'admin' });
  });

  it('ignores a legal review written straight into the settings', async () => {
    await adminCtx();
    await setSetting(c.t.db, LEGAL_KEY, JSON.stringify({ reviewer: 'x', date: '2026-11-01', note: 'n', confirmedBy: 'admin', auditId: 'fake' }));
    expect(await legalReviewValid(c.t.db)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('unfreezes only after a passing check, and resolves stuck sends', async () => {
    const { as } = await adminCtx();
    await c.chain.freeze('test');
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/unfreeze', { password: 'pw' })).status).toBe(204);
    expect(c.chain.frozen).toBeNull();
    c.rpc.balances.set(await c.custody.hot(), 0);
    await c.chain.credit(HOUSE, 1 * SOL, 'house_stake', 'paper-stake'); // books now exceed the reserves
    await c.chain.freeze('test');
    expect((await as.post('/api/admin/mainnet/unfreeze', { password: 'pw' })).body.code).toBe('STILL_FAILING');
    expect(c.chain.frozen).not.toBeNull();
    const [row] = await c.t.db.select().from(users).where(eq(users.username, 'admin'));
    expect(row).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the audit trail**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/audit.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { asc, desc } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { adminAudit } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { newId } from '../lib/ids.ts';

const sha = (s: string) => createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
const link = (prev: string | null, id: string, adminId: string, action: string, payload: string, at: Date) => sha(JSON.stringify([prev, id, adminId, action, payload, at.toISOString()]));

/** Appends one admin money action (spec §7). The table refuses UPDATE and DELETE; each row hashes the one before it. */
export async function audit(db: Db, adminId: string, action: string, payload: Record<string, unknown>, now: Date): Promise<string> {
  const [prev] = await db.select({ hash: adminAudit.hash }).from(adminAudit).orderBy(desc(adminAudit.createdAt), desc(adminAudit.id)).limit(1);
  const id = newId(), text = JSON.stringify(payload);
  await db.insert(adminAudit).values({ id, adminId, action, payload: text, prevHash: prev?.hash ?? null, hash: link(prev?.hash ?? null, id, adminId, action, text, now), createdAt: now });
  return id;
}

/** True when every row links to the previous one and hashes to what it says. */
export async function verifyAudit(db: Db): Promise<boolean> {
  let prev: string | null = null;
  for (const r of await db.select().from(adminAudit).orderBy(asc(adminAudit.createdAt), asc(adminAudit.id))) {
    if (r.prevHash !== prev || r.hash !== link(prev, r.id, r.adminId, r.action, r.payload, r.createdAt)) return false;
    prev = r.hash;
  }
  return true;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the legal-review part of the gate**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/gate.ts`:

```ts
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { adminAudit } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { getSetting, setSetting } from '../world/launchpad.ts';
import { audit, verifyAudit } from './audit.ts';

export const LEGAL_KEY = 'legal_review';
/** What the owner types on /admin to record the review (spec §9.4). */
export const LEGAL_CONFIRMATION = 'I confirm a lawyer reviewed Shillex mainnet and I accept the risk';
export interface LegalReview { reviewer: string; date: string; note: string; confirmedBy: string }

/** Only the admin form calls this. The gate trusts the setting only when its audit row agrees (legalReviewValid). */
export async function recordLegalReview(db: Db, adminId: string, r: LegalReview, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  const auditId = await audit(db, adminId, 'legal_review', { ...r }, now);
  await setSetting(db, LEGAL_KEY, JSON.stringify({ ...r, auditId }));
}

export async function legalReview(db: Db): Promise<(LegalReview & { auditId: string }) | null> {
  const raw = await getSetting(db, LEGAL_KEY);
  return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
}

/** The review counts only with its audit row: same reviewer, date, note and admin, and an intact audit chain. */
export async function legalReviewValid(db: Db): Promise<boolean> {
  const r = await legalReview(db);
  if (!r) return false;
  const [row] = await db.select().from(adminAudit).where(eq(adminAudit.id, r.auditId));
  if (!row || row.action !== 'legal_review') return false;
  const p = JSON.parse(row.payload) as LegalReview;
  return p.reviewer === r.reviewer && p.date === r.date && p.note === r.note && p.confirmedBy === r.confirmedBy && (await verifyAudit(db));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the admin routes**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/admin.ts`:

```ts
import type { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest } from 'fastify';
import { verify } from '@node-rs/argon2';
import { and, desc, eq, inArray } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { MainnetAdminView } from '@shillex/shared';
import { ledgerAccounts, ledgerChainSeen, ledgerReconciliations, users } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { requireUser } from '../lib/auth.ts';
import { errors } from '../lib/errors.ts';
import { setSetting } from '../world/launchpad.ts';
import { HOUSE, currentSeason, lamportsOf, move } from './accounts.ts';
import { audit } from './audit.ts';
import { books } from './books.ts';
import { currentNotice, publishNotice } from './consent.ts';
import { CAPS_KEY, type MainnetCaps } from './custody.ts';
import { toHttp } from './errors.ts';
import { LEGAL_CONFIRMATION, legalReview, recordLegalReview } from './gate.ts';
import { reconcile } from './reconcile.ts';
import type { MainnetHandles } from './routes.ts';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';

const SOL = 1e9;
const lamports = (sol: number) => Math.round(sol * SOL);
const CAP_FIELDS = { agentCapSol: 'agentLifetime', agentDailyCapSol: 'agentDaily', userMonthlyCapSol: 'userMonthly', worldCapSol: 'world', hotMaxSol: 'hotMax', outflowDailyMaxSol: 'outflowDaily' } as const;

/** Money actions on /admin (spec §7): admins only, the admin password again on every POST, one audit row each. */
export async function registerMainnetAdminRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, h: MainnetHandles) {
  const { db, config, now } = app.deps;
  const requireAdmin = (req: FastifyRequest) => {
    const u = requireUser(req);
    if (u.kind !== 'human' || !config.adminUsernames.includes(u.username)) throw errors.forbidden('ADMIN_ONLY', 'Admins only');
    return u;
  };
  const confirm = async (req: FastifyRequest) => {
    const u = requireAdmin(req);
    const { password } = z.object({ password: z.string().min(1).max(200) }).parse(req.body);
    const [row] = await db.select({ h: users.passwordHash }).from(users).where(eq(users.id, u.id));
    if (!row?.h || !(await verify(row.h, password))) throw errors.forbidden('BAD_PASSWORD', 'Wrong password');
    return u;
  };
  const custody = () => { if (!h.custody) throw errors.conflict('NO_CUSTODY', 'Custody runs only with MAINNET=rehearsal or on'); return h.custody; };
  const money = { config: { rateLimit: { max: 10, timeWindow: '1 minute' } } };

  await app.register(async (r) => {
    r.get('/admin/mainnet/status', async (req): Promise<MainnetAdminView> => {
      requireAdmin(req);
      const b = await books(db), c = h.custody, caps = c ? await c.caps() : null;
      const bal = async (a: string | null) => (a && c ? await c.d.rpc.balance(a).catch(() => null) : null);
      const hot = c ? await c.hot() : null, hotSol = await bal(hot), coldSol = await bal(c?.cold ?? null);
      const [last] = await db.select().from(ledgerReconciliations).orderBy(desc(ledgerReconciliations.at)).limit(1);
      const seen = await db.select().from(ledgerChainSeen).where(inArray(ledgerChainSeen.outcome, ['from_cold', 'refund_failed'])).orderBy(desc(ledgerChainSeen.createdAt)).limit(50);
      const top10 = (await db.select({ l: ledgerAccounts.lamports }).from(ledgerAccounts).orderBy(desc(ledgerAccounts.lamports)).limit(10)).reduce((n, x) => n + x.l, 0);
      const n = await currentNotice(db), lr = await legalReview(db);
      const { gateStatus } = await import('./gate.ts');
      return {
        mode: h.mode, season: b.season, frozen: h.chain.frozen,
        hot: hot ? { address: hot, sol: hotSol === null ? null : hotSol / SOL } : null,
        cold: c?.cold ? { address: c.cold, sol: coldSol === null ? null : coldSol / SOL } : null,
        books: { ledgerTotalSol: b.ledgerTotal / SOL, houseSol: (await lamportsOf(db, HOUSE)) / SOL, ok: b.ok, mismatches: b.mismatches },
        lastReconciliation: last ? { at: last.at.toISOString(), ok: last.ok, details: last.details } : null,
        withdrawals: c ? await c.withdrawals({ open: true }) : [],
        fromCold: seen.filter((s) => s.outcome === 'from_cold').map((s) => ({ signature: s.signature, sol: s.lamports / SOL, at: s.createdAt.toISOString() })),
        refundFailures: seen.filter((s) => s.outcome === 'refund_failed').map((s) => ({ signature: s.signature, sol: s.lamports / SOL, from: s.fromAddress, reason: s.reason })),
        caps: caps
          ? { agentCapSol: caps.agentLifetime / SOL, agentDailyCapSol: caps.agentDaily / SOL, userMonthlyCapSol: caps.userMonthly / SOL, worldCapSol: caps.world / SOL, hotMaxSol: caps.hotMax / SOL, outflowDailyMaxSol: caps.outflowDaily / SOL }
          : { ...config.mainnetLimits },
        excessSol: caps && hotSol !== null ? Math.max(0, hotSol - caps.hotMax) / SOL : 0,
        hotCoversTop10: hotSol !== null && hotSol >= top10,
        notice: n, legalReview: lr ? { reviewer: lr.reviewer, date: lr.date, note: lr.note, confirmedBy: lr.confirmedBy } : null,
        gate: h.mode === 'off' ? ['MAINNET is off'] : await gateStatus({ db, config, rpc: c?.d.rpc ?? null, custody: c, now }),
      };
    });

    r.post('/admin/mainnet/stake', money, async (req, reply) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      const { signature } = z.object({ signature: z.string().min(20).max(120) }).parse(req.body);
      const at = now();
      await db.transaction(async (x) => {
        const tx = x as unknown as Db;
        const done = await tx.update(ledgerChainSeen).set({ outcome: 'stake' }).where(and(eq(ledgerChainSeen.signature, signature), eq(ledgerChainSeen.outcome, 'from_cold'))).returning();
        if (!done.length) throw errors.conflict('NOT_A_COLD_TRANSFER', 'That signature is not an unrecorded transfer from the cold address');
        await move(tx, { from: 'external', to: HOUSE, lamports: done[0]!.lamports, kind: 'house_stake', ref: signature, season: await currentSeason(tx), at });
      });
      await audit(db, u.id, 'stake', { signature }, at);
      return reply.status(204).send();
    });

    r.post('/admin/mainnet/house-withdraw', money, async (req) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      const { sol } = z.object({ sol: z.number().positive().max(10_000) }).parse(req.body);
      const c = custody();
      if (!c.cold) throw errors.conflict('NO_COLD', 'Set MAINNET_COLD_ADDRESS first');
      try {
        const w = await c.enqueue({ agentId: null, userId: null, to: c.cold, lamports: lamports(sol), approvedBy: u.id });
        await audit(db, u.id, 'house_withdraw', { sol, id: w.id }, now());
        void c.processWithdrawals().catch((e) => console.error('[mainnet] withdrawals', e));
        return w;
      } catch (e) { throw toHttp(e); }
    });

    r.post('/admin/mainnet/to-cold', money, async (req) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      const { sol } = z.object({ sol: z.number().positive().max(10_000) }).parse(req.body);
      const c = custody();
      if (!c.cold) throw errors.conflict('NO_COLD', 'Set MAINNET_COLD_ADDRESS first');
      // Hot to cold moves reserves only: the books are unchanged, except the house paying the network fee.
      const s = await c.moveToCold(lamports(sol));
      await audit(db, u.id, 'to_cold', { sol, signature: s }, now());
      return { signature: s };
    });

    r.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/admin/mainnet/withdrawals/:id/approve', money, async (req, reply) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      try { await custody().approve(req.params.id, u.id); } catch (e) { throw toHttp(e); }
      await audit(db, u.id, 'approve_withdrawal', { id: req.params.id }, now());
      void custody().processWithdrawals().catch((e) => console.error('[mainnet] withdrawals', e));
      return reply.status(204).send();
    });

    r.post<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/admin/mainnet/withdrawals/:id/resolve', money, async (req, reply) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      const b = z.object({ outcome: z.enum(['sent', 'return']), signature: z.string().max(120).optional() }).parse(req.body);
      try { await custody().resolveStuck(req.params.id, b.outcome, b.signature); } catch (e) { throw toHttp(e); }
      await audit(db, u.id, 'resolve_withdrawal', { id: req.params.id, ...b }, now());
      return reply.status(204).send();
    });

    r.post('/admin/mainnet/unfreeze', money, async (req, reply) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      const check = await reconcile({ db, chain: h.chain, custody: h.custody, mode: h.mode, now });
      if (check.ok !== true) throw errors.conflict('STILL_FAILING', 'The books still do not reconcile; fix that first');
      await h.chain.unfreeze();
      await audit(db, u.id, 'unfreeze', { reconciliation: check.id }, now());
      return reply.status(204).send();
    });

    r.post('/admin/mainnet/caps', money, async (req, reply) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      const { caps } = z.object({ caps: z.partialRecord(z.enum(Object.keys(CAP_FIELDS) as [keyof typeof CAP_FIELDS]), z.number().nonnegative()) }).parse(req.body);
      const override: Partial<MainnetCaps> = {};
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(caps) as [keyof typeof CAP_FIELDS, number][]) {
        if (v > config.mainnetLimits[k]) throw errors.badRequest('CAPS_ONLY_LOWER', `${k} can only go below the configured ${config.mainnetLimits[k]} SOL; raising it needs a config change`);
        override[CAP_FIELDS[k]] = lamports(v);
      }
      await setSetting(db, CAPS_KEY, JSON.stringify(override));
      await audit(db, u.id, 'caps', { caps }, now());
      return reply.status(204).send();
    });

    r.post('/admin/mainnet/notice', money, async (req) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      const { legalPosition } = z.object({ legalPosition: z.string().trim().min(10).max(2000) }).parse(req.body);
      const n = await publishNotice(db, legalPosition, now());
      await audit(db, u.id, 'notice', { version: n.version, legalPosition }, now());
      return n;
    });

    r.post('/admin/mainnet/legal-review', money, async (req, reply) => {
      const u = await confirm(req);
      const b = z.object({ reviewer: z.string().trim().min(2).max(120), date: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/), note: z.string().trim().min(5).max(2000), confirm: z.string() }).parse(req.body);
      if (b.confirm !== LEGAL_CONFIRMATION) throw errors.badRequest('CONFIRMATION_MISMATCH', `Type exactly: ${LEGAL_CONFIRMATION}`);
      await recordLegalReview(db, u.id, { reviewer: b.reviewer, date: b.date, note: b.note, confirmedBy: u.username }, now());
      return reply.status(204).send();
    });
  }, { prefix: '/api' });
}
```

Add `moveToCold` to `Custody` in `custody.ts`:

```ts
  /** Sends hot-vault SOL to the cold address (reserves move, the books don't); the house pays the network fee. */
  async moveToCold(lamports: number): Promise<string> {
    const { db, rpc, now } = this.d;
    if (!this.d.cold) throw new CustodyError('NO_COLD', 'Set MAINNET_COLD_ADDRESS first');
    await assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, this.d.network);
    const s = await rpc.send(await this.signer(), this.d.cold, lamports);
    await db.transaction(async (x) => {
      const tx = x as unknown as Db;
      await move(tx, { from: HOUSE, to: EXTERNAL, lamports: Math.min(s.feeLamports, await lamportsOf(tx, HOUSE)), kind: 'network_fee', ref: s.signature, season: await currentSeason(tx), at: now() });
    });
    return s.signature;
  }
```

```ts
describe('rehearsal status', () => {
  it('needs 14 clean days, 90% coverage and a paid withdrawal', async () => {
    const c = await ledgerCtx(); close = () => c.t.close();
    const now = c.t.clock.now();
    expect((await rehearsalStatus(c.t.db, now)).why).toMatch(/no rehearsal/);
    await cleanRehearsal(c.t.db, now, 13);
    expect((await rehearsalStatus(c.t.db, now)).why).toMatch(/13\.\d of 14 days/);
    c.t.clock.advance(1.5 * DAY);
    expect((await rehearsalStatus(c.t.db, c.t.clock.now())).why).toMatch(/checks passed/); // the last 1.5 days have no checks
  });
  it('starts the 14 days again after a failure', async () => {
    const c = await ledgerCtx(); close = () => c.t.close();
    const now = c.t.clock.now();
    await cleanRehearsal(c.t.db, now);
    expect(await rehearsalStatus(c.t.db, now)).toEqual({ ok: true, why: '' });
    await c.t.db.insert(ledgerReconciliations).values({ id: newId(), season: 'rehearsal', mode: 'rehearsal', at: new Date(now.getTime() - DAY), ok: false, ledgerTotal: 0, onchainTotal: 0, details: { mismatches: ['x'], shortBy: 0, error: null } });
    expect((await rehearsalStatus(c.t.db, now)).ok).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('the launch gate', () => {
  it('names every missing condition, then opens when all hold', async () => {
    const c = await custodyCtx(); close = () => c.t.close();
    c.rpc.genesis = MAINNET_GENESIS;
    const bare = loadConfig({ NODE_ENV: 'test', SESSION_SECRET: 's', WORLD_CHAIN: 'ledger', MAINNET: 'on', MAINNET_RPC_URL: 'https://api.mainnet-beta.solana.com' });
    const missing = await gateStatus({ db: c.t.db, config: bare, rpc: c.rpc, custody: c.custody, now: c.t.clock.now });
    expect(missing.join('\n')).toMatch(/MAINNET_COLD_ADDRESS/);
    expect(missing.join('\n')).toMatch(/MAINNET_KEY/);
    expect(missing.join('\n')).toMatch(/paid RPC/);
    expect(missing.join('\n')).toMatch(/legal review/);
    expect(missing.join('\n')).toMatch(/rehearsal/);
    const config: Config = loadConfig(onEnv());
    await recordLegalReview(c.t.db, 'admin-id', { reviewer: 'A. Lawyer', date: '2026-11-01', note: 'Reviewed.', confirmedBy: 'admin' }, c.t.clock.now());
    await cleanRehearsal(c.t.db, c.t.clock.now());
    expect(await gateStatus({ db: c.t.db, config, rpc: c.rpc, custody: c.custody, now: c.t.clock.now })).toEqual([]);
    c.rpc.genesis = 'Devnet';
    expect((await gateStatus({ db: c.t.db, config, rpc: c.rpc, custody: c.custody, now: c.t.clock.now }))[0]).toMatch(/mainnet-beta/);
  });
});

describe('booting mainnet', () => {
  it('refuses to boot against the wrong network (review focus 5)', async () => {
    const c = await ledgerCtx(); close = () => c.t.close();
    const devnet = new FakeRpc();
    c.t.app.deps.config = loadConfig(onEnv());
    await expect(bootMainnet(c.t.app, { chain: c.chain, worldKey: c.key, rpc: devnet, intervals: false })).rejects.toThrow(/mainnet-beta/);
    const mainnet = new FakeRpc(); mainnet.genesis = MAINNET_GENESIS;
    c.t.app.deps.config = loadConfig(onEnv({ MAINNET: 'rehearsal' }));
    await expect(bootMainnet(c.t.app, { chain: c.chain, worldKey: c.key, rpc: mainnet, intervals: false })).rejects.toThrow(/devnet only/);
  });
  it('refuses MAINNET=on while the gate is closed, naming what is missing', async () => {
    const c = await ledgerCtx(); close = () => c.t.close();
    const rpc = new FakeRpc(); rpc.genesis = MAINNET_GENESIS;
    c.t.app.deps.config = loadConfig(onEnv());
    await expect(bootMainnet(c.t.app, { chain: c.chain, worldKey: c.key, rpc, intervals: false })).rejects.toThrow(/MAINNET=on refused.*legal review/s);
  });
  it('refuses a MAINNET_KEY equal to WORLD_KEY', async () => {
    const c = await ledgerCtx(); close = () => c.t.close();
    const same = randomBytes(32).toString('base64');
    c.t.app.deps.config = loadConfig(onEnv({ MAINNET: 'rehearsal', MAINNET_KEY: same, WORLD_KEY: same }));
    await expect(bootMainnet(c.t.app, { chain: c.chain, worldKey: c.key, rpc: new FakeRpc(), intervals: false })).rejects.toThrow(/differ from WORLD_KEY/);
  });
  it('boots a rehearsal: custody on devnet, routes up', async () => {
    const c = await ledgerCtx(); close = () => c.t.close();
    c.t.app.deps.config = loadConfig(onEnv({ MAINNET: 'rehearsal' }));
    await publishNotice(c.t.db, 'Rehearsal: devnet SOL only.', c.t.clock.now());
    const h = await bootMainnet(c.t.app, { chain: c.chain, worldKey: c.key, rpc: new FakeRpc(), intervals: false });
    expect(h).toMatchObject({ mode: 'rehearsal' });
    expect(h!.custody!.network).toBe('devnet');
    expect((await c.t.as(null).get('/api/mainnet/reserves')).body).toMatchObject({ mode: 'rehearsal', network: 'devnet' });
    await h!.stop();
  });
  it('does nothing outside a ledger world', async () => {
    const c = await ledgerCtx(); close = () => c.t.close();
    expect(await bootMainnet(c.t.app, { chain: new MemoryChain(), worldKey: c.key, intervals: false })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Finish the gate**

In `apps/server/src/ledger/gate.ts`, replace the stub `gateStatus` with:

```ts
import { and, asc, eq, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Config } from '../config.ts';
import { ledgerReconciliations, ledgerWithdrawals } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { books } from './books.ts';
import { currentNotice } from './consent.ts';
import type { Custody } from './custody.ts';
import { assertCustodyNetwork, isAddress, type CustodyRpc } from './rpc.ts';
import { parseMainnetKey } from './vault.ts';

const DAY = 86_400_000;
export const REHEARSAL_DAYS = 14;
const CHECKS_PER_DAY = 144; // one every 10 minutes

/** Spec §9.5: at least 14 days of clean checks since the last failure, 90% of them present, and one withdrawal actually paid. */
export async function rehearsalStatus(db: Db, now: Date): Promise<{ ok: boolean; why: string }> {
  const rows = await db.select({ at: ledgerReconciliations.at, ok: ledgerReconciliations.ok }).from(ledgerReconciliations)
    .where(eq(ledgerReconciliations.mode, 'rehearsal')).orderBy(asc(ledgerReconciliations.at));
  if (!rows.length) return { ok: false, why: 'no rehearsal has run yet (MAINNET=rehearsal on devnet)' };
  let lastFail = -1;
  rows.forEach((r, i) => { if (r.ok === false) lastFail = i; });
  const streak = rows.slice(lastFail + 1);
  if (!streak.length) return { ok: false, why: 'the last rehearsal check failed; the 14 days start again after a clean one' };
  const days = (now.getTime() - streak[0]!.at.getTime()) / DAY;
  if (days < REHEARSAL_DAYS) return { ok: false, why: `the rehearsal has run clean for ${days.toFixed(1)} of ${REHEARSAL_DAYS} days` };
  const since = now.getTime() - REHEARSAL_DAYS * DAY, need = Math.ceil(0.9 * REHEARSAL_DAYS * CHECKS_PER_DAY);
  const passed = streak.filter((r) => r.ok === true && r.at.getTime() >= since).length;
  if (passed < need) return { ok: false, why: `only ${passed} of ${REHEARSAL_DAYS * CHECKS_PER_DAY} rehearsal checks passed in the last 14 days (${need} needed)` };
  const [w] = await db.select({ n: sql<number>`count(*)::int` }).from(ledgerWithdrawals).where(and(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.season, 'rehearsal'), eq(ledgerWithdrawals.status, 'sent')));
  if (!w?.n) return { ok: false, why: 'no withdrawal has been paid during the rehearsal' };
  return { ok: true, why: '' };
}

/** Everything that still blocks MAINNET=on (spec §9); an empty list means the gate is open. */
export async function gateStatus(d: { db: Db; config: Config; rpc: CustodyRpc | null; custody: Custody | null; now: () => Date }): Promise<string[]> {
  const missing: string[] = [], c = d.config;
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(c.mainnetLimits)) if (!Number.isFinite(v) || (k === 'houseFeeBps' ? v < 0 : v <= 0)) missing.push(`the safeguard ${k} is not set`);
  if (!isAddress(c.coldAddress)) missing.push('MAINNET_COLD_ADDRESS is not a Solana address');
  try { parseMainnetKey(c.mainnetKey); if (c.mainnetKey === c.worldKey) missing.push('MAINNET_KEY must differ from WORLD_KEY'); }
  catch { missing.push('MAINNET_KEY is not 32 bytes of base64'); }
  if (!c.mainnetRpcUrl) missing.push('MAINNET_RPC_URL is not set');
  else if (/api\.mainnet-beta\.solana\.com/.test(c.mainnetRpcUrl)) missing.push('MAINNET_RPC_URL must be a paid RPC: the public endpoint is rate-limited and unfit for custody');
  if (d.rpc) { try { await assertCustodyNetwork(d.rpc, 'mainnet'); } catch (e) { missing.unshift(`MAINNET_RPC_URL: ${(e as Error).message}`); } }
  const b = await books(d.db);
  if (!b.ok) missing.push('the books do not balance');
  if (d.custody) {
    try { if ((await d.custody.reserves()) < b.ledgerTotal) missing.push('on-chain reserves are below the books'); }
    catch (e) { missing.push(`the reserves could not be read: ${(e as Error).message}`); }
  }
  if (!(await currentNotice(d.db))) missing.push('no risk notice is published');
  if (!(await legalReviewValid(d.db))) missing.push('no legal review is recorded on /admin');
  const r = await rehearsalStatus(d.db, d.now());
  if (!r.ok) missing.push(r.why);
  return missing;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write bootMainnet**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/boot.ts`:

```ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { Connection } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { users } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { notify } from '../services/notify.ts';
import type { Chain } from '../world/chain.ts';
import { registerMainnetAdminRoutes } from './admin.ts';
import { Custody, capsFromConfig } from './custody.ts';
import { gateStatus } from './gate.ts';
import { LedgerChain } from './ledger-chain.ts';
import { reconcile } from './reconcile.ts';
import { registerMainnetRoutes, type MainnetHandles } from './routes.ts';
import { LEDGER_RUG_RULE, setRugRule } from './rug-rule.ts';
import { Web3CustodyRpc, assertCustodyNetwork, type CustodyNetwork, type CustodyRpc } from './rpc.ts';
import { parseMainnetKey } from './vault.ts';

const S = 1000;

/**
 * Everything a ledger world adds at boot: frozen state, the ledger's rug rule, custody (rehearsal on devnet, on behind the
 * launch gate), the poll / pay / reconcile intervals and the routes. Throws, and so stops the server, when MAINNET is
 * misconfigured or the gate is closed. Returns null outside a ledger world.
 */
export async function bootMainnet(app: FastifyInstance, o: { chain: Chain; worldKey: Buffer; rpc?: CustodyRpc; intervals?: boolean }): Promise<MainnetHandles | null> {
  const { db, config, now } = app.deps;
  if (!(o.chain instanceof LedgerChain)) return null;
  const chain = o.chain;
  await chain.loadFrozen();
  setRugRule(LEDGER_RUG_RULE);
  let custody: Custody | null = null;
  if (config.mainnet !== 'off') {
    const network: CustodyNetwork = config.mainnet === 'on' ? 'mainnet' : config.custodyLocal ? 'local' : 'devnet';
    if (!config.mainnetRpcUrl && !o.rpc) throw new Error(`MAINNET=${config.mainnet} needs MAINNET_RPC_URL`);
    const key = parseMainnetKey(config.mainnetKey);
    if (config.mainnetKey === config.worldKey) throw new Error('MAINNET_KEY must differ from WORLD_KEY');
    const rpc = o.rpc ?? new Web3CustodyRpc(new Connection(config.mainnetRpcUrl!, 'finalized'));
    await assertCustodyNetwork(rpc, network);
    custody = new Custody({
      db, rpc, key, network, cold: config.coldAddress ?? null, caps: capsFromConfig(config), now, chain, worldKey: o.worldKey,
      // LOGIN's payout wallet arrives in Task 17; until then no deposit can be requested.
      payoutOf: async () => null,
      notify: async (userId, agentId, summary) => {
        const [a] = await db.select({ u: users.username }).from(users).where(eq(users.id, agentId));
        await notify(app.deps, { userId, type: 'agent_moment', actorId: agentId, targetType: 'user', targetId: agentId, data: { summary, href: `/agents/${a?.u ?? ''}` } });
      },
    });
    if (config.mainnet === 'on') {
      const missing = await gateStatus({ db, config, rpc, custody, now });
      if (missing.length) throw new Error(`MAINNET=on refused. Still missing:\n- ${missing.join('\n- ')}`);
    }
  }
  const timers: NodeJS.Timeout[] = [];
  const every = (ms: number, name: string, fn: () => Promise<unknown>) => {
    let busy = false;
    const t = setInterval(() => {
      if (busy) return;
      busy = true;
      fn().catch((e) => console.error(`[mainnet] ${name} failed`, e)).finally(() => { busy = false; });
    }, ms);
    t.unref?.(); timers.push(t);
  };
  if (o.intervals !== false) {
    if (custody) {
      const c = custody;
      every(15 * S, 'deposits', () => c.pollDeposits());
      every(30 * S, 'withdrawals', () => c.processWithdrawals());
    }
    every(10 * 60 * S, 'reconciliation', () => reconcile({ db, chain, custody, mode: config.mainnet, now }));
  }
  const handles: MainnetHandles = { mode: config.mainnet, chain, custody, stop: async () => { timers.forEach(clearInterval); } };
  await registerMainnetRoutes(app, handles);
  await registerMainnetAdminRoutes(app, handles);
  return handles;
}
```
