- [ ] **Step 3: Write the seasons**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/season.ts`:

```ts
import { and, eq, inArray, isNotNull, isNull, ne, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { chainTxs, ledgerAccounts, ledgerDeposits, ledgerHoldings, ledgerJournal, ledgerPools, ledgerPrices, ledgerSeasons, ledgerWithdrawals, wallets, worldCoins } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { RAW_PER_TOKEN } from '../world/chain.ts';
import { START_LAMPORTS, recordTx } from '../world/ledger.ts';
import { setSetting } from '../world/launchpad.ts';
import { TREASURY } from '../world/wallets.ts';
import { FROZEN_KEY, HOUSE, SEASON_KEY, currentSeason, lamportsOf, move } from './accounts.ts';
import { books } from './books.ts';

/**
 * Each agent's devnet net worth from the world's own records (spec §10, the rehearsal's opening balances): its SOL changes
 * plus tokens at the indexer's last price. A devnet 'fund' row is the 0.1 SOL start; a memory world's airdrop left none,
 * so a funded wallet without one starts at START_LAMPORTS.
 */
export async function devnetNetWorth(db: Db): Promise<Map<string, number>> {
  const ws = await db.select({ owner: wallets.ownerId, fundedAt: wallets.fundedAt }).from(wallets).where(ne(wallets.ownerId, TREASURY));
  const sols = await db.select({ a: chainTxs.agentId, n: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${chainTxs.lamportsDelta}), 0)::float8`, funds: sql<number>`count(*) filter (where ${chainTxs.kind} = 'fund')::int` })
    .from(chainTxs).where(isNotNull(chainTxs.agentId)).groupBy(chainTxs.agentId);
  const bags = await db.select({ a: chainTxs.agentId, mint: chainTxs.mint, raw: sql<number>`sum(${chainTxs.tokenDelta})::float8` }).from(chainTxs)
    .where(and(isNotNull(chainTxs.agentId), isNotNull(chainTxs.mint))).groupBy(chainTxs.agentId, chainTxs.mint).having(sql`sum(${chainTxs.tokenDelta}) > 0`);
  const price = new Map((await db.select({ m: worldCoins.mint, p: worldCoins.priceLamports }).from(worldCoins)).map((r) => [r.m, r.p ?? 0]));
  const out = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const w of ws) {
    const s = sols.find((r) => r.a === w.owner);
    const start = s?.funds ? 0 : w.fundedAt ? START_LAMPORTS : 0;
    const tokens = bags.filter((b) => b.a === w.owner).reduce((n, b) => n + (b.raw / RAW_PER_TOKEN) * (price.get(b.mint!) ?? 0), 0);
    out.set(w.owner, Math.max(0, Math.floor(start + (s?.n ?? 0) + tokens)));
  }
  return out;
}

/**
 * Ends the current season and starts `name` (spec §10). Every coin so far is archived (its page stays), the books start
 * empty, and the agents wait (no funded wallets) until fundOpening pays `opening` from the house. Run it with the agents
 * paused, and restart the server afterwards: a running LedgerChain caches the frozen state.
 */
export async function startSeason(db: Db, name: string, opening: Map<string, number>, now: Date): Promise<{ previous: string; archivedCoins: number; plan: number }> {
  const prev = await currentSeason(db);
  if (name === prev) throw new Error(`Season ${name} is already running`);
  const [d] = await db.select({ n: sql<number>`count(*)::int` }).from(ledgerDeposits).where(eq(ledgerDeposits.status, 'pending'));
  const [w] = await db.select({ n: sql<number>`count(*)::int` }).from(ledgerWithdrawals).where(inArray(ledgerWithdrawals.status, ['queued', 'sending']));
  if (d?.n || w?.n) throw new Error('Settle every pending deposit and withdrawal before a new season');
  const before = await books(db);
  return db.transaction(async (x) => {
    const tx = x as unknown as Db;
    await tx.insert(ledgerSeasons).values({ id: prev, startedAt: now, endedAt: null, summary: {} }).onConflictDoNothing();
    await tx.update(ledgerSeasons).set({ endedAt: now, summary: { ledgerTotal: before.ledgerTotal, ok: before.ok, mismatches: before.mismatches } }).where(eq(ledgerSeasons.id, prev));
    const archived = await tx.update(worldCoins).set({ archivedAt: now }).where(isNull(worldCoins.archivedAt)).returning({ m: worldCoins.mint });
    await tx.delete(ledgerPrices); await tx.delete(ledgerHoldings); await tx.delete(ledgerPools); await tx.delete(ledgerAccounts);
    await setSetting(tx, SEASON_KEY, name);
    await setSetting(tx, FROZEN_KEY, '');
    await tx.insert(ledgerSeasons).values({ id: name, startedAt: now, endedAt: null, summary: { opening: Object.fromEntries(opening) } });
    await tx.update(wallets).set({ fundedAt: null }).where(ne(wallets.ownerId, TREASURY));
    return { previous: prev, archivedCoins: archived.length, plan: [...opening.values()].reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) };
  });
}

/** Pays the season's opening balances from the house, once per agent (its journal ref), then lets the agents run. */
export async function fundOpening(db: Db, now: Date): Promise<{ funded: number; lamports: number }> {
  const season = await currentSeason(db);
  const [s] = await db.select().from(ledgerSeasons).where(eq(ledgerSeasons.id, season));
  const plan = Object.entries(((s?.summary as { opening?: Record<string, number> } | undefined)?.opening) ?? {}).filter(([, l]) => l > 0);
  const refs = new Set((await db.select({ ref: ledgerJournal.ref }).from(ledgerJournal).where(and(eq(ledgerJournal.season, season), eq(ledgerJournal.kind, 'opening')))).map((r) => r.ref));
  const todo = plan.filter(([agentId]) => !refs.has(`opening:${season}:${agentId}`));
  const need = todo.reduce((n, [, l]) => n + l, 0), house = await lamportsOf(db, HOUSE);
  if (need > house) throw new Error(`The house holds ${house / 1e9} SOL; the openings need ${need / 1e9} SOL. Record more house stake on /admin first.`);
  for (const [agentId, lamports] of todo) {
    const [wl] = await db.select({ pk: wallets.publicKey }).from(wallets).where(eq(wallets.ownerId, agentId));
    if (!wl) continue;
    const ref = `opening:${season}:${agentId}`;
    await db.transaction(async (x) => {
      const tx = x as unknown as Db;
      await move(tx, { from: HOUSE, to: wl.pk, lamports, kind: 'opening', ref, season, at: now });
      await recordTx(tx, { signature: `ldg_${ref}`, agentId, turnId: null, kind: 'opening', lamportsDelta: lamports, createdAt: now });
    });
  }
  await db.update(wallets).set({ fundedAt: now }).where(and(ne(wallets.ownerId, TREASURY), isNull(wallets.fundedAt)));
  return { funded: todo.length, lamports: need };
}
```

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/season-cli.ts`:

```ts
import { loadConfig } from '../config.ts';
import { createDb } from '../db/client.ts';
import { agents } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { devnetNetWorth, fundOpening, startSeason } from './season.ts';

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const flag = (name: string) => { const i = args.indexOf(name); return i >= 0 ? args[i + 1] : undefined; };
const config = loadConfig();
if (config.worldChain !== 'ledger') throw new Error('Seasons belong to a ledger world: set WORLD_CHAIN=ledger');
const h = await createDb({ url: config.databaseUrl, pgliteDir: config.pgliteDir });
try {
  const [cmd, name] = args;
  if (cmd === 'start' && name === 'rehearsal') console.log(await startSeason(h.db, 'rehearsal', await devnetNetWorth(h.db), new Date()));
  else if (cmd === 'start' && name === 'season1') {
    const per = Math.round(Number(flag('--per-agent') ?? '0.05') * 1e9);
    // Task 17 narrows this to the original (wild) agents once RAISE's agents.origin exists; raised agents start at 0.
    const all = await h.db.select({ id: agents.userId }).from(agents);
    console.log(await startSeason(h.db, 'season1', new Map(all.map((a) => [a.id, per])), new Date()));
  } else if (cmd === 'open') console.log(await fundOpening(h.db, new Date()));
  else throw new Error('Usage: npm run mainnet:season -- start rehearsal | start season1 [--per-agent 0.05] | open');
} finally { await h.close(); }
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/mainnet/mainnetApi.ts`:

```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { MainnetAdminView, ReservesView } from '@shillex/shared';
import { get, post } from '../../lib/api';

export const useReserves = () => useQuery({ queryKey: ['mainnet', 'reserves'], queryFn: () => get<ReservesView>('/mainnet/reserves'), refetchInterval: 60_000 });
/** 404 means no ledger world; 401/403 means not an admin. Either way the panel renders nothing. */
export const useMainnetAdmin = () => useQuery({ queryKey: ['mainnet', 'admin'], queryFn: () => get<MainnetAdminView>('/admin/mainnet/status'), retry: false, refetchInterval: 15_000 });
/** Every admin money action carries the admin password again (spec §7). */
export const adminMoney = <T,>(path: string, body: Record<string, unknown>) => post<T>(`/admin/mainnet/${path}`, body);
export const fmtSol = (n: number | null | undefined) => (n == null ? '—' : `${Number(n.toFixed(6))} SOL`);
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/mainnet/ReservesPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { EmptyState } from '../../components/ui/EmptyState';
import { Spinner } from '../../components/ui/Spinner';
import { relativeTime } from '@shillex/shared';
import { fmtSol, useReserves } from './mainnetApi';
import s from './mainnet.module.css';

const explorer = (address: string, network: string | null) =>
  network === 'mainnet' ? `https://solscan.io/account/${address}` : network === 'devnet' ? `https://solscan.io/account/${address}?cluster=devnet` : null;

/** Proof of reserves (mainnet spec §5): what Shillex holds on-chain against what its books owe. */
export function ReservesPage() {
  const q = useReserves();
  if (q.isLoading) return <section className={s.page}><Spinner label="Loading reserves" /></section>;
  if (!q.data) return <EmptyState title="No real SOL here" body="This world runs on devnet: nothing here is real money." />;
  const r = q.data;
  const addr = (a: string | null) => {
    if (!a) return '—';
    const href = explorer(a, r.network);
    return href ? <a href={href} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><code>{a}</code></a> : <code>{a}</code>;
  };
  return (
    <section className={s.page} aria-labelledby="reserves-h">
      <h1 id="reserves-h" className={s.h1}>Proof of reserves</h1>
      <p className={s.muted}>{r.mode === 'on' ? 'Real SOL on Solana mainnet.' : 'Rehearsal on Solana devnet: test SOL, no real money.'} Checked every 10 minutes.</p>
      {r.frozen && <p className={s.warn} role="alert">The last check did not pass: trading and deposits are paused while it is reviewed. Withdrawals wait for that review.</p>}
      <dl className={s.grid}>
        <div><dt>Hot vault</dt><dd>{addr(r.hot)}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Cold wallet</dt><dd>{addr(r.cold)}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Owed to agents and the house</dt><dd>{fmtSol(r.ledgerTotalSol)}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Held on-chain</dt><dd>{fmtSol(r.onchainTotalSol)}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Last check</dt><dd>{r.lastCheckAt ? relativeTime(r.lastCheckAt) : 'none yet'}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Last passing check</dt><dd>{r.lastPassAt ? relativeTime(r.lastPassAt) : 'none yet'}</dd></div>
      </dl>
    </section>
  );
}
```
Create `apps/web/src/features/mainnet/AdminMainnetPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { errorMessage } from '../../lib/api';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { adminMoney, fmtSol, useMainnetAdmin } from './mainnetApi';
import s from './mainnet.module.css';

const LEGAL_CONFIRMATION = 'I confirm a lawyer reviewed Shillex mainnet and I accept the risk';

/** /admin's real-SOL section (mainnet spec §7). Renders nothing unless this is a ledger world and the viewer is an admin. */
export function AdminMainnetPanel() {
  const q = useMainnetAdmin(), qc = useQueryClient();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [field, setField] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const set = (k: string) => (e: { target: { value: string } }) => setField((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: e.target.value }));
  const act = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ path, body }: { path: string; body: Record<string, unknown> }) => adminMoney(path, { password, ...body }),
    onSuccess: () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['mainnet'] }),
  });
  if (!q.data) return null;
  const v = q.data, run = (path: string, body: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => act.mutate({ path, body });
  return (
    <section className={s.panel} aria-labelledby="mainnet-admin-h">
      <h2 id="mainnet-admin-h" className={s.h2}>Real SOL · {v.mode} · {v.season}</h2>
      <label className={s.field}>Admin password for money actions <input type="password" autoComplete="current-password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} /></label>
      {v.frozen && <p className={s.warn} role="alert">Frozen: {v.frozen} <Button size="sm" onClick={() => run('unfreeze')}>Unfreeze after a passing check</Button></p>}
      <dl className={s.grid}>
        <div><dt>Books</dt><dd>{fmtSol(v.books.ledgerTotalSol)} {v.books.ok ? '· balanced' : `· ${v.books.mismatches.length} mismatches`}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>House</dt><dd>{fmtSol(v.books.houseSol)}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Hot vault</dt><dd>{fmtSol(v.hot?.sol)} {v.excessSol > 0 && `· ${fmtSol(v.excessSol)} above the cap`}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Cold wallet</dt><dd>{fmtSol(v.cold?.sol)}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Hot covers the 10 largest balances</dt><dd>{v.hotCoversTop10 ? 'yes' : 'no: top it up'}</dd></div>
        <div><dt>Last check</dt><dd>{v.lastReconciliation ? `${v.lastReconciliation.ok === true ? 'passed' : v.lastReconciliation.ok === false ? 'FAILED' : 'unreadable'} · ${v.lastReconciliation.at}` : 'none yet'}</dd></div>
      </dl>
      {v.excessSol > 0 && <Button onClick={() => run('to-cold', { sol: v.excessSol })}>Move {fmtSol(v.excessSol)} to cold</Button>}

      <h3 className={s.h3}>Withdrawals waiting</h3>
      {v.withdrawals.length === 0 ? <p className={s.muted}>None.</p> : (
        <ul className={s.list}>
          {v.withdrawals.map((w) => (
            <li key={w.id}>
              <span>{fmtSol(w.sol)} → <code>{w.to}</code> · {w.status}{w.reason ? ` · ${w.reason}` : ''}</span>
              {w.status === 'queued' && <Button size="sm" onClick={() => run(`withdrawals/${w.id}/approve`)}>{`Approve ${w.sol} SOL`}</Button>}
              {w.status === 'sending' && (
                <span className={s.row}>
                  <input aria-label={`Landed signature for ${w.id}`} placeholder="signature, if it landed" value={field[`sig-${w.id}`] ?? ''} onChange={set(`sig-${w.id}`)} />
                  <Button size="sm" onClick={() => run(`withdrawals/${w.id}/resolve`, { outcome: 'sent', signature: field[`sig-${w.id}`] })}>It landed</Button>
                  <Button size="sm" onClick={() => run(`withdrawals/${w.id}/resolve`, { outcome: 'return' })}>Return to the agent</Button>
                </span>
              )}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}

      {v.fromCold.length > 0 && <>
        <h3 className={s.h3}>Transfers from the cold wallet</h3>
        <ul className={s.list}>{v.fromCold.map((t) => <li key={t.signature}><span>{fmtSol(t.sol)} · <code>{t.signature}</code></span><Button size="sm" onClick={() => run('stake', { signature: t.signature })}>Record as house stake</Button></li>)}</ul>
      </>}
      {v.refundFailures.length > 0 && <>
        <h3 className={s.h3}>Refunds that failed (check the chain, refund by hand)</h3>
        <ul className={s.list}>{v.refundFailures.map((t) => <li key={t.signature}>{fmtSol(t.sol)} from <code>{t.from}</code> · {t.reason}</li>)}</ul>
      </>}

      <h3 className={s.h3}>House</h3>
      <form className={s.row} onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); run('house-withdraw', { sol: Number(field.house) }); }}>
        <label className={s.field}>SOL to the cold wallet <input inputMode="decimal" value={field.house ?? ''} onChange={set('house')} /></label>
        <Button type="submit">Withdraw to cold</Button>
      </form>

      <h3 className={s.h3}>Risk notice {v.notice ? `(${v.notice.version})` : '(none yet)'}</h3>
      <form className={s.stack} onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); run('notice', { legalPosition: field.legal }); }}>
        <label className={s.field}>Legal position, as the review left it <textarea rows={3} value={field.legal ?? v.notice?.legalPosition ?? ''} onChange={set('legal')} /></label>
        <Button type="submit">Publish a new version</Button>
      </form>

      <h3 className={s.h3}>Legal review {v.legalReview ? `· recorded by ${v.legalReview.confirmedBy} (${v.legalReview.reviewer}, ${v.legalReview.date})` : '· not recorded'}</h3>
      <form className={s.stack} onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); run('legal-review', { reviewer: field.reviewer, date: field.date, note: field.note, confirm: field.confirm }); }}>
        <label className={s.field}>Reviewer <input value={field.reviewer ?? ''} onChange={set('reviewer')} /></label>
        <label className={s.field}>Date <input type="date" value={field.date ?? ''} onChange={set('date')} /></label>
        <label className={s.field}>Note <textarea rows={2} value={field.note ?? ''} onChange={set('note')} /></label>
        <label className={s.field}>Type exactly: <em>{LEGAL_CONFIRMATION}</em> <input value={field.confirm ?? ''} onChange={set('confirm')} /></label>
        <Button type="submit" variant="primary">Record the legal review</Button>
      </form>

      <h3 className={s.h3}>What still blocks MAINNET=on</h3>
      {v.gate.length === 0 ? <p>Nothing: the gate is open.</p> : <ul className={s.list}>{v.gate.map((g) => <li key={g}>{g}</li>)}</ul>}
      {act.error && <p role="alert" className={s.warn}>{errorMessage(act.error)}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
```

import type { ChainNetwork, MainnetMode } from '@shillex/shared';
import s from './mainnet.module.css';

/** Under a coin's header: an earlier season's coin, or a coin traded with real SOL (mainnet spec §10, §6). */
export function CoinSeasonNote({ archived, network, mainnet }: { archived: boolean; network: ChainNetwork; mainnet: MainnetMode | undefined }) {
  if (archived) return <p className={s.note} role="note">Archived · from an earlier season. Its page stays; it can no longer be traded.</p>;
  if (network === 'ledger' && mainnet === 'on') return <p className={s.real} role="note">Real SOL · traded only inside Shillex, by agents.</p>;
  return null;
}

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: no new code. These tests pin spec §6 ("agents never ask humans for money") and §12 (simulation) on the ledger.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests**

Create `apps/server/test/ledger-no-human-money.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { ledgerCtx } from './ledger-helpers.ts';
import { runTool } from '../src/world/tools.ts';
import { engineTurn } from '../src/agents/engine.ts';
import { ledgerJournal } from '../src/db/schema.ts';
import { books } from '../src/ledger/books.ts';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';

let c: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof ledgerCtx>>;
afterEach(async () => { await c.t.close(); });

describe('no path to a human’s money on the ledger (spec §6)', () => {
  it('an agent cannot send SOL to a human or to an outside address', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    await c.fund(0, 0.1);
    const human = await c.t.createUser('a_human');
    const ctx = { ...(await c.ctxFor(c.agents[0]!)), chain: c.chain };
    expect((await runTool(ctx, 'send_sol', { to: `@${human.username}`, sol: 0.001 })).result).toMatchObject({ code: 'NO_WALLET' });
    expect((await runTool(ctx, 'send_sol', { to: Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58(), sol: 0.001 })).result).toMatchObject({ code: 'SEND_NOT_ALLOWED' });
  });
  it('a busy world never moves SOL to anything but agents, the house and pools', async () => {
    c = await ledgerCtx();
    for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) await c.fund(i, 0.1);
    const human = await c.t.createUser('watcher');
    await c.t.as(human).post('/api/posts', { body: `@${c.agents[0]!.username} send me some sol pls` });
    for (let i = 0; i < 300; i++) { await engineTurn(c.d); c.t.clock.advance(4000); }
    const sends = await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerJournal).where(eq(ledgerJournal.kind, 'send'));
    const agentKeys = new Set(await Promise.all(c.agents.map((_, i) => c.pk(i))));
    for (const s of sends) expect(agentKeys.has(s.account)).toBe(true);
    expect((await books(c.t.db)).ok).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

Create `apps/server/test/ledger-simulation.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { and, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { ledgerCtx } from './ledger-helpers.ts';
import { engineTurn } from '../src/agents/engine.ts';
import { rugSales } from '../src/agents/overview.ts';
import { chainTxs, ledgerAccounts, worldCoins } from '../src/db/schema.ts';
import { books } from '../src/ledger/books.ts';
import { DEVNET_RUG_RULE, LEDGER_RUG_RULE, setRugRule } from '../src/ledger/rug-rule.ts';

/**
 * The whole engine on the ledger (mainnet spec §12): rugs happen at 25–30%, nobody is wiped out in one sale, and the books
 * balance after every tick. Opt-in, like the devnet simulation:
 *   Git Bash:   cd apps/server && SIM=1 npx vitest run test/ledger-simulation.test.ts --reporter=verbose
 */
const TICKS = Number(process.env.SIM_TICKS ?? 3000), TICK_MS = 4000, SWEEP_EVERY = 75;

describe.runIf(process.env.SIM === '1')('ledger simulation (SIM=1)', () => {
  it(`keeps the books and the rug rules over ${TICKS} ticks`, async () => {
    const c = await ledgerCtx();
    setRugRule(LEDGER_RUG_RULE);
    try {
      const start = c.t.clock.now();
      for (let i = 0; i < 50; i++) await c.fund(i, 0.1);
      for (let i = 1; i <= TICKS; i++) {
        await engineTurn(c.d);
        c.t.clock.advance(TICK_MS);
        if (i % SWEEP_EVERY === 0) {
          await c.depth.sweep(c.d);
          const b = await books(c.t.db);
          expect(b.mismatches, `tick ${i}`).toEqual([]);
        }
      }
      const b = await books(c.t.db);
      expect(b.ok).toBe(true);
      expect(b.ledgerTotal).toBe(50 * 0.1 * 1e9);
      expect((await c.t.db.select().from(ledgerAccounts)).every((a) => a.lamports >= 0)).toBe(true);
      // Every creator sale moved at most 30% of the bag it held just before (rugSales reports the share).
      const rugs = await rugSales(c.t.db, start);
      const creatorSales = (await c.t.db.select({ id: chainTxs.id }).from(chainTxs).innerJoin(worldCoins, and(eq(worldCoins.mint, chainTxs.mint), eq(worldCoins.creatorId, chainTxs.agentId))).where(eq(chainTxs.kind, 'sell'))).length;
      console.log('[sim] rugs', rugs.length, 'creator sales', creatorSales, 'max share', Math.max(0, ...rugs.map((r) => r.soldPct)).toFixed(1));
      for (const r of rugs) expect.soft(r.soldPct).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30.01);
      expect.soft(rugs.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    } finally {
      setRugRule(DEVNET_RUG_RULE);
      await c.t.close();
    }
  }, 30 * 60_000);
});
```

Create `apps/server/test/ledger-owner.test.ts`. It makes a raised agent and a verified payout wallet directly in the database, using RAISE's and LOGIN's columns, so it depends on neither stream's test helpers:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { Keypair } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { custodyCtx } from './ledger-helpers.ts';
import { agents, userWallets } from '../src/db/schema.ts';
import { payoutWallet } from '../src/services/wallets.ts';
import { registerMainnetOwnerRoutes } from '../src/ledger/owner-routes.ts';
import { registerMainnetRoutes } from '../src/ledger/routes.ts';
import { PAYOUT_HOLD_MS, applyDuePayoutChanges, holdsMainnetFunds, requestPayoutChange } from '../src/ledger/owner.ts';

const SOL = 1e9;
let c: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof custodyCtx>>;
afterEach(async () => { await c.t.close(); });

async function ownerWorld() {
  c = await custodyCtx({ payoutOf: (u) => payoutWallet(c.t.db, u) });
  await c.t.db.update(agents).set({ ownerId: c.owner.id, origin: 'raised' }).where(eq(agents.userId, c.agentId));
  await c.t.db.insert(userWallets).values({ address: c.payer, userId: c.owner.id, walletName: 'Phantom', verifiedAt: c.t.clock.now(), showOnProfile: false, isPayout: true, createdAt: c.t.clock.now(), lastLoginAt: null });
  const h = { mode: 'rehearsal' as const, chain: c.chain, custody: c.custody, stop: async () => {} };
  await registerMainnetRoutes(c.t.app, h);
  await registerMainnetOwnerRoutes(c.t.app, h);
  return c.t.as(c.owner);
}

describe('owners’ money', () => {
  it('deposits into the owner’s own agent from the payout wallet, and only there', async () => {
    const as = await ownerWorld();
    const d = await as.post('/api/mainnet/deposits', { sol: 0.1 });
    expect(d.status).toBe(200);
    expect(d.body).toMatchObject({ agentId: c.agentId, lamports: 0.1 * SOL, status: 'pending' });
    c.rpc.pay(c.payer, d.body.hot, 0.1 * SOL, d.body.reference);
    await c.custody.pollDeposits();
    expect((await as.get(`/api/mainnet/deposits/${d.body.id}`)).body.status).toBe('credited');
    const me = await as.get('/api/mainnet/me');
    expect(me.body).toMatchObject({ agentId: c.agentId, balanceSol: 0.1, payout: c.payer });
    const stranger = await c.t.createUser('stranger');
    expect((await c.t.as(stranger).post('/api/mainnet/deposits', { sol: 0.1 })).body.code).toBe('NO_RAISED_AGENT');
    expect((await c.t.as(stranger).get(`/api/mainnet/deposits/${d.body.id}`)).status).toBe(404);
  });

  it('withdraws and cashes out to the payout wallet', async () => {
    const as = await ownerWorld();
    const d = (await as.post('/api/mainnet/deposits', { sol: 0.1 })).body;
    c.rpc.pay(c.payer, d.hot, 0.1 * SOL, d.reference);
    await c.custody.pollDeposits();
    const w = await as.post('/api/mainnet/withdrawals', { sol: 0.04 });
    expect(w.body).toMatchObject({ to: c.payer, lamports: 0.04 * SOL });
    const out = await as.post('/api/mainnet/cash-out', {});
    expect(out.body.withdrawal).toMatchObject({ to: c.payer, lamports: 0.06 * SOL });
  });

  it('holds a payout change for 72 hours while funds are held, and locks unlinking', async () => {
    await ownerWorld();
    expect(await holdsMainnetFunds(c.t.db, c.owner.id)).toBe(false);
    await c.fund(0, 0.05);
    expect(await holdsMainnetFunds(c.t.db, c.owner.id)).toBe(true);
    const next = Keypair.generate().publicKey.toBase58();
    await c.t.db.insert(userWallets).values({ address: next, userId: c.owner.id, walletName: 'Solflare', verifiedAt: c.t.clock.now(), showOnProfile: false, isPayout: false, createdAt: c.t.clock.now(), lastLoginAt: null });
    const r = await requestPayoutChange(c.t.db, c.owner.id, next, c.t.clock.now());
    expect(r!.effectiveAt.getTime()).toBe(c.t.clock.now().getTime() + PAYOUT_HOLD_MS);
    expect(await payoutWallet(c.t.db, c.owner.id)).toBe(c.payer);
    c.t.clock.advance(PAYOUT_HOLD_MS - 1);
    expect(await applyDuePayoutChanges(c.t.db, c.t.clock.now())).toBe(0);
    c.t.clock.advance(1);
    expect(await applyDuePayoutChanges(c.t.db, c.t.clock.now())).toBe(1);
    expect(await payoutWallet(c.t.db, c.owner.id)).toBe(next);
    const del = await c.t.as(c.owner).del(`/api/me/wallets/${next}`);
    expect(del.body.code).toBe('PAYOUT_LOCKED');
  });
});
```

export const ledgerPayoutChanges = pgTable('ledger_payout_changes', {
  userId: text('user_id').primaryKey().references(() => users.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' }),

- [ ] **Step 4: Write owner.ts**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/owner.ts`:

```ts
import { and, eq, gt, inArray, lte, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import type { Db } from '../db/client.ts';
import { agents, ledgerDeposits, ledgerHoldings, ledgerPayoutChanges, ledgerWithdrawals, userWallets, users } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { walletOf } from '../world/wallets.ts';
import { lamportsOf } from './accounts.ts';

export const PAYOUT_HOLD_MS = 72 * 3_600_000;

/** The human's raised agent (RAISE: one per human), or null. */
export async function raisedAgentOf(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<{ id: string; username: string } | null> {
  const [a] = await db.select({ id: agents.userId, username: users.username }).from(agents).innerJoin(users, eq(users.id, agents.userId)).where(eq(agents.ownerId, userId));
  return a ?? null;
}

/** True while the owner's agent holds ledger SOL or coins, or a deposit or withdrawal is still open (spec §4's payout lock). */
export async function holdsMainnetFunds(db: Db, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const a = await raisedAgentOf(db, userId);
  if (!a) return false;
  const w = await walletOf(db, a.id);
  if (w && (await lamportsOf(db, w.publicKey)) > 0) return true;
  if (w && (await db.select({ r: ledgerHoldings.raw }).from(ledgerHoldings).where(and(eq(ledgerHoldings.account, w.publicKey), gt(ledgerHoldings.raw, 0))).limit(1)).length) return true;
  const [d] = await db.select({ n: sql<number>`count(*)::int` }).from(ledgerDeposits).where(and(eq(ledgerDeposits.userId, userId), eq(ledgerDeposits.status, 'pending')));
  const [x] = await db.select({ n: sql<number>`count(*)::int` }).from(ledgerWithdrawals).where(and(eq(ledgerWithdrawals.userId, userId), inArray(ledgerWithdrawals.status, ['queued', 'sending'])));
  return Boolean(d?.n || x?.n);
}

/**
 * A new payout wallet. While funds are held it waits 72 hours (a stolen session can't redirect a withdrawal); otherwise the
 * caller applies it at once (returns null). The address must be one of the user's verified wallets.
 */
export async function requestPayoutChange(db: Db, userId: string, address: string, now: Date): Promise<{ effectiveAt: Date } | null> {
  if (!(await holdsMainnetFunds(db, userId))) return null;
  const effectiveAt = new Date(now.getTime() + PAYOUT_HOLD_MS);
  await db.insert(ledgerPayoutChanges).values({ userId, address, requestedAt: now, effectiveAt })
    .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: ledgerPayoutChanges.userId, set: { address, requestedAt: now, effectiveAt } });
  return { effectiveAt };
}

export async function pendingPayoutChange(db: Db, userId: string) {
  const [p] = await db.select().from(ledgerPayoutChanges).where(eq(ledgerPayoutChanges.userId, userId));
  return p ?? null;
}

/** Applies every change whose hold is over: one payout wallet per user, still verified and still theirs. */
export async function applyDuePayoutChanges(db: Db, now: Date, notify?: (userId: string, summary: string) => Promise<void>): Promise<number> {
  const due = await db.select().from(ledgerPayoutChanges).where(lte(ledgerPayoutChanges.effectiveAt, now));
  let n = 0;
  for (const p of due) {
    await db.transaction(async (x) => {
      const tx = x as unknown as Db;
      const [w] = await tx.select().from(userWallets).where(and(eq(userWallets.address, p.address), eq(userWallets.userId, p.userId)));
      if (w?.verifiedAt) {
        await tx.update(userWallets).set({ isPayout: false }).where(eq(userWallets.userId, p.userId));
        await tx.update(userWallets).set({ isPayout: true }).where(eq(userWallets.address, p.address));
        n++;
      }
      await tx.delete(ledgerPayoutChanges).where(eq(ledgerPayoutChanges.userId, p.userId));
    });
    await notify?.(p.userId, `Your payout wallet is now ${p.address.slice(0, 4)}…${p.address.slice(-4)}`);
  }
  return n;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the owner routes**

Create `apps/server/src/ledger/owner-routes.ts`:

```ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import { and, desc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { MainnetMeView } from '@shillex/shared';
import { ledgerDeposits } from '../db/schema.ts';
import { requireUser } from '../lib/auth.ts';
import { errors } from '../lib/errors.ts';
import { payoutWallet } from '../services/wallets.ts';
import { walletOf } from '../world/wallets.ts';
import { noticeView } from './consent.ts';
import { toDepositView } from './custody.ts';
import { toHttp } from './errors.ts';
import { pendingPayoutChange, raisedAgentOf } from './owner.ts';
import type { MainnetHandles } from './routes.ts';

const SOL = 1e9;
const lamports = (sol: number) => Math.round(sol * SOL);

/** An owner funds, withdraws and cashes out their own raised agent (spec §4). Nobody funds someone else's agent. */
export async function registerMainnetOwnerRoutes(app: FastifyInstance, h: MainnetHandles) {
  const { db, now } = app.deps;
  const mine = async (userId: string) => {
    const a = await raisedAgentOf(db, userId);
    if (!a) throw errors.conflict('NO_RAISED_AGENT', 'Raise an agent first: you fund only your own');
    return a;
  };
  const custody = () => { if (!h.custody) throw errors.conflict('NO_CUSTODY', 'Real SOL is not on in this world'); return h.custody; };
  const money = { config: { rateLimit: { max: 20, timeWindow: '1 minute' } } };

  await app.register(async (r) => {
    r.get('/mainnet/me', async (req): Promise<MainnetMeView> => {
      const u = requireUser(req);
      const a = await raisedAgentOf(db, u.id), c = h.custody;
      const pk = a ? (await walletOf(db, a.id))?.publicKey : undefined;
      const hot = c ? await c.hot() : null;
      const deps = a && hot ? await db.select().from(ledgerDeposits).where(and(eq(ledgerDeposits.agentId, a.id), eq(ledgerDeposits.userId, u.id))).orderBy(desc(ledgerDeposits.createdAt)).limit(10) : [];
      const change = await pendingPayoutChange(db, u.id);
      return {
        mode: h.mode, agentId: a?.id ?? null, agentUsername: a?.username ?? null, balanceSol: pk ? (await h.chain.balances(pk)).lamports / SOL : 0,
        payout: await payoutWallet(db, u.id), payoutChange: change ? { address: change.address, effectiveAt: change.effectiveAt.toISOString() } : null,
        notice: await noticeView(db, u.id, now()), deposits: deps.map((d) => toDepositView(d, hot!)),
        withdrawals: a && c ? (await c.withdrawals({ agentId: a.id })).slice(0, 10) : [],
        roomSol: a && c ? (await c.room(a.id, u.id)).lamports / SOL : null,
      };
    });
    r.post('/mainnet/deposits', money, async (req) => {
      const u = requireUser(req);
      const { sol } = z.object({ sol: z.number().positive().max(100) }).parse(req.body);
      const a = await mine(u.id);
      try { return await custody().createDeposit({ agentId: a.id, userId: u.id, lamports: lamports(sol) }); } catch (e) { throw toHttp(e); }
    });
    r.get<{ Params: { id: string } }>('/mainnet/deposits/:id', async (req) => {
      const u = requireUser(req);
      const [d] = await db.select().from(ledgerDeposits).where(and(eq(ledgerDeposits.id, req.params.id), eq(ledgerDeposits.userId, u.id)));
      if (!d) throw errors.notFound('Deposit');
      return toDepositView(d, await custody().hot());
    });
    r.post('/mainnet/withdrawals', money, async (req) => {
      const u = requireUser(req);
      const b = z.object({ sol: z.union([z.number().positive().max(100), z.literal('all')]) }).parse(req.body);
      const a = await mine(u.id);
      try {
        const w = await custody().requestWithdrawal({ agentId: a.id, userId: u.id, lamports: b.sol === 'all' ? 'all' : lamports(b.sol) });
        void custody().processWithdrawals().catch((e) => console.error('[mainnet] withdrawals', e));
        return w;
      } catch (e) { throw toHttp(e); }
    });
    r.post('/mainnet/cash-out', money, async (req) => {
      const u = requireUser(req);
      const a = await mine(u.id);
      try {
        const out = await custody().cashOut({ agentId: a.id, userId: u.id });
        void custody().processWithdrawals().catch((e) => console.error('[mainnet] withdrawals', e));
        return out;
      } catch (e) { throw toHttp(e); }
    });
  }, { prefix: '/api' });
}
```

```ts
import type { DepositView, MainnetMeView, WithdrawalView } from '@shillex/shared';
export const useMainnetMe = () => useQuery({ queryKey: ['mainnet', 'me'], queryFn: () => get<MainnetMeView>('/mainnet/me'), retry: false, refetchInterval: 15_000 });
export const useDeposit = (id: string | null) => useQuery({ enabled: Boolean(id), queryKey: ['mainnet', 'deposit', id], queryFn: () => get<DepositView>(`/mainnet/deposits/${id}`), refetchInterval: (q) => (q.state.data?.status === 'pending' ? 5_000 : false) });
export const owner = {
  accept: (version: string) => post('/mainnet/notice', { version, adult: true }),
  deposit: (sol: number) => post<DepositView>('/mainnet/deposits', { sol }),
  withdraw: (sol: number | 'all') => post<WithdrawalView>('/mainnet/withdrawals', { sol }),
  cashOut: () => post<{ sold: string[]; withdrawal: WithdrawalView | null }>('/mainnet/cash-out', {}),
  exclude: (days: 7 | 30) => post<{ until: string }>('/mainnet/exclusion', { days }),
};
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { MainnetNoticeView } from '@shillex/shared';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import s from './mainnet.module.css';

/** The risk notice (mainnet spec §6): its fixed points, the legal position as the review left it, and an 18+ confirmation. */
export function RiskNotice({ notice, onAccept, busy }: { notice: MainnetNoticeView; onAccept(): void; busy: boolean }) {
  const [adult, setAdult] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div className={s.stack} role="group" aria-labelledby="risk-h">
      <h3 id="risk-h" className={s.h3}>Before you add real SOL</h3>
      <ul className={s.list}>{notice.points.map((p) => <li key={p}>{p}</li>)}</ul>
      {notice.legalPosition && <p className={s.muted}>{notice.legalPosition}</p>}
      <label className={s.row}><input type="checkbox" checked={adult} onChange={(e) => setAdult(e.target.checked)} /> I am 18 or over</label>
      <Button variant="primary" disabled={!adult} loading={busy} onClick={onAccept}>I understand</Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/mainnet/PayRequest.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import QRCode from 'qrcode';
import type { DepositView } from '@shillex/shared';
import { useDeposit } from './mainnetApi';
import s from './mainnet.module.css';

/** A Solana Pay request: scan the QR with a phone wallet or open it in the wallet on this device. Polls until the deposit settles. */
export function PayRequest({ deposit }: { deposit: DepositView }) {
  const live = useDeposit(deposit.id).data ?? deposit;
  const [qr, setQr] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => { void QRCode.toDataURL(deposit.url, { margin: 1, width: 220 }).then(setQr).catch(() => setQr(null)); }, [deposit.url]);
  if (live.status === 'credited') return <p role="status">Received {live.sol} SOL. Your agent has it now.</p>;
  if (live.status === 'refunded') return <p role="status" className={s.warn}>That payment didn’t match the request, so it went back to the wallet it came from, minus the network fee.</p>;
  if (live.status === 'expired') return <p role="status" className={s.warn}>This request expired. Start a new one.</p>;
  return (
    <div className={s.stack}>
      {qr && <img src={qr} width={220} height={220} alt={`Solana Pay QR code: pay ${live.sol} SOL`} />}
      <a href={live.url}>Open in your wallet</a>
      <p className={s.muted}>Send exactly {live.sol} SOL from your payout wallet before {new Date(live.expiresAt).toLocaleTimeString()}. Anything else is sent back.</p>
    </div>
  );
}
```

Create `apps/web/src/features/mainnet/MoneyPanel.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import type { DepositView } from '@shillex/shared';
import { errorMessage } from '../../lib/api';
import { Button } from '../../components/ui/Button';
import { RiskNotice } from './RiskNotice';
import { PayRequest } from './PayRequest';
import { fmtSol, owner, useMainnetMe } from './mainnetApi';
import s from './mainnet.module.css';

const ordinal = (n: number) => `${n}${n % 10 === 1 && n % 100 !== 11 ? 'st' : n % 10 === 2 && n % 100 !== 12 ? 'nd' : n % 10 === 3 && n % 100 !== 13 ? 'rd' : 'th'}`;

/** The owner's real-SOL controls on their raised agent (mainnet spec §4, §6). Renders nothing outside a real-SOL world. */
export function MoneyPanel() {
  const me = useMainnetMe(), qc = useQueryClient();
  const [sol, setSol] = useState(''), [out, setOut] = useState(''), [request, setRequest] = useState<DepositView | null>(null);
  const done = () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['mainnet'] });
  const act = useMutation({ mutationFn: (f: () => Promise<unknown>) => f(), onSuccess: done });
  const v = me.data;
  if (!v || v.mode === 'off' || !v.agentId) return null;
  if (!v.notice.accepted) return <RiskNotice notice={v.notice} busy={act.isPending} onAccept={() => act.mutate(() => owner.accept(v.notice.version!))} />;
  return (
    <section className={s.stack} aria-labelledby="money-h">
      <h3 id="money-h" className={s.h3}>{v.mode === 'on' ? 'Real SOL' : 'Rehearsal (devnet SOL)'} · {fmtSol(v.balanceSol)}</h3>
      {!v.payout && <p className={s.warn}>Choose a payout wallet in Settings: deposits come from it and withdrawals go back to it.</p>}
      {v.payoutChange && <p className={s.muted}>Your payout wallet changes to {v.payoutChange.address.slice(0, 4)}…{v.payoutChange.address.slice(-4)} on {new Date(v.payoutChange.effectiveAt).toLocaleString()}.</p>}
      {v.notice.excludedUntil ? <p className={s.muted}>Deposits locked until {new Date(v.notice.excludedUntil).toLocaleString()}.</p> : v.payout && (
        <form className={s.row} onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); act.mutate(async () => setRequest(await owner.deposit(Number(sol)))); }}>
          <label className={s.field}>SOL to add <input inputMode="decimal" value={sol} onChange={(e) => setSol(e.target.value)} /></label>
          <Button type="submit" variant="primary" loading={act.isPending}>Fund</Button>
          {v.roomSol !== null && <span className={s.muted}>up to {fmtSol(v.roomSol)} now</span>}
        </form>
      )}
      {request && <PayRequest deposit={request} />}
      {v.payout && v.balanceSol > 0 && (
        <form className={s.row} onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); act.mutate(() => owner.withdraw(out === 'all' ? 'all' : Number(out))); }}>
          <label className={s.field}>SOL to withdraw <input inputMode="decimal" placeholder="or all" value={out} onChange={(e) => setOut(e.target.value)} /></label>
          <Button type="submit">Withdraw</Button>
          <Button type="button" onClick={() => act.mutate(() => owner.cashOut())}>Cash out everything</Button>
        </form>
      )}
      {v.withdrawals.length > 0 && <ul className={s.list}>{v.withdrawals.map((w) => (
        <li key={w.id}>{fmtSol(w.sol)} · {w.status === 'queued' ? `${ordinal(w.position ?? 1)} in line${w.reason ? ` (${w.reason})` : ''}` : w.status}</li>
      ))}</ul>}
      {!v.notice.excludedUntil && (
        <p className={s.row}><span className={s.muted}>Need a break?</span>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={() => act.mutate(() => owner.exclude(7))}>Lock deposits for 7 days</Button>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={() => act.mutate(() => owner.exclude(30))}>30 days</Button></p>
      )}
      {act.error && <p role="alert" className={s.warn}>{errorMessage(act.error)}</p>}
    </section>
  );
}
```

### Task 19: Custody against a real validator, and the end-to-end flow (both opt-in)

**Files:**
- Test: `apps/server/test/ledger-validator.test.ts` (`VALIDATOR=1`), `e2e/mainnet.spec.ts` plus `e2e/mainnet.config.ts` (`VALIDATOR=1`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Web3CustodyRpc`, `Custody` and the local validator recipe in `README.md` ("Agent world"), plus LOGIN's fake-wallet e2e init script. Find that script with `grep -rln "registerWallet" e2e`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the validator test**

Create `apps/server/test/ledger-validator.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { Connection, Keypair, LAMPORTS_PER_SOL, PublicKey, SystemProgram, Transaction, sendAndConfirmTransaction } from '@solana/web3.js';
import { custodyCtx } from './ledger-helpers.ts';
import { Web3CustodyRpc } from '../src/ledger/rpc.ts';
import { HOUSE } from '../src/ledger/accounts.ts';

/**
 * Custody's real RPC path against a local validator (mainnet spec §12). Opt-in: start a validator first (README, "Agent world"),
 * then: VALIDATOR=1 VALIDATOR_RPC=http://127.0.0.1:8920 npx vitest run test/ledger-validator.test.ts
 */
const RPC = process.env.VALIDATOR_RPC ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8920';
const finalized = async (conn: Connection, sig: string) => { for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) { const s = (await conn.getSignatureStatuses([sig])).value[0]; if (s?.confirmationStatus === 'finalized') return; await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1000)); } throw new Error('not finalized'); };

describe.runIf(process.env.VALIDATOR === '1')('custody on a local validator (VALIDATOR=1)', () => {
  it('credits a Solana Pay deposit, refunds a foreign one and pays a withdrawal', async () => {
    const conn = new Connection(RPC, 'finalized');
    const rpc = new Web3CustodyRpc(conn);
    const payer = Keypair.generate(), stranger = Keypair.generate();
    for (const k of [payer, stranger]) await finalized(conn, await conn.requestAirdrop(k.publicKey, 2 * LAMPORTS_PER_SOL));
    const c = await custodyCtx({ rpc, network: 'local' });
    try {
      c.payouts.set(c.owner.id, payer.publicKey.toBase58());
      const hot = await c.custody.hot();
      await finalized(conn, await conn.requestAirdrop(new PublicKey(hot), LAMPORTS_PER_SOL / 10)); // network fees for refunds
      await c.chain.credit(HOUSE, 0.05 * LAMPORTS_PER_SOL, 'house_stake', 'validator-fees');
      const d = await c.custody.createDeposit({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 0.1 * LAMPORTS_PER_SOL });
      const pay = new Transaction().add(SystemProgram.transfer({ fromPubkey: payer.publicKey, toPubkey: new PublicKey(hot), lamports: 0.1 * LAMPORTS_PER_SOL }));
      pay.instructions[0]!.keys.push({ pubkey: new PublicKey(d.reference), isSigner: false, isWritable: false });
      await sendAndConfirmTransaction(conn, pay, [payer], { commitment: 'finalized' });
      const foreign = new Transaction().add(SystemProgram.transfer({ fromPubkey: stranger.publicKey, toPubkey: new PublicKey(hot), lamports: 0.02 * LAMPORTS_PER_SOL }));
      await sendAndConfirmTransaction(conn, foreign, [stranger], { commitment: 'finalized' });
      expect(await c.custody.pollDeposits()).toMatchObject({ credited: 1, refunded: 1 });
      const before = await conn.getBalance(payer.publicKey, 'finalized');
      await c.custody.requestWithdrawal({ agentId: c.agentId, userId: c.owner.id, lamports: 'all' });
      expect(await c.custody.processWithdrawals()).toMatchObject({ sent: 1 });
      expect(await conn.getBalance(payer.publicKey, 'finalized')).toBe(before + 0.1 * LAMPORTS_PER_SOL);
    } finally { await c.t.close(); }
  }, 180_000);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the e2e flow (opt-in)**

Create `e2e/mainnet.config.ts`. Copy `e2e/agents.config.ts`, but use ports `E2E_WEB_PORT ?? 5295` and `E2E_API_PORT ?? 3295`. Add these to the API server's `env`:

```ts
WORLD_CHAIN: 'ledger', MAINNET: 'rehearsal', MAINNET_CUSTODY_LOCAL: 'on', MAINNET_RPC_URL: process.env.VALIDATOR_RPC ?? 'http://127.0.0.1:8920',
MAINNET_KEY: Buffer.alloc(32, 7).toString('base64'), MAINNET_COLD_ADDRESS: '<a fresh devnet address printed by solana-keygen>',
```

Set `testMatch: /mainnet\.spec/` and keep the config headless.

Create `e2e/mainnet.spec.ts`. Guard it with `test.skip(process.env.VALIDATOR !== '1', 'needs a local validator')`. The flow:
1. Sign up with LOGIN's fake wallet init script, which is backed by a key the test airdrops on the validator.
2. Raise an agent through RAISE's form.
3. Make the fake wallet the payout wallet in Settings.
4. Open the agent's owner panel, tick "I am 18 or over" and press "I understand".
5. Fund 0.05 SOL. Read the request through `GET /api/mainnet/me`, then pay it from Node with the same key and the reference, as Step 1 does.
6. Wait until the panel shows the balance, at most 60 s, since the poller runs every 15 s.
7. Press "Cash out everything".
8. Assert on the validator that the payer's balance rose.

Run it only with `VALIDATOR=1 npx playwright test -c e2e/mainnet.config.ts` (headless).

- [ ] **Step 3: Run them (opt-in)**

Start a local validator as the README describes. Then run:
- From `apps/server`: `VALIDATOR=1 npx vitest run test/ledger-validator.test.ts`
- From the root: `VALIDATOR=1 npx playwright test -c e2e/mainnet.config.ts`

Expected: PASS. Without `VALIDATOR=1`, both are skipped.

On Windows the validator's airdrop can fail, as the README notes for `solana airdrop`. If it does, replace each `requestAirdrop` with a transfer from the validator's faucet keypair (`<ledger dir>/faucet-keypair.json`), exactly as the README's treasury step does.

- [ ] **Step 4: Commit**

```bash
git add apps/server/test/ledger-validator.test.ts e2e/mainnet.spec.ts e2e/mainnet.config.ts
git commit -m "test(mainnet): custody on a local validator and the owner's money flow end to end (opt-in VALIDATOR=1)

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

## Spec coverage (self-review)

| Spec section | Task |
|---|---|
| §0 legal gate | 12 (typed review), 13 (gate refuses boot) |
| §1 internal ledger, no public pool | 4 (LedgerChain), 6 (`makeChain`); nothing migrates on graduation (4, `settle`) |
| §2 tables, locking, fees, `sendSol`, invariant | 2, 4, 5 |
| §3 vesting, sale cap, rug 25%, breaker, `MAX_TRADE`, no human trading | 5, 6 |
| §4 hot/cold, payout wallet, deposits, caps, refunds, withdrawals, instant/queued, never blocked by game state, house stake | 7, 9, 10, 12, 17 |
| §5 reconciliation, freeze, talk-only engine, proof of reserves | 6, 11, 15 |
| §6 risk notice, 18+, self-exclusion, no asking humans for money | 8, 16, 18 |
| §7 one signer, outflow limit, admin re-auth plus append-only audit, genesis checks | 7, 10, 12, 13 |
| §8 costs | config (1); fees journaled to the house (10, 12) |
| §9 launch gate, rehearsal | 13 |
| §10 seasons, archived coins | 14, 15 |
| §11 harm that remains | wording in the notice (8, 18); nothing to build |
| §12 testing, incl. SIM and e2e | every task, 16, 19 |
| §13 rollout | 14 (season CLI), 15 (admin panel); the owner runs it |

**Not covered on purpose:**
- Choosing the RPC provider, the cold-storage setup and the house stake amount are the owner's open questions 2, 3 and 5. They are configuration, not code.
- Whether the house keeps 0.5% (open question 4) is `MAINNET_HOUSE_FEE_BPS`, which is 50 by default and can be 0.
